7.	The Mystery of Lemuria by Alexandar Kondratov (Sovient Land). INDIA (Jan. 1974).

2. tolporuḷ āyviyal
ARCHAEOLOGY
āyviyalil putiya oḷi
tolporuḷ āyviyal eṉpatu makkaḷiṉ paḻaimaic ceytikaḷaippaṟṟiya aṟiviyal āyvucārnta ariyatuṟai eṉap poruḷtarum. muṟkālattil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷaiyum, uṇavu viḷaittaliṉ taṉmaikaḷaiyum, āṭai aṇikalaṉkaḷ ākkum vaḻikaḷaiyum, vīṭu, araṇmaṉai māḷikai, araṇ kaḷañciyam kaṭṭum muṟaikaḷaiyum, nāṭu nakaram amaikkum tiṟaṉaiyum, uyirtuṟantāriṉ uṭalkaḷai aṭakkam ceyyum vaḻimuṟaikaḷaiyum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai virivāka āyvu ceytu varukiṟatu. iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai ulaka meṅkum paravi virivaṭaintuḷḷatu.
mēlum aḵtu aṟiviyal pāṅkāka āḻntu āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟatu. ittuṟai, ātiyil nilattai akaḻntu āyvu ceyyum muṟaiyai (Excavation) aṭippaṭaiyāyk koṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu teḷivu.
appāl, attuṟaiyil atikat tolaivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷi lum kāṭukaḷilum malaikaḷilum kukaikaḷilum paḻamporuḷkaḷ putaintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉavā? eṉpataik kāṇa āyvuppayaṇam (Exploration) naṭattutal eṉṉum virivāṉa muṟai eḻuntatu. ip poḻutu nīṇṭa neṭunīrppayaṇam ceytu, āyvukkappal (Research ship), nīrmūḻki (Submarine) mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ aṭittaḷam varai ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa malai, ceṭi, koṭi, pāci, pavaḷam, vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum paḻaṅkālattil kaṭalāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa nāṭu, nakaram, vīṭu, araṇ, karuvikaḷ, ammi, ural tirikai pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittu āyum kaṭal āyvu (Oceanography) eṉṉum putiya āyvu muṟaiyum arumpi vaḷarntuḷḷatu.
varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ (Historians) cevivaḻi marapuraic ceyti kaḷaiyum, ēṭu, cempuppaṭṭayam, kalveṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum āyntu muṟkāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷaiyum āyntu nūlākkam ceytuḷḷaṉar. māṉiṭa iyal aṟiñarkaḷ (Anthropologist) paṇṭaik kāla makkaḷiṉ uṭal amaippu, elumpukaḷiṉ amaippu, vaṇṇappolivu, tōṟṟam, vāḻkkaimuṟai, paṇ pāṭṭu iyal, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, camutāya amaippu (Social Structure) uṇavu, uṭai, aṇikalaṉ, toḻil, ēṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyavai eppaṭippaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu āyvu ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu paṇṭaikkāla makkaḷiṉ kaiviṉaip poruḷkaḷ (Handicrafts) eppaṭippaṭṭaṉa? avaṟṟai avarkaḷ eppaṭi uruvākkiṉar? evvāṟu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar? avaṟṟāl nām aṟiyakkūṭiyateṉṉa? eṉpavaṟṟaiyellām teḷivāy āyntu uṇmaiyai ulakiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.
1. tolporuḷ āyvu, 2. varalāṟṟiyal, 3. māṉiṭa iyal (maṉita uṭal, uḷam iraṇṭum cārnta muḻu varalāṟṟu āyvut tuṟai) ākiya mūṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mika neruṅkiya toṭar puṭaiyaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; ivai oṉṟukkoṉṟu uṟutuṇai yāy nilavukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu paṇṭaikkāla makkaḷiṉ pāṅkāṉa varalāṟṟait tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉ nērāṉa tuṇai yiṉṟic cīrākak kāṇamuṭiyātu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. muṟkāla ilakkiyam, paṇṭaikkāla nāṭu, nakaram, ciṟṟūr, iṉam, vilaṅku, paṟavai, makkaḷ, karuvi taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai aṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷai nāṭutalē muṟaiyākum eṉpatai nām maṟantuviṭak kūṭātu.
iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvu iyal aṟiñarkaḷ, varalāṟ ṟiyal vallunarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum allatu payilum nūlkaḷai nuṇukiyaṟiyap putiya muṟaiyil oḷikāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl avai putiya polivum, poṉṟāc cāṉṟum peṟṟup pukaḻuṭaṉ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ poṟik kappaṭṭu, putaikkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷaiyum, ceppēṭukaḷai yum paṭaikkalaṉkaḷaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyum piṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum nakaraṅkaḷaiyum vīṭukaḷai yum araṇkaḷaiyum akaḻntu āyntu aṟintu varalāṟṟaṟiñar kaḷukkup pala putiya ceytikaḷai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kallil allatu cemputtakaṭṭil allatu kaḷimaṇ takaṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum ēṭukaḷilum varaiyappaṭṭa kāṇaṟkaritāyk karutappeṟum karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyum akaḻntu eṭuttut tuppuravu ceytu avaṟṟil poṟikkappaṭṭa puriyāp putirākat tikaḻum poṉṟā eḻuttukkaḷai āyvu ceyyum aṟiñarkaḷ (Epigraphist) mūlam paṭittaṟintu varalāṟṟāciriyar kaḷukku uṇmaiyāṉa ceytikaḷai uraikkiṉṟaṉar.
eḻuttukkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭa toṭakka kālattil makkaḷ kaṇṭa varivaṭivam ōviya eḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. intap paṭa eḻuttup pala vakaippaṭum. (1) pāpilōṉiya cumēriya aciriya makkaḷ eḻutiya āppu eḻuttukkaḷ (Cuneiform writings) (2) ekiptil uḷḷa makkaḷ kaṇṭacol eḻuttiṟku īṭāka eḻunta, poruḷ vaṭivap paṭa eḻuttukkaḷ (Hieroglyphs) (3) cintuveḷit tirāviṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ payiṉṟa paṭa eḻuttukkaḷ (Logograph, Logosyllophic or pictographic writings) eṉappaṭum. inta ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ, viḷaṅkāp putirāka iruntaṉa. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar (Archaeologist) paṭa eḻuttukkaḷaip paṭikkum paṉmoḻip pulavarkaḷiṭam inta eḻuttukkaḷai oppuvittup poruḷ viḷakkam kēṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ avaṟṟai alaci āyntu kaṇakkiṭum nuṇpoṟiyiṉ (Computer) tuṇaikoṇṭu pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ payiṉṟa, paṭa eḻuttukkaḷai, paṉṉāḷ payiṉṟu āyntu, maṟaikuṟi eḻuttumūlattiṉ poruḷviḷakkam (Decipher) kaṇṭaṉar. itaṉāl, akaḻ āyvil tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ uṟutip paṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uruvākkiya paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, kalai, camayam, kaṭavuṭ paṟṟu, āṭai aṇikalaṉ, kaiviṉaip poruḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ teḷivāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar karuttukkaḷ araṇ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. varalāṟṟaṟiñar kaḷukkut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉar aṟiviyal vaḻikāṭṭi yāka viḷaṅkiṉar. intiya varalāṟu muḻumai peṟṟu viḷaṅka, cintu veḷi akaḻ āyvum ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvum peruntuṇai yāka iruntaṉa. iruḷcūḻnta paṇṭaiya intiya varalāṟṟiṟku ivviru varalāṟukaḷum nalla oḷiviḷakkāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ulakil ituvarai eṅkum cintuveḷiyaip pōṉṟu ki.mu. 4000 āṇṭiṟku muṉṉar eḻunta pala nakaraṅkaḷai akaḻntu kaṇṭatē illai.
virivāṉa ākkappaṇi
ulakil uḷḷa ellā nāṭukaḷum, tattam nāṭukaḷil tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟaiyai niṟuvic ceyalāṟṟi varumpaṭi cey tuḷḷaṉa. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟai mutalil airōppāvilē arumpiyatu. appāl āciyāvilum, amerikkāvilum ēṉaiya kaṇṭaṅkaḷilum malarntuḷḷatu.
iṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar palvēṟu nāṭu kaḷilum palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum āyvu naṭatti varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvarai, tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar naṭattiya ciṟappāṉa akaḻāyvukaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭuvaṉa ekiptu, ellam, upaitiyā, cumēriyā, kirīṭ, peru, mekcikō, cintuveḷi, ātittanallūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa akaḻ āyvukaḷē yākum. ivaṟṟil vaṭa intiyāvil uḷḷa cintuveḷi yilum teṉ intiyā vilum naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻāyvukaḷ mika veṟṟi vāynta - perumaik kuriya akaḻāyvukaḷākum. ivai, ulakilē mikat toṉmaiyāṉa tol kuṭi makkaḷiṉ uyariya nākarikattai uṇarttuvatāka eṇṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa paṇpāṭu cintuveḷi, upaitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ, mekcikō, peru mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tuḷirtteḻunta āriya nākarikaṅkaḷukkellām aṭippaṭaiyāy amaintuḷḷatu. ēṉ? - mūlakkaruvāka amain tirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. itu mikat toṉmaiyāṉa makkaḷ irumpu ūḻiyil (Iron age) arumpiya irumpup paṇpāṭu (Iron Culture) ākum. inta ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭaik kaṇṭa makkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ cintu veḷi cumēriyā ekiptu kirīṭ mutalāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟi ariya periya paṇpāṭukaḷukku aṭikōliyavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar, muṟkālattil tam āyvukkuriya iṭam īteṉak kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, mikunta iṭarp paṭṭuk kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ cevivaḻi marapuraic ceytikaḷaiyum ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷaiyum vaittu āyvukkuriya iṭam īteṉa muṭivuceytu vantaṉar. itil avarkaḷ pala ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷai aṭaintaṉar; eṇṇaṟṟa tōlvikaḷaiyum eytiṉar. ivaiyaṉṟi vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟku aṭippaṭaiyiṭa, nilattait tōṇṭum poḻutum payir ceyya nilattai uḻum poḻutum, kuḷam kuṭṭai, kālvāy mutaliyavaṟṟait tōṇṭumpoḻutum nilattiṉ aṭiyil paḻaṅkācukaḷ, cilaikaḷ, tātup paṭimaṅkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, tāḻikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar. malaikkukai kaḷil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar.
ivaṟṟaiyellām vaittut tolporuḷ āyviṟkuriya iṭam īteṉak kaṇṭaṉar. muṟkālaṅkaḷil ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷai aṟintu aṅkuc ceṉṟu akaḻ āyvu ceyvataṟ kuriya iṭaṅkaḷai aṟintu, aṅku akaḻntu nilattiṉuḷ paḻam poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavā, illaiyā? eṉṟu āyvukaḷ ceytuvaruvar. aṅkup paḻamporuḷ iruppatum uṇṭu; illā mal ēmāṟit tirumpuvatum uṇṭu. iṉṟu, appaṭic ceyvatillai.
aṟiviyal muṟai vaḷarcci eytiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu miṉ cātaṉaṅkaḷ mūlam nilattiṉ mēṟpakutiyiṉ mītu niṉṟu koṇṭē, uḷḷē paḻamporuḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavā, illaiyā? eṉṟu eḷitil kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭalkaḷilkūṭak kappalkaḷ mītu iruntu koṇṭu nīriṉ aṭiyil āḻamāṉa iṭattil irukkum paḻam poruḷkaḷaiyum, pāṟaikaḷ uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, ceṭi koṭikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum paṭampiṭittu varukiṉṟaṉar. mākaṭalil nīriṉuḷḷirukkum nilattaip paṭam eṭuttu āyum iyal (Oceanography) pōṉṟa pala ariya nuṇṇiyal vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. mēlum iṉṟu maṉitaṉ taṉ āṟṟalai vīṇākac celavu ceyyāmal payaṉtarum muṟaiyil maṉita āṟṟalaic celaviṭa vaḻikōlap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
akaḻ āyvut tuṟaiyil īṭupaṭṭōr, nilattait tōṇṭum poḻutu nilattiṉ aṭiyil irukkum poruḷkaḷō vīṭō, cilaiyō, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷō, kūlapporuḷkaḷō evaiyāka iruppiṉum avai ciṟitum cētamuṟātu tōṇṭi eṭuppar. maṇṇuḷḷirukkum tuṇiyō maṇiyō, tātup poruḷō (Metals) cāmpalō, pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ putaiyuṇṭu uḷḷiruntu makkip pōyiruntālum avaṟṟai aṇuvaḷavum cētamuṟāmal uṉṉippāka uṟṟuṇarntu akaḻntu eṭukkiṉṟaṉar.
ceyaṟkaruvi (Artifact)
tolporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟaiyiṉar akaḻntu kaṇṭavai, nakaram veḷippuṟak kōṭṭai (Fort), nakarattiṟku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa araṇ (Citadel), araṇmaṉai, māḷikai vīṭu, kūlakkaḷañciyam, niṉaivucciṉṉam, kōyil, toḻiṟcālai, kallaṟai, putaikuḻi, piṇappāṉai (tāḻi), maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, tātu poruḷkaḷ - ulōkap poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟaṉavāka irukkalām; avai ciṟiyaṉa vākavum periyaṉavākavum irukkalām. allatu aṇikalaṉkaḷ, viḷakku, kiṇṇam, keṇṭi, katti, arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, tūṇṭil, curaṇṭi, karaṇṭi, ūci, muḷvāṅki, kātu kuṭaivāṉ, iṭukki, paḻaṅkācu, tuṇi, kūlapporuḷ, umi pōṉṟaṉavākavum irukkalām. makkippōṉa nellum, iṟṟuppōṉa pullum, karaṭu muraṭāṉa kaṟ karuviyum tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉarukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta karuvūlamākak karutappaṭum. ivaṟṟai avarkaḷ ceyaṟkaruvikaḷ (Artifacts) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolporuḷ āyvuvallunarkaḷ ceyaṟ karuvikaḷ mūlam, ivaṟṟaip paṭaittup payaṉpaṭuttiya muṟkāla mutumakkaḷiṉ kaittiṟaṉ, aṟivuttiṟaṉ, pōrttiṟaṉ, āṇmai, āṟṟal, vāḻkkai muṟai, vaḻipaṭu teyvam, camayam, camūka uṟavu, caṭaṅku muṟai, ūṇ, uṭai, paṇpu, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟai yellām aṟikiṉṟārkaḷ.
puvi aṭukkiyal (Stratigraphy)
tolporuḷ āyviyalār nilattai akaḻntu āyvu ceyyat toṭaṅkum poḻutu, catura vaṭivil ceṅkuttāka nilattait tōṇṭi uḷḷirukkum eḷiya poruḷaiyum viṭṭuviṭāmal kaippaṟṟik kuḻiyil kiṭaikkum poruḷ enta aṭukku varicaiyil ettaṉai yaṭiyiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kuṟittu vaittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷait tōṇṭumpoḻutu maṉita varalāṟṟaik kuṟippiṭum ūḻik kālattiṟkuriya palavakaik kaṟkaruvi kaḷum, tātup poruḷkaḷum, ēṭukaḷum, eḻuttukkaḷum kiṭaik kiṉṟaṉa.
eṉavē, avaṟṟai varicaippaṭuttit tokuttuvaik kiṉṟaṉar. nilattait tōṇṭumpoḻutu uṭpuṟam, nilattiluḷḷa maṇ aṭukkaṭukkāy (Layers) kāṇappaṭum. tōṇṭum poḻutu ovvōr aṭukkilum oru ciṟu kucciyai aṭittu ataṉ aṭukku eṇkaḷaiyum, āḻattiṉ aṭiyai allatu muḻattaiyum kāṭṭum oru vācakattai ōr aṭṭaiyil eḻutit toṅka viṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. appaṭi aṭivarai tōṇṭi eṭutta piṉ, ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaiyum, ataṉ āḻattaiyum pativu ceytukoḷḷukiṟārkaḷ. piṉṉar ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaiyum pirittu vakaippaṭutti maṭ pāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷ, tātupporuḷkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, elumpukaḷ, pāci pavaḷaṅkaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ eṉṟu vakuttut tokuttu vakaippaṭutti vaikkiṉṟārkaḷ. appāl ovvōr aṭukkilum kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukkuk kālam kaṇikkiṉ ṟārkaḷ. itaṉ mūlam puvi aṭukkiyal eṉṉum āyvu muṟai urup peṟṟuḷḷatu. akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa poruḷkaḷai aṟiviyal ārāycci mūlam (Carbon 14 test), poruḷkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattai aḷaviṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭivam amaippu aḻaku nilai cāyam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam kālam kaṇippatum uṇṭu.
putiya āyvu muṟai
amerikka aṟiñar āṇṭrū-iṭaklacu maravaḷaiya muṟai eṉṟa oru putiya āyvu muṟaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. oru kaṭṭiṭattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa marattil kāṇappaṭum vaḷaiyaṅkaḷai vaittu akkaṭṭiṭam kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa kālattaik kaṇippatākum. itu kaṭṭiṭattiṉ kālattait tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kaṇṭu piṭikkum putu muṟaiyākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.
maṟṟoru muṟai paṇṭaik kālattiluḷḷa kallaṟaikaḷilum vīṭukaḷilumiruntu akaḻntu kaṇṭa kūlap poruḷkaḷ, kāykaṟikaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntu pala ceytikaḷaic cēkarikkum muṟaiyākum.
piṟitorumuṟai, paṇṭaik kālattut tātup poruḷkaḷai iracāyaṉa muṟaiyil pakuttu, aḵtu evvāṟu tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu aṟitalākum. iṉṉoru muṟai uyir nūl aṟiñarkaḷ paṇṭaik kālac citaivukaḷiṉiṉṟu kiṭaikkum elumpukaḷ talaiyōṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmūlam, kālam, iṉam, pirivu pōṉṟavaṟṟai aṟiviyal muṟaiyil kaṇṭupiṭittalākum.1
āḻnta āyviyal
iṉṟaiya makkaḷ paṇṭaiya makkaḷaiviṭap pala tuṟaikaḷil ārvaṅkāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpatu mey. ciṟappāka nammuṭaiya muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, moḻi, uṇavu, uṭai, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, karuvikaḷ, kaippaṇikaḷ, vāḻkkai muṟai, paṇpuppāṅku, nākarika nayam mutaliyavaṟṟai aṟiyap peritum nāṭukiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭaik kāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷaiyum avarkaḷuṭaiya karuvikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum aṟiya iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal, nalla vaḻi vakuttuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ ceyta kaippaṇikaḷaik kāṇa ariya paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṟatu. iṉṟu ulakiluḷḷa aṉaittu nāṭukaḷilum tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaikaḷ niṟuvap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
inta āyvuttuṟai muṉṉar nām vāḻum nilattai maṭṭum akaḻntu āyntu vantatu. appāl mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ aṭiyil mūḻkik kiṭakkum kōṇṭ vāṉā, lemūriya kaṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum, nāvalan tīvu pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum avaṟṟil uḷḷa pāṟaikaḷ uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaiyum āyntatu. kappalkaḷ, nīrmūḻkik kalaṅkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ tuṇaiyuṭaṉ āḻkaṭaliṉ aṭit taḷaṅkaḷaiyellām kaṇṭu āyntu varukiṉṟatu. eṉ matippiṟkum aṉpiṟkum uriya cōviyat nāṭṭu aṟiñar  a. kōntrottōv mūṉṟu mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlam pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tamiḻarkaḷiṉ mūtātaikaḷ muṉṉāḷil nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amaittut teṉ maturaiyil mutaṟ caṅkam niṟuvi muṭi puṉaintu, aracu kaṭṭilil amarntu ceṅkōl tāṅki, erutuk koṭi ēṟṟiya kataikaḷai yellām mey eṉṟu ulakiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭa muyaṟci ceytuḷḷār eṉṟu terikiṟatu. āḻkaṭal aṟivā rāycci toṭakka nilaiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. āṉāl, atu viraivil anta ariya uṇmaikaḷai ulakiṟku uṇarttum eṉṟu tam nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2 inta intumāk kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷa āyvuttuṟai vicrānti eṉṉum ciṟappāṉa kappal mūlam uruciya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu 7 allatu 8 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ 6 laṭcam kal tolaivup payaṇam ceytu āyvu naṭatti uruciya moḻiyil 11kku mēṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉai mūṉṟu māk kaṭaliṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil 1974 il kuṟippiṭṭuḷ ḷār. ataṟkup piṉ inta āḻkaṭal āyvu muṟai evvaḷavō vaḷarcci aṭaintuḷḷatu. avaṟṟiṉ vivaram namakku iṉṟuvarai kiṭaikkavillai.
āṉāl inta āyvu muṟai intiyāvilum ataṉ aṇṭai nāṭukaḷilum vaḷaravillai. araca pīṭattil amarntiruppavarkaḷ varalāṟṟāyviṉ, mutaṉmaiyai uṇarntavarkaḷāka illai. talaivarkaḷil cilar nāṭṭil vaṟumai, vēlaiyillāt tiṇ ṭāṭṭam, vēlai niṟuttam, māṇavarkaḷ kiḷarcci mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūṟi varalāṟṟu āyviṟkup paṇam celaviṭak kūṭātu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. ittakaiya mara maṇṭaikaḷ peruki varum pōkūḻ (turatiruṣṭa) nilaiyil paḻaṅkāla nāṭṭiṉ pāṅkai aṟintu ataṉ vīḻcciyai uṇarntu putiya nāṭṭai vaḷamārnta iḷamaip polivu ceṟiyum intiyāvai uruvākka nāṭum iḷaiñar ulakam tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟai cīrpeṟa ilaṅka namatu nāṭṭait tūṇṭa vēṇṭum. pārāḷumaṉṟattiṟkum caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷukkum palkalaip paṭṭam peṟṟa paṇpāḷarkaḷai uṟuppiṉarkaḷākat tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum. aṟivu paṭaitta putiya nākarika intiyāvai uruvākka muyala vēṇṭum.
niṟka, paṇṭaikkāla makkaḷuṭaiya vāḻkkai varalāṟukaḷai nām naṉkaṟiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa āval pāpilōṉiyāvil ōr aracaṉukkuk ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil arumpiyatām. uṭaṉē taṉ nāṭṭil akaḻ āyvu naṭatta āṇai aḷittāṉ eṉṟu an nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. ki.mu. 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kiṟittuviṉ kallaṟaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭikka an nāṭṭu makkaḷ nāṭiṉar. urōmaṉ nāṭṭu maṉṉar maṉṉaṉ mā kāṉcuṭāṇṭiṉ (Constantine the Great) eṉpavarukkum itil akkaṟai arumpiyatu. uṭaṉē avar oru tūtukkuḻuvai, pālattīṉattiṟku aṉuppik kallaṟai irukkum iṭattaik kaṇṭupiṭikka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytāṉ eṉṟu oru varalāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. 1801ām āṇṭu elcciṉ pirapu ēteṉcu nakaril uḷḷa oru kirēkka kiṟittavak kōyilil irunta paḻaiya ciṟpaṅkaḷait tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭittu iṅkilāntiṟku eṭuttuc ceṉṟār.
ivaiyellām paḻaṅkālattilē pāpilōṉiyarkaḷukkum, urōmarkaḷukkum āṅkilēyarkaḷukkum paḻam poruḷkaḷait tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum; paḻam ciṟpaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu piṭittu avaṟṟaip pātukāttu vaikkavēṇṭum; varalāṟṟu aṟivai vaḷarkka vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iruntataik kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām. aḵtiṉṟip paḻaṅkālattilē aṅkut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai muḷaittu eḻak kāraṇamāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇalām; iyampalām; eḻutalām.
intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvu
intiyā paḻamperum nāṭu. teṉintiyāvilē makkaḷiṉam tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vaḷampeṟṟu uyarntatu eṉṟu ulakil aṟiñar palar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉaip pērāciriyar erṉacuṭ ekkal (Ernest Heaecrel) eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.3 atai makliṉ eṉṉum aṟiñarum ātarittuḷḷār.4 kirām ulliyam pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa aṟiviyal pulavarkaḷ araṇ ceytuḷḷaṉar.5
tamiḻnāṭṭil tolpaḻaṅkal ūḻitoṭṭu (Eolithic age) paḻaṅkal ūḻi (Paleolithic age), iṭaikkal ūḻi (Mesolithic age), putukkal ūḻi (Neolithic age), tātupporuḷ ūḻi (Metal age), varalāṟṟu ūḻi (Historic age) pōṉṟa ellā ūḻikaḷum maṉita iṉam tōṉṟi, karuvikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttat terinta paṇṭaik kālantoṭṭu iṉṟuvarai ellāk kālaṅkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntu vantuḷḷaṉa. itai mikat teḷivākac ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟup pērāciriyarum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaip pērāciri yarumāka irunta aṟiñar tiru. vi.em. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar, em.ē. avarkaḷ tam nūlil virivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār.6 atai aṟiñarkaḷ palarum virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
tamiḻmakkaḷ - ēṉ tirāviṭa iṉam, viḷakkamākak kūṟuvata ṉāl - maṉita kulattiṉ mūtātaiyarkaḷ tamiḻakattil tōṉṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭaik kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, eṉap pirittu vāḻntuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu tamiḻpperum ilakkaṇanūlāka viḷaṅkum tolkāppiyam pōṉṟa paḻamperum nūlkaḷ navil kiṉṟaṉa. kuṟiñci nilamakkaḷ (villarkaḷ) malaiyil vāḻntu vēṭṭuvat toḻilaik kaṇṭaṉar. villaip paṭaikkalamākak koṇṭaṉar. mullai nilamakkaḷ (āyarkaḷ) tiṉai, kēḻvaraku mutaliya puṉceyt tāṉiyaṅkaḷaip payiriṭappayiṉṟaṉar. āṭu māṭukaḷaip paḻakkip pālkaṟakkavum tayir, mōr, veṇṇey pōṉṟaṉa ceyyavum paḻakikkoṇṭaṉar.
marutanilamakkaḷ nīr niṟainta kuḷakkaraikaḷilum āṟṟaṅkarai kaḷilum avaṟṟai aṭuttuḷḷa camaveḷikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi erutukaḷaip paḻakkiṉar. kaḷimaṇ nilaṅkaḷaik kaṟkaḷāl kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi naṉcey nilamākkip payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. piṉṉar ēraik kaṇṭu uḻutupayiriṭṭu nellaiyum eḷḷaiyum ēṉaiya kūlap poruḷkaḷaiyum payiriṭat toṭaṅkiṉar. in nilattilē makkaḷiṉ naṉmuyaṟciyāl pala toḻilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai vaṉaital, nūlnūṟṟal, tuṇineytal, irumpukkaruvikaḷaiyum marapporuḷkaḷaiyum ceytal, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷai vārttal, kallai, maravai, caṭṭi, tūṇ mutaliyavaṟṟai uruvākkal, eḷvittu, tēṅkāyk kopparai, āmaṇakku vittukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ney vaṭittal, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷai eṭuttup puṭamiṭṭup pacum poṉṉākki aṇikalaṉkaḷaic camaittal, tātup poruḷkaḷait takaṭukaḷākki avaṟṟil eḻut tāṇi koṇṭu eḻututal, vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭutal, kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ amaittal, kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭutal kōpuraṅkaḷ, kōṭṭaikaḷ eḻupputal mutaliya toḻilkaḷ vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa. āṭutal, pāṭutal, ōviyan tīṭṭutal, pala vaṇṇac cāyaṅkaḷai uruvākkutal, kavitaikaḷ iyaṟṟal, kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷ eḻututal, camaya aṟivaip perukkutal pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ceyalkaḷai āṟṟivantaṉar. uḻavut toḻilai vaḷarttaṉar; vaṇikam naṭattiṉar. maṟṟum paṟpala kaittoḻilkaḷaip pēṇi vaḷarttaṉar.
innila makkaḷ kāṭu kaṭantu, kaṭal kaṭantu, nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu pukuntu kuṭiyēṟa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. kaṭal vaṇikattil īṭupaṭṭu veṟṟiyīṭṭiṉar. piṟa nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi avvan nāṭukaḷait tāyakamākak koṇṭaṉar. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa malaikaḷilē taṅkaḷiṉ teyvamākiya mantiramalaivācalaik kaṇṭaṉar. civaneṟiyai nilai nāṭṭiṉar. taṅkaḷiṉ uyariya paṇpāṭṭaiyum aṟaneṟiyaiyum oḻukkattaiyum nākarikattaiyum parappiṉar. in nilattiṟkuriya uḻukulattōr tāṅkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya nilaṅkaḷilellām payirt toḻilai vaḷarttaṉar. marutanilam toḻil vaḷarccikku oru maiyamāka amaintatu. celvap perukkiṟku uyarnta ūṟṟāka uyarnteḻuntatu. inta vaḷamārnta nilattil mutaṉmutalākap parattaiyar (Concubine) tōṉṟum vāyppu uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavillai.
neytalnilap paratavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭal vaṇikam vaḷarat tuṇainiṉṟaṉar; mīṉpiṭittaṉar; uppu viḷaittaṉar; caṅku aṟutta ṉar; tōṇi camaittaṉar; kappal kaṭṭiṉar; āḻkaṭal kaṭantu kappalkaḷaic celuttum ariya kalaiyait tamakkē uriyateṉak koṇṭu pēṇi vaḷarttaṉar. kaṭal āḻattaiyum nīrōṭṭaṅkaḷai yum alaikaḷiṉ pōkkukaḷaiyum aṟintaṉar; kaṭalil vāḻum periya timiṅkilaṅkaḷaiyum, koṭiya cuṟāmīṉkaḷaiyum, piṭikka vaḻivakuttaṉar; kaṭalil cippikaḷaiyum muttukkaḷaiyum pavaḷaṅ kaḷaiyum, caṅkukaḷaiyum kaṇṭu eṭuttaṉar; vāṉanūṟ pulamai peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻar tiraikaṭalōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu eṉṟa poṉmoḻiyai naṉmoḻiyākak koṇṭu vaḷarat tamiḻnāṭṭu neytal nilap paratavarkaḷ tuṇainiṉṟaṉar eṉṟāl aḵtu oru ciṟitum mikaiyākātu.
tamiḻakattu neytalnila makkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyiṉṟit tamiḻar allatu tirāviṭar eṉpōr ekiptu, upaitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, kirīṭ, teṉamerikkā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭu kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi irukkamuṭiyātu eṉpatu eḷitil viṭṭuviṭakkūṭiya ceyti aṉṟu.
intiyāvil ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭilum piṟa tirāviṭa nāṭukaḷilum paḻaṅkālattiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ mūtātaiyar viṭṭucceṉṟa eccamiccaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākak kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyellām innāṭṭaik kavara eṇṇic cila aṉṉiya nāṭṭu aracukaḷāl poṉṉaiyum poruḷaiyum koṭuttu aṉuppappaṭṭa kiṟittava camayak kurumārkaḷ cilar ceṉṟa nūṟ ṟāṇṭilē uṇarntaṉar. intiya nāṭṭiṉ paḻamperum nākarikaṅ kaḷaiyum camayac ciṟappaiyum iṟaivaḻipāṭṭaiyum moḻic ciṟappaiyum naṉkāyntu aṭikkaṭi ayalnāṭṭiluḷḷa aracukaḷuk kum, makkaḷukkum uṇarttivantaṉar. eṉavē airōppāvil toṭakkanilaiyil irunta tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai māṇavarkaḷ. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ arumpi appāl aḻintu pōṉa intiyanāṭṭiṉ nākarikattaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum nakar amaipput tiṟaṉaiyum, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭum muṟaiyaiyum, kappal kaṭṭum kalaiyaiyum, maruttuvam ceyyum māṇpaiyum, moḻi amaikkum nuṇukkattaiyum, eḻuttākkum pāṅkaiyum akaḻntu āya ārvamuṟṟaṉar. iḵtaṉṟi mēṉāṭṭiluḷḷa tol poruḷ āyvu vallunarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟip paḻaṅkācu cēkarippavar kaḷum, paḻaiya cilaikaḷ cempuppaṭimaṅkaḷ, paḻaiya ōlaic cuvaṭikaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ cēkarip pōrkaḷumāka aṟiñarkaḷ palar mēlaināṭu kaḷiṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟkuḷ ki.pi. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvilē pukuntu pala ariya paḻamporuḷkaḷaic cēkarittut tam nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōy āyntaṉar. ap poruḷkaḷait taṅkaḷ paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭampeṟacceytaṉar. iṉṟum avai pāricu, perliṉ, mācukō, ilaṇṭaṉ, ileyṭaṉ, niyūyārk, pōcuṭaṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccālaikaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppataik kāṇalām.
tolporuḷ āyvu iyaliṉ toṭakkam
intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvu iyalaik kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭci ki.pi. 1860ām āṇṭiṟkuc caṟṟumuṉ toṭaṅku vittatu. āṉāl 1850ām āṇṭiṟku muṉpē tirunelvēli māvaṭṭak kiṟittuva capaiyil nallāyar (Bishop) ākap paṇi yāṟṟiya muṉaivar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ (The Rt. Rev. Dr. Robert Caldwel, D.D., LL.D. Bishop of Tinnevelly) iṭaiyaṉkuṭiyiliruntu koṇṭu tamiḻppulavarkaḷiṭam tamiḻkaṟṟup pulamai eytiṉār. avar paḻam pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ talainakarāyum iṭaiccaṅkam nilaviya mūtūrāyum varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉa māyum viḷaṅkiya koṟkaikkum kāyalukkum ceṉṟu tol poruḷ āyvuceytuḷḷār. avar aṅku āyvu ceyyum poḻutu makkaḷ avarait taṭuttaṉar. paḻaṅkālap pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷum paḻaṅkāla vaṇikarkaḷum ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷai maṇṇil putaittu vaittuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟai iṉṟum pala pūtaṅkaḷ kāttu varukiṉṟaṉa. nīṅkaḷ nilattai akaḻntāl ac celvaṅkaḷaik kavara varuvatāka eṇṇip pūtaṅkaḷ uṅkaḷaik koṉṟu viṭum. muṉṉar māvaṭṭa atikāriyiṉ icaivupeṟṟu iṅku vanta atikāri oruvar koṟkaiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyya vantu iravil tam kūṭārattil paṭuttiruntār. tiṭīreṉṟu tam uṭampil ēṟpaṭṭa atircciyaik kaṇṭu viḻittup pārkka, avar uṭal pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyiluḷḷa tam illattil kaṭṭiliṉ mēl iruppatai aṟintu tikiluṟṟār. ataṟkuppiṉ koṟkaiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyyum eṇṇattaik kaiviṭṭuviṭṭār eṉṟu puṉaikatai pōṉṟa ceytiyaik kūṟiṉar. nīṅkaḷ iṅku akaḻntu pārkka muṟpaṭṭāl pūtaṅkaḷ uṅkaḷai maṭṭumaṉṟu, eṅkaḷaiyum, eṅkaḷ ūraiyum aḻittuviṭum! eṉṟu eccarittaṉar.
avarkaḷiṉ parivuraikaḷaik kēṭṭu viyantu, aiyā, nāṉ poṉṉaiyō, maṇiyaiyō aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyō akaḻntu eṭuttuc cella nāṭi iṅku varavillai eṉṟu avarkaḷukku amaiti kūṟi māvaṭṭa atikāriyiṉ āṇaiyōṭum avaruṭaiya paṇiyāḷōṭum vantirukkiṟēṉ eṉṟuraittu akaḻntu āyvukaḷ ceytuḷḷār. itu paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ avar eḻutiya tirunelvēli araciyal potuvaralāṟu7 eṉṟa nūlilum, koṟkaiyilum kāyalilum naṭattiya āyvup payaṇam8 eṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyilum naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1861ām āṇṭu kiḻakkintiyak kumpiṉi āṭciyāḷariṉ (Rulers of the East India company) poṟi vallunarāṉa alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām (Alexander Cunningham) avarkaḷai oru mēṟpārvai yāḷarāka amartti intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyait toṭaṅkumāṟu āṇaiyiṭṭatu. kumpiṉi aracu avar atait toṭaṅki vaikkumāṟum ceytatu. appāl 1870ām āṇṭil avarē intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉ iyakkunarāka amarttap peṟṟār.9
1861ām āṇṭil tāṉ intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaikkum naṉku aṭikōlap peṟṟatu. mutalāvatu iyakkunar taḷapati (Director General) ākat tiru. alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām 1870ām āṇṭil amarttap peṟṟār eṉṟu ataṉ varalāṟu kūṟu kiṟatu. alekcāntar kaṉṉiṅkām nāṭṭiṉ nālāpakkaṅkaḷukkum ceṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvu ceyya muṟpaṭṭār. avar vaṅkam, pañcāp, cintu mānilaṅkaḷuk kellām ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkē kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷaic cēkarittu vantār. āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa marapuvaḻic ceytikaḷaiyum ilakkiyac ceytikaḷaiyum tiraṭṭi aṟikkaikaḷ pala veḷiyiṭṭu vantār. ivarukku vaṭa moḻip payiṟciyumuṇṭu.
1881ām āṇṭil teṉṉintiyat tolporuḷ āyvup poṟuppu (Archaeological Servey of South India) ivariṭam oppuvikkappaṭṭatu. appoḻutu cēmcu parkecu (James Burgess) eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāvilum mēṟku intiyāvilum iv vāyvut tuṟaiyil īṭupaṭṭiruntār. anta āṇṭilē eyc.eyc. kōl (H.H. Cole) paḻamporuḷkaḷiṉ pātukāppāḷarāka amarttap peṟṟār. 1883ām āṇṭil ip patavi muṭivu eytiyatāl tol āyvup paṇi mānila aracukaḷiṭam mīṇṭum oppaṭaikkap paṭṭatu.10
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil naṭuvaṇ aracu tolporuḷ pātukāppu mutaliyavaṟṟai mīṇṭum taṉ poṟuppil koṇṭuvantatu. naṭuvaṇ aracu aṉaittu intiyat tolporuḷ tuṟaiyaic cemmaippaṭutti, virivākkiyatu. vayavar cāṇ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) 1876-1958 eṉṉum pēraṟiñarai 1902 ām āṇṭu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai iyakkunar taḷapati 
(Director General of Archaeologic Department) āka amarttiyatu. avar ēṟakkuṟaiya irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷ paṇiyāṟṟi 1928ām āṇṭil ōyvu peṟṟār; ōyvu peṟṟu, mokañcatārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum (Mohenjo Daro and Indus civilization) eṉṟa ulakappukaḻpeṟṟa nūlai eḻuti mūṉṟu tokutikaḷāka veḷi yiṭṭār; taṭcacīlam, cāñci mutaliya pauttarkaḷiṉ puṉita iṭaṅkaḷai akaḻntāyntu oru ciṟu nūl veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār; 1931 ām āṇṭu avar ellāp patavikaḷiṉiṉṟum vilaki ōyvu peṟṟu avaratu tāy nāṭākiya iṅkilāntu ceṉṟu 1958ām āṇṭu uyir tuṟantār.
mārcal muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattiya cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu ulakilē oppaṟṟa periya akaḻ āyvu eṉṟu pārāṭṭappeṟṟu, ivarukku īṭillāp perumaiyai īṭṭiyatu. ivartam mokañca tārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa nūlil cintuveḷi nākarikam, civaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu vanta tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭimakkaḷiṉ uḻavut toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭu eṉṟu āyntu muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷār. innūl mānilam aḻiyum varai avarukku maṅkāp pukaḻai īṭṭit taravallatu.
cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu
cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu veṟṟi īṭṭiyatu. tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉ mītu makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭam īrkkappeṟṟatu. intiya naṭuvaṇ aracu cintuveḷi nākarikattaip peṟṟa aracu eṉṟu peru mitam koṇṭatu. aracu tārāḷamākap paṇam nalkit tiṅkaḷ veḷi yīṭukaḷum, tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai āṇṭu aṟikkaikaḷum, nūlkaḷum veḷiyiṭat tūṇṭiyatu. mīṇṭum pala iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻ āyvu naṭatta āṇaiyiṭṭatu.
āṅkila āṭci, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkak kālattilē paṇṭaiya nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ pātukāppuc caṭṭam oṉṟai niṟuviyuḷḷatu. ataṉ payaṉākap pala ciṟappu vāynta paḻaṅkāla niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷukkuk kaṭattik koṇṭu pōkaviṭātu taṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. eṉṟālum keṭṭa neñcattiṉar cilarmūlam nammuṭaiya aimpoṉ paṭimaṅkaḷ cilaikaḷ, tūṇkaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, kācukaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, cep pēṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉa kavarntu pōkappaṭṭatai nām maṟaikkavō maṟakkavō muṭiyātu. itaṟku nam araciṉarum kōyil pūcārikaḷum poṟuppāḷar āvar.
niṟka, 1926 ām āṇṭu tolporuḷ āyvup payaṇam 
(Exploration) naṭattuvataṟkeṉṟum, akaḻ āyvukku (Excavation) ceyvataṟkeṉṟum oru taṉippirivu uruvākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 1935 ām āṇṭiltāṉ intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṉ kīḻttol poruḷ āyvu, naṭuvaṇ araciṉ poṟuppil koṇṭu varap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 1944ām āṇṭil muṉaivar ār.i. mārṭimar uyilar tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉ iyakkunar taḷapatiyāy vantatum, tēṅkik kiṭanta tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaikkup put tuṇarcci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu, āriyar allāta makkaḷ kaṭṭi eḻuppiya nākarikam eṉpatai uyilar araṇ ceytār. arappā, mokañcatārō nakarkaḷiṉ kāvaṟ kōṭṭai kaḷai (citadels) akaḻntu kaṇṭu āriyarkaḷiṉ irukkuvētam kūṟum tācar, tacayūkkaḷiṉ araṇkaḷ ivaiyēeṉṟu ulakaṟiya uraittār; mokañcatārō eṉṟu avar eḻutiya ciṟu nūlil anta nakar araṇiṉ (citadel) paṭattaiyum irukkuvēta culōkaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭic cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam aṉṟu eṉṉum pēcciṟku muṟṟuppuḷḷi vaittār. ivarē arikkaṉ mēṭu akaḻ āyvai naṭattit, tamiḻar 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ naṭattiya kaṭal vaṇikattiṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi, intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟai naṭattum paṇṭaiya intiyā (Ancient India) eṉṟu intiyat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉar veḷiyiṭṭu varum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil oru periya kaṭṭurai varaintu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum yavaṉa nāṭṭu vaṇikattiṟku meyccāṉṟu īteṉa eṭuttak kāṭṭiṉār.
mēlum uyilar, intiyattolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaikku nalla maṟuvāḻvu aḷittār. āṅkāṅkē paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālaikaḷ eḻac ceytār. putiya muṟaiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyya araciṉ aluvalarkaḷaiyum taṉiyārkaḷaiyum payiṟṟuvittut tolporuḷ āyvu virivaṭaiya vaḻikōliṉār. muṉṉar araciṉ potup paṇittuṟaiyē paṇṭaiya niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷaip paḻutu pārttal, kāppāṟṟutal pōṉṟavaṟṟaic ceytu vantatu. atai avar tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyē nēraṭiyākac ceyyumāṟu tūṇṭiṉār.
intiyā urimai eytiyatum 1950ām āṇṭil tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟai putiya araciyal amaippiṉ paṭi naṭuvaṇ araciṉ poṟuppilum, mānila araciṉ poṟuppilum oruṅkē varakkūṭiya tuṟaikaḷil oṉṟāka amaintatu. intap putiya neṟi muṟaikaḷiṉpaṭi mānila aracu ovvoṉṟum taṉakkeṉṟu taṉit taṉiyē tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉai niṟuvik koṇṭatu.
1958ām āṇṭil paḻaiya niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, tol poruḷ kaḷaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pātukāppiṟkāka oru putiya caṭṭam eḻuntatu. nāṭṭiṉ paḻam poruḷkaḷ (Antiquities) varalāṟ ṟiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum nākarikattiṟkum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭai yaṉavāy iruppatāl avaṟṟai ayalnāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyvataik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum atikārattai aracu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṭam oppaṭaittatu. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟai yiṉarē iṉṟu tol poruḷ kaḷaṅkaḷai āytal, akaḻtal, paḻam poruḷkaḷ paḻutupaṭā vaṇṇam vētiyal muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu pātukāttal, āyvu muṭivukaḷait tokuttu aḻaku poliya acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭutal pōṉṟavaṟṟaic ceytu varu kiṉṟaṉar.
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai taṉ ceyalmuṟai eḷimaik kāka intiyāvai 9 tolporuḷ vaṭṭaṅ kaḷākap pirittuḷḷatu. ataṉ talaimai aluvalakam pututilliyil uḷḷatu. aṅkut tolporuḷ āyvut tērccikkeṉṟu tolporuḷiyal āyvuppaḷḷi oṉṟu naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṟatu. mēlum tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟaiyil intiyap palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ ūkka muṭaṉ māṇavarkaḷukkup payiṟciyaḷittu varukiṉṟaṉa.
kāḷipaṅkaṉ
intiyā urimai peṟṟu eḻuntapiṉ naṭanta āyvukaḷil oṉṟu irācattāṉil pikkāṉir māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa kāḷipaṅkaṉ eṉṉum iṭattil naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻāyvākum. iḵtu arappā mokañcatārō pōṉṟa nakaramākum. itu cintu veḷip paṇ pāṭṭiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka ki.mu. 2045 mutal ki.mu. 2075ām āṇṭu kaḷukkuḷ eḻunta nakaramākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. arappāvaip pōṉṟu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum, kaḻivu nīrōṭaikaḷum, kiṇaṟukaḷum akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. arappāvaip pōl kuṭiyiruppu iṭaṅkaḷum poṉ cempu maṇikkōvaikaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
cintu veḷiyil kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷpōl iṅkum pala muttiraikaḷ atē eḻuttukkaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavāyk kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilar itaṉ kālam ki.mu. 1500āka matippiṭukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. iṅkuḷḷa makkaḷ neruppait teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭirukka lām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuc cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyap paṭṭa civaliṅkaṅkaḷ palavum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta akaḻ āyvil kiṭaitta uṭainta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ pala uḷa.
kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam
itu cōḻarkaḷiṉ tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉam. itaṉaip pūmpukār eṉṟum kūṟuvar. itu cīrkāḻikku aṇmaiyil kāviri kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭattil uḷḷatu. iṅku intiya naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyaic cērnta tiru. ē.vi. irāmaṉ ēṟakkuṟaiya 15 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ akaḻ āyvu naṭatti ṉār. iṅkup pala paḻaiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, cōḻar kācukaḷum, urōma nāṇayaṅkaḷum, caṅkukaḷum, vilai uyarnta maṇikaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. itaṉ kālam ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭāka matikkap paṭukiṟatu. iṅku oru kaṭṭiṭamum akaḻntu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
iṅku ōr aḻakiya puttar kōyilum kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum ceṅkaṟcuvarkaḷ mītu aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ivaiyaṉṟi vēṟu pala kaṭṭiṭac citaivukaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅku irācarācaṉ kālattiya kācukaḷum kaṟuppu civappu vaṇṇan tīṭṭappaṭṭa uṭainta maṇpāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷ palavum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭaitta maṇ pommaikaḷ caturak kācukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu iṅkuc citaintuḷḷa kaṭṭi ṭaṅkaḷ 9 allatu 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērntavaiyeṉac cilarāl eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu akaḻ āyvu
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭukku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa pālāṟṟiṉ karaiyai aṭuttuḷḷa kōrttalaiyāṟṟup paṭukai intiya naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai vallunar tiru. vi.ṭi. kiruṭṭiṇamūrtti avarkaḷ talaimaiyil akaḻ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭatu. aṅkup paḻaṅkal ūḻiyaic cērnta makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa kallālāṉa kaikkōṭāri, kattikaḷ, kuḻavikaḷ pōṉṟavai yākum. ivaiyaṉṟi uritteṭukkum kaittoḻilkaḷum, tiṇṇiya mutukuḷḷa vāḷkaḷ ceyyum kaittoḻilkaḷum, ciṟukaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa niṉaivucciṉṉaṅkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭukku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷil paḻaṅkal ūḻiyil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ uṇavu camaitta aṭuppukaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup pala uṭainta maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum cāmpalkaḷum araikuṟai yāy erintu miñciya marakkaṭṭai kaḷum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṟiviyal muṟaiyil āyvu ceyyappaṭṭu (Carbon 14 Test) ivai paḻaṅkal ūḻiyaic cārnta makkaḷ (Palaeolithicmen) viṭṭuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu.
irāmanātapuram, nellai māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil akaḻāyvu
irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tirumaṅkalam pōṉṟa nakarkaḷukku aṇmaiyil putiya kal ūḻiyaic cērnta makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷ pala kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa uvari, meyññāṉapuram, nācarattu, cāyarpuram, putūr pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil putiya kal ūḻi yil vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷ palavaṟṟai naṭuvaṇ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉar kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷaṉar.
eṉavē, tamiḻnāṭṭil cumār aimpatāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē makkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. tamiḻmakkaḷ paḻaṅkal ūḻiyiliruntu māḻai (ulōka) ūḻimūlam varalāṟṟuk kālattaiyum kaṇṭa tolkuṭiyaic cērnta mutu makkaḷ eṉpatu aiyantiripaṟak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷālum varalāṟṟuttuṟai vallunarkaḷālum tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟai vallunarkaḷālum uṟuti ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
koṟkai akaḻ āyvu
koṟkai, pāṇṭiyanāṭṭiṉ talainakar, tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉam, iṭaiccaṅkam nilaviya mūtūr, it tuṟai mukattiṉ mūlamē tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉar, cōṉakar, ekiptiyar, cumēriyar pōṉṟa veḷināṭṭiṉaruṭaṉ kaṭal vaṇikattai naṭattiṉar. ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikarkaḷ poṟkācukaḷaiyum, poṟpāḷaṅkaḷaiyum aḷḷi aḷḷik koṭuttuviṭṭuk koṟkai muttukkaḷaiyum cantaṉak kaṭṭaikaḷ, ēlam, kirāmpu, eḵku āyutaṅkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum peṟṟut tam kappalil ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. nam kappalkaḷum ayalnāṭukaḷukkac ceṉṟu mīṇṭaṉa.
iṉṟu koṟkaiyilirunta kaṭal cumār 4 kal tolaiviṟkup piṉvāṅkip pōyviṭṭatu. eṉavē, pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tam talai nakarai maturaikku māṟṟiṉar. ēṟattāḻa 2,000 āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ koṟkai nilaikulaintu aḻintu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉāl avvūr iṉṟu ētumaṟṟa ciṟṟūrāyk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.
ataṉai namatu tamiḻ araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai iyakkunar muṉaivar nākacāmi avarkaḷum avaruṭaiya utaviyāḷar tiru. macītu avarkaḷum akaḻntu cila maṭpāṇṭat tuṇṭukaḷai yum, cila tātupporuḷkaḷaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar. mēlum koṟkaip paṇpāṭu ki.mu. 785-kku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiviyal pāṅkāka āyntu muṭivukaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. koṟkaiyai mīṇṭum virivāka āyvataṟku namatu aracu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyai muṭukkiviṭumāṟu nammakkaḷ araciṉai vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.
tirukkōyil kaṭṭiṭak kaṇkāṇippu niṟuvaṉam
tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi, kaṭṭiṭakkalai nuṇukkam ivaṟṟiṉ āyvukkeṉa intiya naṭuvaṇaracu kōyil kaṇ kāṇippu niṟuvaṉam (Temple Survey Project) eṉṟa amaippiṉai uruvākkiyuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu, vaṭa intiya āyvukkeṉṟu pōpālil amaintuḷḷatu. maṟṟoṉṟu ceṉṉaiyil teṉ ṉintiyakkōyil kaṭṭiṭattuṟaic ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷiṉ āyvuk keṉṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟkeṉṟu iyakkunar oruvarum niḻaṟpaṭam eṭuppavarkaḷum, eḻuttarkaḷum paṇiyāṭkaḷum iyaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. inta niṟuvaṉam ceṉṉaiyil uḷḷa tamiḻaka araciṉ talaimaic ceyalakattiṟku aṇmaiyil iruntu varukiṟatu. aṅkuc ceṉṟu avarkaḷai ōraḷavu nāṉ payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ.
kalveṭṭu āyvuttuṟai
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaikku utaviyāka 1883 ām āṇṭu paḻaiya paṭṭayaṅkaḷ, kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ pōṉṟa vaṟṟaik kaṇṭupiṭittuppaṭiceytu viḷakki iṉṟaiya varivaṭivil eḻuti nūlvaṭivil ākkam ceytu veḷiyiṭuvataṟkeṉṟu kal veṭṭu āyvuttuṟai oṉṟu (Epigraphic Department) toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. kalveṭṭu, paṭṭayaṅkaḷ āyvuttuṟait talaivarākac cāṇ peyitpul piḷīṭ eṉṉum āṅkilēyarai aracu amartti yatu. avar nāṭeṅkum tēṭi kiṭaikkum kalveṭṭukaḷaiyum, cempuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷaiyum tiraṭṭiṉār; palavaṟṟaip paṭiyeṭut tār; avaṟṟai nūlvaṭivamākki varalāṟṟuk kāla varicaiyil tokuppataṟkut tiṭṭamiṭṭup paṇiceytār.
kalveṭṭu, cempuppaṭṭaya āyvai oru taṉi aluvalakam kavaṉittu varukiṟatu. iḵtu āṅkila āṭciyiṉ kālattil utaka maṇṭalattil iyaṅki vantatu. intat tuṟaiyiṉ talaivarāka āṅkilēyarkaḷē iruntu vantaṉar. ippoḻutu ittuṟai intiyarkaḷiṉ talaimaiyil iyaṅki varukiṟatu. ippoḻutu itaṉ aluvalakam maicūrukku māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl, ivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭuvarum paṇikaḷai empōṉṟa varkaḷē aṟiyamuṭiya villai. ivarkaḷ paṭitta paṭṭayaṅkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷivantatākat teriyavillai. namatu aracu ataṟku āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum. tolporuḷ vētiyiyal ārāyccit talaimai aluvalakam tērāṭūṉilum, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa āyvu aluvalakam nākapuriyilum paḻam poruḷ kāṭcittuṟai āḷumai kalkattāvilum iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nalla varalāṟṟai aṟiya iṉṉum viraivākak kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷivaravēṇṭum. kiṭṭat taṭṭa nūṟāyiram kalveṭṭu kaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu collap paṭukiṟatu. kalveṭṭu āyvuttuṟai iṉṟu āmai vēkattil iyaṅkivarukiṟatu. inta vēkattil aḵtu iyaṅkivantāl tamiḻar kaḷiṉ niṟaivāṉa varalāṟṟaik kāṇa iṉṉum 100 āṇṭukaḷāvatu cellum eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. namatu aracu ittakaiya oru tuṟaiyai maicūril naṭatti varukiṟatu eṉpatu aṟiñar palarukkukkūṭa teriyātu.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
II
1.  kalaik kaḷañciyam, tokuti VI, mutaṟpatippu, 1959 (ceṉṉai) tolporuḷ iyal, pakkam 224.
2. The Riddles of Three Oceans :
		“Oceanographers may be right when they say that this portion of  the Indian Ocean is an ancient area in transition and has not yet  completed its development. That is, the Seychelles micro Continent  is not a region which has subsided but, on the contrary a portion of the ocean floor which has not yet risen to the surface. Neither a positive nor negative answer can yet be given to this question. We shall have to wait and see what is revealed by further geophysical and Oceanographic studies of the Indian Ocean; now only in their initial stage.
		Oceanographers, geologist and geophysicists are devoting their closest attention to the north western section of Indian Ocean, which has the most complex relief and where the earth’s crust is still in motion, as evidenced by volcanic eruptions and earthquakes. Many of them believe that this part of the Ocean has developed differently from all the other sections. The granite massifs of East Africa, the Arabian Peninsula and the Indian subcontinnt continue out into the Indian Ocean” (The Riddles of Three Oceans - Alexander Kondratov (Moscow) 1974, pp. 174-175.
3.	“Indian Ocean founded a continent which extended from Sunda Island along the coast of the Asia to the east coast of Asia to the east coast of Africa. This large continent is of great importance from being the possible cradle of the human race” - “Anthropology”.
	“These are a number of circumstances (specially chronological facts) which suggest that the primeval home of man was a continent now sunk below the surface of the Indian Ocean which extended along the South of Asia as it is at present (and probably in direct connection with it) towards the east as far as further India and the Sunda Islands towards the west as far as the Madagascar and the south shores of Africa.
	This large continent of former times Selater an English man has called Lemuria from the monkey like animals which inhabited it and it is at the same time of great importance from being the probable cradle of the human race which in all likelihood here that developed out of anthropoid apes” - Prof. Ernest Haeckel.
4.	Investigations in relation to race show it is by no means impossible that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of Northern and Mediteranean races proceeded to the globe which they now inhabit. Human remains and traces have been found on the east coast of an age which is indeterminate but quite beyond the ordinary calculations of History. Antiquarian research is only now beginning to find means of supplementing the deficiency caused by the absence of materials constructed or collected by usual historical methods. These results are specially to be regarded as without doubt, the people who have many ages occupied this portion of the Penisula are a people influencing the whole world not perhaps by moral and intellectual attributes to a great extent by a superior physical qualities” - Dr.E.Maclean.
5.	“India was an amazing breeding ground for the evolution of life forms. Man himself may have struggled upwards out of the anthropoid within the limits of India” - The World We Live In - Graham Williams Vol.1. p. 1114.
6.	Origin and Spread of the Tamils.
	(a)	“Bruce Foote examined, among others the most important Prehistoric burial places covering over hundred and fourteen acres of land at Adichanallur on the South Bank of the Tambraparni. (Thennporunai river) in the Tinnevelly District. These graves at Adichanallur are to be dated in te early Iron Age which succeeded the Neolithic Age in South India” - Origin And Spread of the Tamils by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar, M.A. (Madras) 1971, p. 21.
	(b)	“We have a continuity of culture from Paleolithic to Neolithic, from Neolithic to Megalithic and Megalithic to Iron age in South India. It cannot be that the Primitive indigenous culture was buried by the invading Dravidian. To escape out of the difficulty, an ingenious theory of Proto - Dravidian has been propounded. The Proto-Dravidian is as unsound as the theory of a Dravidian invasion. My firm conviction is that the ancient Tamils were inheritors of the Lithic Cultures of South India” - Ibid. p. 15.
	(c)	“The late Professor P.T. Srinivasa Aiyangar was a warm champion of the theory of cultural and even racial continuity of the people of South India from the earliest times. He sought to explain” that the Old Stone Age in South India ended very gradually and shaded off very imperceptibly into the neolithic culture and that throughout South India, there was no geological or other indication of catastrophic phenomena when the Palaeolithic Age ended and the next age began. The substitution of traprock for quartzite, the acquisition of the skill to polish the tools made with trap-rock till they became extra-ordinarily smooth to the touch, the domestication of the wild dog, and the cultivation of the wild rice led to the peaceful evolution of the epoch of new stone tools from that of the older rough implements, the settled life of the neolithians from the nomad life of the palaeolithians” - Journal of the Bihar and Orissa Research Society Vol. xxiv. pp. 39-40.
	(d)	Thus according to this view, the Dravidian race was indigenous to the Country; and the Tamil and other allied peoples were indigenous; and their languages were evolved where they are now spoken. A careful study of South Indian Pre-historic antiquities in Situ cannot lead to any other conclusion than that the passage of culture from stage to stage in ancient times was not a catastrophic change such as indicates the struggle or alien intruders with the pre-existing population but a peaceful course of evolution. An inspection of the map of neollthic India is enough to prove that the Country was thickly populated by people of one homogenous from of culture and that the people ought to have been autochthonous, as the Tamil people have always claimed to be in the tradition recorded in their ancient literature. In the most ancient layers of the Tamil language can be discovered not only ample traces of neolithic culture, but also the birth of the Iron age culture that succeeded it” - Journal of th Bihar and Orissa Research Society vol. xxiv. pp. 41-42.
7.	A Political and General History of Tinnevelly - Dr. R. Caldwell (Bishop of Tinnevelly) Published by the Madras Govt. 1881.
8.	Exploration at Korkai and Kayal by the Rt. Rev. Robert Caldwell. D.D., LLD. (Bishop of Tinnevelly) 1877.
9.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adicha-nallur and Perumbair - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras, 1915.
10.	Vide Reports of th Archaeological Survey of Southern Circle for 1890-1900 to 1903-1904 and of the Archaeological Survey of India for 1902 -1903 and 1903-1904.

intap pakuti ākkattiṟku utaviya nūlkaḷ

3. ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ akaḻāyvu
intiyāvilē naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻāyvukaḷil ātitta nallūrp paṟampil naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻāyvu, mutal akaḻāyvākum. inta akaḻāyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ tām ulakilē mikat toṉmai vāynta poruḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟṟaikkup pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻumpiya putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil eḻunta irumpu ūḻiyil, tamiḻmakkaḷ ātitta nallūr irumpup paṇpāṭṭaik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai vallunar pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ātittanallūr
ātitta nallūrp paṟampu, intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil uḷḷa tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil tirunelvēlic cantippup pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattiṉiṉṟu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai vaḻiyāyt tiruccentūr cellum cālaiyil, pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyiliruntu kiḻakkē 11.6 kilō mīṭṭar tolaivil taṇporunai āṟṟiṉ (tāmpiravaruṇi āṟṟiṉ) teṉkaraiyil uḷḷatu. intap paṟampiṉ varampu 114 kuṟukkattukku (ēkkarukku) mēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. inta aḷavu itaṉ aṇmaiyil uḷḷa āticcanallūr eṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷāl aḻaikkappaṭṭu varum ciṟṟūrai uḷḷaṭakkiyē kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ātitta nallūrp paṟampu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum oru maṇmēṭu taṇporunai āṟṟiṉ teṉ karaiyiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkit teṉvaṭalāka nīṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ vaṭapakuti uyarntum, teṉpakuti tāḻntum uḷḷatu. iṉṟum,kuṉṟeṉa nimirntu niṟkum im mēṭṭu nilam (paṟampu) kaḷḷiyum, muḷḷiyum, kallum, muḷḷum, caralum, paralum, veḷḷuṭaiyum karuvuṭaiyum (karuvēlum), putarum patarum aṭarntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu payirt toḻilukkup payaṉpaṭāta pāṅkaṟṟa nilamākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. inta iṭattaip paṇṭaiya tamiḻarkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalaip putaikkum īmakkāṭākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. (paṭam 1aip pārkka). cintuveḷiyil akaḻntu kaṇṭa perunakar oṉṟiṟku mokañcatārō eṉṟu peyar. mokañcatārō eṉpatu intic col. ataṉ poruḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ nakaram eṉpatākum. intap peyar annakarukku ēṟṟatāka eṉṉāl karuta muṭiyavillai. āṉāl, ataṟku ñāyiṟṟu nallūr (ātitta nallūr) eṉṟa peyaraḷittuviṭṭu, ātitta nallūril akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭa iṭattiṟku mokañcatārō eṉṟu peyariṭṭu aḻaittāl mikap poruttamāka irukkum. ēṉeṉil itu muṟṟilum īmakkāṭē. iṅku āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ cavak kuḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mokañcatārō appaṭippaṭṭa īmakkāṭaṉṟu. makkaḷ vāḻnta mānakaramākum. maṟaintupōṉa māperum nākarikamāṉa cintu veḷi nākarikam, upaitiya nākarikam, ellam nākarikam, cumēriya nākarikam pōṉṟa paḻamperum nākarikaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭaiyāy amain tiruntu aḻintupōṉatu ātitta nallūr nākarikam.
mutal akaḻāyvu 
tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai piḷḷaip paruvattil irukkum poḻutē - aḵtāvatu 1876 ām āṇṭilē cermaṉ nāṭṭil uḷḷa perliṉ nakaril vāḻnta muṉaivar cākōr avarkaḷ 
(Dr. Jagore) ātittanallūrkku vantu intap paṟampil uḷḷa putar kaḷaik kaḷaintu nilattai akaḻntu aṅkup putaiyuṇṭu kiṭanta paḻam poruḷkaḷai eṭukkat tiṭṭamiṭṭār.
avaratu tiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟa muṉaintu avar perliṉ nakaraiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭuk kappalil ēṟi intiyāviṟku vantu pukai vaṇṭiyilum, māṭṭu vaṇṭiyilum nīṇṭanāḷ payaṇam ceytu, intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyil uḷḷa kumarimuṉaikku vaṭakkil ēṟattāḻa 100 ki.mīṭṭar tolaivil uḷḷa ātitta nallūrai aṭaintār. avar nērāka ātittanallūrukku varavillai. avar tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri tiru. icuṭṭu varṭṭu (Mr. Stuart the acting collector of Tinnevelly District) māvaṭṭap poṟiyiyal vallunar (District engineer) ākiyavarkaḷ piṉtoṭara, ātitta nallūrp paṟampai aṭaintu aṅkuḷḷa kallum maṇṇum kalanta kaḷar nilattai akaḻa muṟpaṭṭār. iḵtu intiyāvil tuḷirttuk kiḷaittuḷḷa akaḻ āyviṉ toṭakka viḻāvāka eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu. ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil etirpārttirāta veṟṟi yiṉai muṉaivar cākōr eytiṉār. aṅkup pala tāḻikaḷum, elumpukaḷum, poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷum, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷam, irumpup poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta paḻamporuḷkaḷaik kaṇṭu muṉaivar cākōr kaḷippuṟṟār. kāṇaṟkariya karuvūlaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttatākak karuti aṉaittaiyum mikap pātukāppākap peṭṭiyil aṭaittup perliṉ nakarukkak koṇṭu pōṉār. appāl avvariya paḻam poruḷkaḷ aṉaittaiyum aṅkuḷḷa paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil (Berlin Museum for Volkrkunde) iṭam peṟac ceytār.1 itaṟkumēl intap poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya ceyti etuvum namakku eṭṭavillai.
iraṇṭāvatu akaḻāyvu
itait toṭarntu 1903-1904 ām āṇṭukaḷiṉ kuḷir kālattil pirañcu nāṭṭaic cērnta pārīcu nakaril uḷḷa em. ulūyicu ilāppikyū (M. Louis Lapicque) avarkaḷāl ātitta nallūrp paṟampil mīṇṭum akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭu muṉpāl paḻamporuḷkaḷ pala akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭu aṉaittum pāricu, paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭampeṟac ceyyap peṟṟaṉa. aṟiñar ilāppikyū avarkaḷ iṅkuk kaṇṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷ aṉaittaiyum āyntatiṉ viḷaivāka apporuḷkaḷ aṉait taiyum tirāviṭarkaḷ āti muṉṉōrkaḷukkuc contamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟa muṭiviṟku vantār.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku evvakait toṭarpum aṟṟa cermaṉkārar tamiḻakattil nuḻaintu ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻāyvu naṭatti vilai matikka muṭiyāta karuvūlamāṉa paḻamporuḷkaḷaip pakaṟ koḷḷai aṭippataippōl kavarntu ceṉṟār. itaik kaṇṭu ulakam kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi āṭciyai (East India company Rule) ēḷaṉamākac cirittatu. ataik kaṇṭu piriṭṭīcuk kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉi āṭcikkuc coraṇai ēṟpaṭṭatu. 1899 mutal 1906 ām āṇṭuvarai ātitta nallūrp paṟampil mīṇṭumakaḻāyvu naṭattumāṟu aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷukku āṇai piṟappittatu. avar ap pakutikaḷai uṉṉippāka uṟṟu uṇarntu akaḻntu ērāḷamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷai eṭuttār. muṉṉar akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷaiyotta paṭikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkuḷḷa putaikuḻikaḷ (Burial grounds) māperum paḻaimai vāyntavai eṉṟu muṭipu ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattiṟkup piṉṉarp payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭavaiyāy irukka vēṇṭum. ivai pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ tōṟṟa mūlattai uṭaiyaṉa eṉṟum irīyā eṇṇiṉār. ï‹W m¿P® mby¡rhªj® ïßah mt®fŸ mfœªJ f©l Mâ¤ješÿ®¥ gH«bghUŸfŸ mid¤J« br‹id¥ gH«bghUŸ fh£á¢ rhiyÆš xU gh§fhd ïl¤âš tF¤J¤ bjhF¤J¢ Óuhf it¡f¥ bg‰ WŸsd.*
muṉaivar cākōrum, aṟiñar em. ulūyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷum ātittanallūrppaṟampil akaḻntu eṭuttuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷ eṉṉeṉṉa eṉṟu evarum aṟiyār. avarkaḷil evarum taṅkaḷ paḻam poruṭkāṭciccālaiyil iṭam peṟac ceytirukkum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷukku viḷakkamāṉa paṭṭiyal etuvum veḷiyiṭṭiruppatāka namakku evvakaic ceytiyum ituvarai kiṭaikkavillai. āṉāl, intiya nāṭṭiṉ aṉṟaiya aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka (trustees) irunta piriṭṭīcuk kiḻakkintiyak kampeṉiyiṉ pērāḷarāka iruntavar, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri tiru. icuṭṭūvarṭ eṉpavar āvar. avar 1876 ām āṇṭil, muṉaivar cākōr akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭutta ceyti kaḷaip paṟṟik kumpiṉiyiṉ mēlatikārikaḷukku oru kaṭitam eḻuti yuḷḷār. antak kaṭitattuṭaṉ aṉṟaiya piriṭṭīcukkumpiṉi āṭci yiṉ tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyil 1902-03, 1903-1905 ām āṇṭu kaḷiṉ intiyāvil tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai aḷavāyvu ceytavarkaḷum 1899-1900ām āṇṭukaḷ mutal 1903-1904 āṇṭukaḷ varai tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉ, teṟku vaṭṭārac cuṟṟāyvu ceytavarkaḷum aḷitta aṟikkaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avai kūṟum ceytikaḷ :
nāṅkaḷ 25 allatu 30 caturakkecam (1 - caturakkecam 3 aṭi nīḷamum 3 aṭi akalamum aṭaṅkiyatu) parappaḷavuḷḷa mēleḻunta vāriyākak kallum caraḷum niṟainta pāṟaimītuḷḷa oru pakutiyai yum atē mūlakkūṟukaḷaiyum kal pāḷaṅkaḷaiyum paṭikakkal niṟainta paṟampiṉ maṟṟum oru pakutiyaiyum, akaḻ āyvu ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉōm. aṅku, nāṅkaḷ 25kku mēl 30 eṇ varaiyuḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai (tāḻikaḷai)k kaṇṭupiṭittōm. avaṟṟiṉ uruva aḷavu 3-9 aṭi mutal 3-6 aṭivarai uyaramum ēṟattāḻa pattu aṅkulam vāyakalamum aḷavuḷḷaṉavāka iruntaṉa. pala iṉṟu kaṭaikaḷil viṟkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiviṭa uṟutiyum nākarikamum uṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. inta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil maṇkaṭṭiyum caraḷkaḷum aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟōṭu paḻamporuḷ ārvalar (Antiquarian) maṉitavakai vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum toṭarpukaḷaiyum, paṇpāṭukaḷaiyum paṟṟi āyvu ceypavar, paḻam poruḷkaḷ tiraṭṭiccērppavar (antiquarians, the ethrologist and collectors of Antiquities) mutal aṟiviyal vallunar varaiyuḷḷavarkku mikak kavarcci yūṭṭum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ atil aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa parumaṉil uḷḷa aṭukkaḷaip pāṇṭaṅkaḷum maṇṇāl ākkappaṭṭavaiyāka iruntaṉa. avai aimpatu vakaikku mēṟpaṭṭavaiyākum. mēlum aṅku irumpuppaṭaik kalaṉkaḷ (Iron weapons) tuṇaik karuvikaḷ (Implements) ciṟappākak kattikaḷ allatu kuṭṭaiyāṉa veṭṭu vāyp pakutiyaiyuṭaiya vāḷkaḷ (Short sword-blades) kaik kōṭarikaḷ (Hatches) ākiyavaiyum periya aḷavil elumpukaḷum, talaiyōṭukaḷum (maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷum) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iṉṟu mika mutaṉmaiyāṉa ceyti yākum. aḵtāvatu, iraṇṭu ciṟumaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ oru periya maṭpāṇṭattiṉuḷ vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpatākum. periya maṭpāṇṭattuḷ kiṭṭattaṭṭa ellā elumpukaḷum aṭaṅki yiruntaṉa, (ivaṟṟil eṉṉa poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa) eṉṟu kūṟuvatil piḻai etuvum eḻa muṭiyātu. āṉāl, tirunelvēlic cāmai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum ularnta kūlapporuḷum arici eṉap paṭum kūlapporuḷum mēlē uḷḷa veḷip pakutiyil iruntaṉa. intak kūlap poruḷkaḷ tāmākavē ukki maṟaintupōyiṉa. āṉāl mēlē tūvappaṭṭa - perumpālum maṇal cattuḷḷavai - eñci iruntaṉa.
eṅkaḷuṭaiya mutaṉmaiyāṉa akaḻ āyvu toṭaṅkap paṭṭa iṭattiṉiṉṟu kuṟaintatu 270 allatu 360 mīṭṭar tolai vil uḷḷa palaviṭaṅkaḷil mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa cāvu viṉaikkuriya putaikalaṉkaḷ (tāḻikaḷ urns) paṟampiluḷḷa caraḷaik kaṟkaḷiṉ mēṟpākattil uṭainta ōṭṭut tuṇṭukaḷākap pala citaṟuṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. avai ellā vaḻikaḷilum akaḻāyvai meyppikkak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl, oru ceytiyil maṭṭum avai piṇaṅkaḷaip putaikkum paḻaiya iṭukāṭṭiṉaip paṟṟik kuṟippāka eṭuttukkāṭṭum iṭamāka iruntatu. allatu aṭinilak kallaṟaiyil (cata comb) antak kālattil piṇattaip putaittu vaikkum perum vaḻakku nilaviyatu eṉpatu iṉṟaiya intiyā viṟkup perumpālum teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟalām. makkaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭṭaip periya kuvaḷai allatu maṭpāṇṭat tiṉuḷḷē vaittu ataṉuḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku ciṟiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷ maṟṟōr ulakai aṭaiyumpoḻutu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum poruṭṭu veḷip paṭaiyāyt teriyumāṟu ēṟpāṉa uṇavum, irumpālāṉa paṭaik kalaṉkaḷuṭaṉ taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intap poruḷkaḷ aṉaittaiyum akaḻntu eṭuttuc ceṉṟa muṉaivar cākōr avaṟṟaip perliṉ paḻam poruṭkāṭciccālaiyil (Museum) iṭampeṟac ceytuḷḷār. intap poruḷkaḷil oṉṟum ceṉṉaip paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟavillai. ivaṟṟaiyellām māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷar tiru. icuṭṭuvarṭ aracukku eḻutiya anta maṭalil naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉpatu ceṉṉai araciṉ potup paṇittuṟai naṭavaṭikkaikaḷ (27-3-1876 iṉ 329 ām eṇṇuḷḷa cāṉṟu) eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aṅku akaḻāyvu naṭaipeṟṟapiṉṉar nilam paḷḷamum mēṭumāyk kiṭantatu. caraḷaikaḷ aṅkum iṅkum kuvintu kiṭantaṉa. maṇ eṅkum citaṟuṇṭu kiṭantatu. elumpukaḷ aṭaṅkiyirunta mutu makkaḷ tāḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷ iṅkoṉṟum aṅkoṉṟumāy nērākavum, cāyntum, uṭaintum kiṭantaṉa. akaḻvataṟku eṉṟu vanta vēlaiyāṭkaḷ kuruṭṭunampikkai koṇṭu pēy picācu eṉṟu kūṟi naṭuṅki aṅkup pukuntu paṇi ceyya añci niṉṟaṉar. mēṟkoṇṭu akaḻntu āya muṭiyavillai. mēṟpākattil kiṭakkum cila tāḻikaḷai āyvu ceyyavō, cermaṉ aṟiñar viṭṭucceṉṟa paḻamporuḷkaḷai eṭuttukkoṇṭu varavō veḷippaṭuttavō muṭiyātiruntatu. ērāḷamāṉa paḻaiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷ atika uḻaippiṉṟi eṭuttuk koṇṭu varum nilaiyil kiṭantum avaṟṟiṉ arukē evarum tuṇintu cellavillai. mūṭanampikkaiyil mūḻkik kiṭanta vēlai yāṭkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷil elumpukaḷum kuṟippākat talaiyōṭukaḷum koṭiya pēykaḷākat tōṉṟiṉa.
iṟuti āyvu
ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ mēltaḷattil kiṭanta ariya poruḷkaḷai eṭuttakkoṇṭu varavum, toṭarntu akaḻntu āyvu naṭattavum, aracu eṇṇiyatu. āṉāl, aṉṟuḷḷa cūḻnilai yil anta eṇṇam kaiviṭap paṭṭatu. appāl akaḻ āyvu ceyyavēṇṭā eṉṟu aracu āṇaivantatu. paṟampu evvakaik kalaiyum akaḻvum ceyyappaṭātu viṭappaṭṭatu. akaḻ āyvu ceyyum aṟiñarkaḷukku utavi etuvum kiṭaikkāta nilaiyil anta eṇṇam kaiviṭappaṭṭatu. mēlum toṭarntu āyvu ceyyap paṭavillai. itaṉ payaṉāy ērāḷamāṉa vilai uyarnta paḻam poruḷkaḷ tēṭuvāraṟṟa nilaiyil kiṭantu aḻintoḻintaṉa.
1899 mutal 1900 āṇṭu varai ceṉṉai mānila araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyiṉ matippukkuriya tuṇaik kaṇkāṇip pāḷarāka irunta tiru.a.irīyā avarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalākap paṟampiṉ mēṟṟaḷattaip pārvaiyiṭṭār. appoḻutu aṅkuḷḷa cila pakutikaḷ akaḻāyvu ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpatai uṇarntār. 1903 mutal 1904 varai ovvōr āṇṭum cila tiṅkaḷ avarāl mēṟkoṇṭu akaḻāyvukaḷ naṭattappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷil tiraṭṭap paṭṭavai ceṉṉai araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil vakuttut tokuttu iṭampeṟac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. irīyā avar kaḷiṉ perum muyaṟciyāl kiṭṭattaṭṭa avarukku muṉṉar akaḻ āyvu ceyta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷukku oppāka ērāḷamāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉ maṟupaṭivam (duplicate) kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai ātitta nallūr akaḻāyvup poruḷkaḷukkut tarappaṭṭa paṭṭiyalil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ceṉṉai araciṉ paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyiṉ kaṇkāṇippāḷar tiru.cē.ār. eṇṭarcaṉ 1914 ām āṇṭu ātitta nallūrp perumpāyūrkaḷi ṉiṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
1899ām āṇṭu mutal 1905ām āṇṭu varai teṟku vaṭṭārat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaik kaṇkāṇippāḷarāka iruntu ōyvu peṟṟavarum, ceṉṉai araciṉ paḻamporuṭ kāṭcic cālaiyiṉ matippukkuriya tuṇaik kaṇkāṇippāḷaru māṉa tiru. alekcāntar irīyā ev.e.ē. (kāṭ) avarkaḷ ceṉṉai mānilak kiḻakkintiyakkumpiṉi āṭciyiṉ āṇaippaṭi āṇṭutōṟum ōriru tiṅkaḷ ātitta nallūrp paṟampaic ciṟitu ciṟitāka akaḻāyvu naṭatti, ērāḷamāṉa ariya paḻam poruḷ kaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttu avaṟṟai vakuttut tokuttu, ceṉṉaip paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyiṉ oru periya maṇṭapattil iṭampeṟac ceytār. 1913ām āṇṭu akṭōpart tiṅkaḷil avar tam paṇiyiṉiṉṟu ōyvu peṟumuṉ akaḻāyvup poruḷ kaḷukku oru nalla paṭṭiyalum uruvākkik koṭuttuḷḷār. ap paṭṭiyal aḻakiya 13 paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉum ceṉṉaip paḻamporuṭ kāṭcic cālaiyiṉ kaṇkāṇippāḷarāka irunta tiru.cē.ār eṇṭarcaṉ avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉuraiyuṭaṉum ātitta nallūrilum perumpāyūrilumuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal (Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbair) eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ 1915 ām āṇṭu ceṉṉai aracu paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccālai oruciṟu veḷiyīṭ ṭiṉai3 veḷiyiṭṭatu. ataṟkāka nāṉum tamiḻakamum aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷukkum tiru.cē.ār. eṇṭarcaṉ avarkaḷukkum naṉṟiyaiyum vaṇakkattaiyum eṉṟum celuttak kaṭamaip paṭṭuḷḷōm. avaratu nūliṉ paṭi oṉṟu eṉṉiṭam kiṭaittirā viṭil ippoḻutu uṅkaḷ kaiyilirukkum innūl veḷi vantirukka muṭiyātu.
ulakam ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyō tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya irumpu ūḻiyil nilaviya irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta nākarikac ciṟappaiyō aṟintirukka vāyppu eḻāmal pōyirukkum. aṟiñar irīyā aḷitta ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal 1902-03, 1903-04 āṇṭukaḷukkuriya intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉ āṇṭaṟikkaiyākavum 1908-09 āṇṭil perumpāyūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ aṟikkaiyākavum veḷivantuḷḷatu. aṟupat taintu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṟikkaiyākavum, nūl vaṭivamākavum ākkap peṟṟa ivvēṭu 50 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē viṟpaṉaiyāki viṭṭatu.
makkaḷ kaikku vanta nūlkaḷ pala, kāla veḷḷattāl paḻaiyaṉavāki iṟṟukkeṭṭu maṟaintu pōyviṭṭaṉa. eṉavē iṉṟaiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukkum, tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉarukkum maṉita iṉanūl aṟiñarkaḷukkum (Anthropologists) ātitta nallūr tamiḻar nākarikattai aṟiyum vāyppu illātu pōyviṭṭatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukku ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvum, cintuveḷi akaḻāyvum intumāk kaṭaliṉ aṭittaḷam varai cōviyat mākkaṭal āyvāḷarkaḷ (Oceanographers) kaṭalil 75,000,000 caturak kilō mīṭṭar parappu allatu ulakamāk kaṭal kaḷil aintil oru pākaṅkoṇṭa intumākkaṭalai vityārcu (Vitiyarz) eṉṟa uyariya aṟiviyal pāṅkāṉa ariya kaṭalāyvuk kappal mūlam pallāṇṭu toṭarntu pala ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa kal tolaivu 1. cuṟṟulāc ceṉṟu perum poruḷ celaviṭṭu naṭattiya cuṟṟuc celaviṉ (expedition) muṭipukaḷum, tamiḻmakkaḷukku iṉṟiyamai yātaṉavākum.
cōviyat kaṭalāyvu muṭipukaḷ namakku iṉṉum kiṭaikka villai. ātittanallūr akaḻāyviṉ ciṟappai aṟintirāta cilar vāyk koḻuppuk koṇṭu tamiḻarkaḷukku 2000-m āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya vāḻvu illai eṉṟum āriya kulappirivil ivarkaḷ cūttirar eṉṟu karutappaṭuvar eṉṟum kūṟi vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ 50 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉiruntu atāvatu paḻaṅkallūḻikku muṉ iruntu vāḻntu, putiya kallūḻi irumpu ūḻi ākiyavaṟṟaik kaṭantu, iṉṟaiya varalāṟṟuk kālam varai vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmai, aṟiviyal muṟaiyil nilaināṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattiṉ mūtātaiyarkaḷ ilemūriyāk kaṇṭattil tōṉṟit tamiḻakam, nāvalantīvu oḷināṭu, mūnāṭu eṉṟellām kūṟum maṟaintu pōṉa nāṭukaḷil vāḻntavarkaḷ eṉṟu karuti annāṭu kaḷaip paṟṟiyum cōviyat aṟiñarkaḷum, piṉlāntup pēraṟiñar kaḷum, piriṭṭicu moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷum piṟarum mutal iṭai, kaṭaic caṅkaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum avaṟṟil veḷi vanta nūlkaḷaip paṟṟiyum anta nilattaip paṟṟiyum iṉṟu āya muṟpaṭṭuḷ ḷārkaḷ.
cintuveḷi akaḻāyvu tirāviṭa nākarikam ki.mu. 4000 ām āṇṭukaḷukku muntiyatu eṉṟa uṇmaiyai nilaināṭṭiyatu. tirāviṭarkaḷ ekiptu cumēriyā ellam, kirīṭ, meccikkō peru nāṭṭiṉarkaḷōṭu pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē toṭar puṭaiya makkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmaiyai nilaināṭṭiyatu. ātitta nallūr akaḻāyvu maṉita iṉattiṉ mūtātaiyarkaḷāṉa tamiḻar kaḷiṉ (tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ) āti muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ toṭṭil teṉṉintiyā eṉpatai uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu. cintuveḷi nākarikam iruḷārnta intiya varalāṟṟiṟku oru viṭiveḷḷiyākat tikaḻntatu. āṉāl, intiya varalāṟṟiṟku ātittanallūr nākarikam karumēkaṅkaḷ aṭarnta kār iruḷiṉ naṭuvē ātittaṉ taṉ poṟkatirkaḷai vīci iruḷiṉai akaṟṟi oḷitarum iḷam ñāyiṟu ākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟu iṉṟu vāḻum nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam vāynta aṟiñarkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
paṟampu amaippu
ip paṟampil akaḻ āyvu ceyvataṟkuriya iṭam īṇṭuḷḷa ciṟṟūraiyum uḷḷaṭakki, 114 ēkkar mēṟparappuḷḷa taricu nilamākum. intap paṟampu nilam tirunelvēlic cantippiṉiṉṟu ēṟattāḻa 20 kilōmīṭṭar tolaivil tirunelvēlic cantippu tiruccentūr araca pāṭṭaiyil uḷḷatu. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ip paṟampiṉ mēṟṟaḷam payirt toḻil ceyvataṟkut takutiyaṟṟa, payaṉaṟṟa kaḷar nilam. innilam paṇṭutoṭṭu iṭu kāṭākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. inta iṭukāṭṭiṟku aṇmaiyil oru ciṟṟūrum uḷḷatu. inta ūrukku aṇmaiyil teṉpuṟamākac cila piṇakkuḻikaḷum uḷa. itu niṉaivuk keṭṭāta kālantoṭṭu nilavi vantuḷḷatu. teṉ ticaiyāṉatu maraṇattiṉ teyvamāṉa emaṉ ticaiyeṉa eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. ūrukkut teṟkē eṅkum iṭukāṭukaḷum cuṭukāṭukaḷum iṭam peṟṟiruppatu iyalpu.
paṟampiṉ naṭuvil mūṉṟaṭi mēṟparappil caraḷaik kaṟkaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa. ataṉ aṭippakutiyil taṉikkūṟākac citainta paṭikakkal pāṟai irukkiṟatu. piṇaṅkaḷait tāḻikaḷil vaittup putaippataṟkāka akaḻntu ovvoru piṇattiṟkum taṉittaṉiyāka uṭkuṭaivu ceyyappaṭṭirukkum puḻaivu pāṟaikaḷil uḷa.
inta iṭukāṭṭil vāyciṟuttum uṭal peruttum nīṇṭa vaṭivam vāynta periya tāḻikaḷ pala akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tāḻikaḷ ēṟattāḻa 6 aṭi āḻattil putaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mēl maṭṭattiliruntu 3 aṭi mutal 12 aṭivarai āḻamum uḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟiṉmītu oṉṟākavum putaikkappaṭṭu irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇavum iṭantarukiṟatu. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya putai kuḻikaḷil itutāṉ mikap paranta iṭukāṭākum. paṇṭaiya ceṉṉai mānilattil (iṉṟaiya tamiḻnāṭṭil) pala pakutikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa putai kuḻikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṅkum ātittanallūr pōṉṟa periya parappuḷḷa iṭukāṭukaḷum, atika eṇṇikkaiyuḷḷa tāḻikaḷum (putai kalaṉkaḷum) kāṇap paṭavillai.
īmattāḻikaḷum avaṟṟiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷum
īmattāḻikaḷum avaṟṟiṉuḷḷē elumpukaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu pōṉṟa tātupporuḷkaḷā lāṉa ēṉaṅkaḷum, kūlapporuḷkaḷum tuṇimaṇikaḷum vaittup putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟaippaṟṟi virivākap piṉvarum pakutikaḷil viḷakkam tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷil vaittup putaikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷ eṉṟum avai avarkaḷukku viruppamāṉavaiyeṉṟum iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ maṟu ulaka vāḻkkaiyil payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷumāṟu avarkaḷuṭaṉ vaittup putaikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷil, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷum, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷum vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷāṉa erumai, āṭu, māṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ veṇkala uruvaṅkaḷum kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷāṉa puli, yāṉai, maṟi, māṉ pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷum iruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mēlum makkaḷ aṇiyum vaḷai, mōtiram, kāppu, kaṭakam, kaṭukkaṉ pōṉṟavaiyum uḷa. paṟavai uruvaṅkaḷum, veṇkala aritaṭṭu (sieves) kiṇṇa vaṭivilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kiṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟamuḷḷa pakuti meṉmaiyāṉa aṭiyil poruttappaṭṭa takaṭu kaṉattatu. kiṇṇat til tuḷaiyiṭutal (perforation) palvēṟu vakaiyil uḷa. pāṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ mītu irumpuk karuviyāl aḻuttappaṭṭa puḷḷi vaṭivaṅkaḷ aṇiaṇiyāy aḻakuṟa amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ciṟappāka aṭip pākattaic cuṟṟi orē naṭuviṭamuḷḷa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum, arai vaṭṭaṅ kaḷum tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil caṭṭattaic cuṟṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu poruntumāṟum poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mēlum pala maṉita elumpukaḷum maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil oru talaiyōṭu ciṟappāka nilattiṉiṉṟu muḻuvatum nīṅkiya tāḻiyiṉ aṭippakutiyiṉiṉṟu ataṉ aṭippaṭai uruvam ciṟitum kuṉṟātu cīraḻiyātu nallanilai yil eṭukkappaṭṭatu. it tāḻiyil periya aḷavil elumpukaḷ aṭaṅki iruntaṉa. ciṟiya elumpukaḷ citaintum cīrkeṭṭum pōyiruntaṉa. kai elumpukaḷ talaiyōṭṭiṟkuk kīḻē kūṭutalāka iruntaṉa. mutukelumpum, vilāvelumpum ēṉaiya ciṟiya elumpu kaḷum uṭampiṟkup piṉṉāl irukkum eṉṟu evarum etir pārppar. āṉāl, tāḻiyil elumpukkūṭu uṭkārntirukkum nilaiyil, keñcip piṟar ātaravait tēṭum nilaiyil vaikkappaṭ ṭiruntatu. tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa elumpuk kūṭukaḷil muḻu elumpukaḷum aṭaṅki iruppatu cilavaṟṟiltāṉ kāṇappeṟṟaṉa. ivai kiṭṭattaṭṭa mūṉṟaṭik kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷa periya tāḻikaḷiltām peritum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
muṉaivar tarcuṭṭaṉ (Dr. Thurston) ātittanallūr talai yōṭukaḷil ceṉṉaip paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyiṉ cēkarippil uḷḷa, aḵtāvatu nalla nilaiyil uḷḷa - āṟu talai yōṭukaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷai namakku aḷittuḷḷār. avaṟṟiṉ aḷavukaḷ aṭiyil varuvaṉavākum :

intat talaiyōṭukaḷil iraṇṭu tāṭai elumpukaḷ nīṇṭaṉavāka iruppatu teḷivākat terikiṟatu. paḻamporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil uḷḷavaṟṟil āntirar talaiyōṭukaḷilum tamiḻar jlaiyōṭukaḷilummvaravarkaḷuṭaiyakrapukTWkaḷna‹kupuyppaṭukiṉṟaṉa.kY« tiru. tarcuṭṭaṉ iṉṟaiya tamiḻakattil vāḻum nīṇṭa talaiyōṭṭaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷil (Dolichocephalic) vakuppil eñciyirukkum mika nīṇṭa talaiyōṭṭaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ (Hyper dolichocephalic) vakuppu iṉaṅkaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa aḷavu vēṟṟumaikaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu uḷḷaṉaveṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷār :

iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭampaic cuṭukāṭṭiliṭṭuc cuṭṭapiṉ anta elumpukaḷait tērnteṭukkum intiya muṟaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu ātittanallūril cāvu viṉaikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutalām. eṉṟālum aṅkup piṇaṅkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ illai. všyh Éjkhd f©L ão¥òfS« m§F vY«òfŸ òij¡f¥ g£ld v‹w c©ikia¤jh‹ cz®¤âíŸsd.*
cila elumpukaḷil pañcāṭaiyiṉ pativaṭaiyāḷam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tātupporuḷkaḷiṉ turuvōṭu iṇaintu nīṇṭa nāḷāka oruppaṭṭiruntatiṉ cāyal naṉku terikiṟatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷil arici, tiṉai, cāmai pōṉṟa kūlap poruḷkaḷ tāḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tuṇiyaip pōlaṉṟi ataṟku māṟāka ōr aṅkula nīḷattil tuṇṭutuṇṭāka aprēkattiṉ uṭainta tuṇṭukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cila poruḷkaḷ kallālāṉa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṅkiya cenniṟam vāynta maṇippācikaḷum veṇkalappācikaḷum kaḻuttu mālaikaḷumākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. putaikuḻikaḷukku veḷiyē cantaṉakkaṭṭaiyiṉ aravaikkallum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟāka ventu uṟutiyākavum aḻakākavum kāṇappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷuḷḷē kallāl ceyyappeṟṟa cila pāttiraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇṇai melliyatākat taṭṭi aḻakiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷāka vaṉaintu cūḷaiyil iṭṭu vēkavaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai civappu, kaṟuppu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil kaviṉuṟak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu vaṇṇaṅ kaḷum oṉṟu cērntu orumittu oḷirkiṉṟaṉa. orucila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷ vaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyum, cuṇṇam tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉa vāyum polivuṟṟiruntaṉa. ivai ciṟiya aḷavil aḻaku ceyyap paṭṭiruntaṉa. aṭuttaṭuttuḷḷa cila puḷḷikaḷ caṭṭattaic cuṟṟi mūlaiviṭṭattōṭu niralpaṭat tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tāḻikaḷ kaṉam vāyntaṉavāyum cevvaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyum karaṭu muraṭākavum ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
ātittanallūrp paṟampu tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaikku oru vaṟṟāta karuvūla ūṟṟāka nilavukiṟatu. aḵtu eṭukka eṭukkak kuṟaiyāta vilaiyuyarnta paḻamporuḷkaḷait taṉṉakattē aṭakki vaittuk koṇṭirukkum kaḷañciyamākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. iṅku īmakkāṭṭil namatu mūtātaiyarkaḷāl cēmittu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa paḻam poruḷkaḷ pala uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ cila maṭṭumē akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭu ayalnāṭukaḷilum intiyāvilu muḷḷa paḻam poruḷ kāṭciccālaikaḷil kavarcciyāṉa kāraṇap poruḷākap paṇpuruvam vāynta pāl poruḷāka matikkappeṟṟuk kavarcci yāṉa uyariya iṭaṅkaḷil vaikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅkup periya aḷavil mūṉṟu akaḻāyvukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyilum cilar akaḻāyvu naṭattip pala ariya poruḷkaḷai eṭuttut tiraṭṭi vaittuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅku naṭaipeṟṟa ovvōr akaḻāyvum putiya oḷiyiṉai vaḻaṅki evaiyēṉum cila putiya poruḷkaḷai aḷittu uḷḷaṉa. āyvu naṭattiya aṟiñarkaḷiṭaiyē pala putiya karuttukkaḷai arumpac ceytuḷḷaṉa. atōṭu itaip pōṉṟa payaṉuḷḷa akaḻāyvu ituvarai tōṉṟavillai. intiyā vil varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ciṉṉaṅkaḷai aṭakki yirunta pala pāṅkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, etaiyum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷukku oppuk kūṟa muṭiyātu. iḵtaṉṟi ātittanallūrp paṟampiṉ akaṉṟa periya nilapparappu veḷiyil mikutiyāṉa aḷavu iṉṉum toṭap paṭāmalē iruntu varukiṟatu. kiḻakkiluḷḷa mēṭṭunilam aḵtā vatu ātittanallūr kirāma ellaikkuṭpaṭṭa iṭam periya aḷavil kaṉṉi nilamāka iṉṉum iruppatākac collappaṭukiṟatu. inta iṭattai ōr aṭi āḻam akaḻntu pārttāl mēṭṭu nilattiliruntu mēṟparappil maḻai nīrāl arittuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭa caraḷ taḷam kāṇappaṭu kiṟatu. ataṉkīḻ āṟu aṭi āḻattiṟkuc cāmpal, elumpukaḷ, vilaṅ kiṉ kompukaḷ, uṭainta ōṭṭut tuṇṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kalanta maṇ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. curukkamākak kūṟiṉāl kirāmattil uḷḷa paḻaṅkālak kaḻivup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collalām.
akaḻāyviṟ kaṇṭa ariya poruḷkaḷ
akaḻāyvup poruḷkaḷil tāḻikaḷil uḷḷa elumpukaḷ talaiyōṭukaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaiviṭac ciṟappāṉa poruḷkaḷ tātup poruḷkaḷ eṉalām. tātup poruḷkaḷil poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliyavai ciṟappāṉavai. poṉ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ talaiyil kaṭṭappaṭum paṭṭattiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu. veṇkalam pāttiraṅkaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavai ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭatu. irumpu poṉṉālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum vēṟuvakaiyāṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum ceyvataṟku uriya karuvikaḷaic ceyyap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. taccuvēlai, kollu vēlai, kaṉṉār vēlai, kalvēlai, poṟkollar vēlai mutaliyavaṟṟukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uḷi, cuttiyal, paṟṟuk koraṭu, piṭittu arāvum aram, iḻaippuḷi mutaliya karuvikaḷ ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭatu. uḻavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum koḻu, arivāḷ, paṉṉarivāḷ, kaṭappāṟai, maṇveṭṭi mutaliya karuvikaḷ ceyyavum, vīṭṭu vēlaikku uriya arivāḷmaṇai tēṅkāyt tiruki, katti, veṭṭarivāḷ, karaṇṭi, curaṇṭi, akappai pōṉṟavai ceyyavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa paṭaik kalaṉkaḷum irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavai kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṅkuk kiṭaikkappeṟṟa ēṉaṅkaḷukkum palakai mūṭikaḷ pōṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irumpuk karuvikaḷukku marattiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭa kaippiṭikaḷ poruttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷiṉ uḷḷē yum, veḷiyēyum ērāḷamāṉa eṇṇikkaiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil cempō veṇkalamō kiṭaiyātu. vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu cempum, veṇkalamum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu iṅkup pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷai nāṭṭiṉ uyarnta iṉattārkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭutti vantuḷḷaṉar. atu tamiḻ nāṭṭil kiṭaitta tātupporuḷākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭil mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ kālaṭi eṭuttu vaippataṟku muṉpē, poṉ ceyyum taṭṭārkaḷum, maravēlai ceyyum taccarkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṉaiyum maṭkōvarkaḷum, nūlāṭaikaḷ neyyum kaikkōḷarkaḷum, vittukkaḷaic cekkiliṭṭu āṭṭi ney eṭukkum eṇṇey vaṇikarkaḷum, makkaḷuṭaiya uṭalukku uṟum piṇikaḷai akaṟṟum maruttuvarkaḷumākap palvēṟu toḻilāḷarkaḷ iruntu vantaṉar. palvēṟu toḻilāḷarkaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷ ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭaikkap peṟṟamaiyiṉaik koṇṭu toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻnāṭṭil pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ iruntu vantaṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām.5

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
III
1.	Proceedings of the Madras Government No. 329 of 27th March 1876.
2.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur by A. Rea (Madras) 1915. Preface p. iii-v.
	(a)	“We commenced excavation in the side of a hill consisting chiefly of quarts gravel with holders of the same material and resting on gneiss rock and within a superficial area of twenty five or thirty square yards, we discovered from twenty to thirty baked ear thern pots varying in size from three feet nine by three feet six down to ten inches either way, of very various shapes and of shapes in most cases more elegant and of a better manufacture than any one sees at the present day in the bazaars.
	“These pots when examined, were found to contain besides earth, gravel and stones a variety of objects of great interest” to the antiquarian the ethnologist and to Science generally.
	“Upwards of fifty kind of baked earthernware utensil of all sizes and shapes, a considerable number of iron weapons and implements chiefly knives or short sword-blades and hatchets, and a great quantity of bones and skulls were discovered. In one very interesting case two small pots were found within a large one, together with the bones as in most cases of a nearly complete skeleton, containing what it was impossible to mistake for anything else but the outer coats of grains itself had disappeared, but outer coating-probably of silica-had remained.
	“Several places at considerable distances, one at least 300 or 400 yards a from out principal excavation, were found to contain similar sepulchral urns, and the surface of the quarts hill above was strewn over with broken pieces of pottery of a similar character. Our excavation in all probability only touched one point of an extensive ancient cemetery or catacomb in which the dead of an age, the habits of which are probably quite unknown in India at the present day, are disposed of in this singular manner, that is to say, by placing the skeleton or the body inside a large earthenware vase or pot together with two, three or four small pots together with two, three or four small pots apparently containing food for the dead and weapons and implements of iron ready for use when he reached another world”.
	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbair - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras. 1915 - Introduction.
3.	Ibid. Preface 1.
4.	Ibid. Introduction p. 1-5.
5.	A History of India by James H. Gense S.J. (Madras) 1965. 
pp. 16-17.
	“Aryans were the first invaders of India. They came originally from the treeless grass lands or stepps of South Russia. They were peaceful farmers, the word” “Aryan” meaning “tiller of the earth”.
	(1)	The Punjab. In the hymns of the Rig Veda the names of about twenty-five rivers are mentioned and these many rivers all belong to the system of Indus. They include the five great eastern branches of the Indus and a number of its north-western tributaries. We may therefore conclude that the earliest Aryan invaders made their homes partly to the west and parttl to the east of the Indus.
	(2)	Hindustan Proper. As years went on the decendants of the first immigrants probably increased by fresh arrivals travelled gradually eastwards and occupied the basins of the Jamuna and the Ganges or Hindustan proper ....
	(3)	TheVindhya Range line. In the Course of time the Aryans spread both southwards and eastwards conquered the whole country lying between the Himalayas and the Vindhya Range and reduced the Dasyus to slavery.
	The fresh advance is mentioned in the Laws of Manu. According to these the wide territories contained between the Himalayas and the Vindhyas Range are called Aryavarta or the country of the Aryans. In the land of Aryavarta there was one part spot or rather province, that was privileged above all the others. This was Brahmavarta, the Holy land, situated due north of modern Delhi and to the south of the upper course of the Sutlej between the river Saraswathi and Drishadvati. It was in this region that the Rig Veda is believed to have been completed. Tehre is a couplet in the Mahabharata to the following effect : ‘Those who dwell in the Kurukshetra to the south of the Saraswathi and the north of the Drishadvati dwell in Heaven!’
	(4)	Aryan Penetration of the Deccan. The last stage in the Aryan conquest was a movement southwards. To the south lay the country which they called Dakshina of which the English world Deccan is said to be a corruption. The Aryan penetration of the Deccan was chiefly confined to the spread of Aryan ideas and institutions among the Dravidians of the South. But this peaceful penetration of theSouth, does not belong to the Vedic Period, it took place many centuries afterwards.
intap pakuti ākkattiṟku utaviya nūlkaḷ
