4. poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ
(GOLD DIADEMS)
ātittanallūrp paṟampil iṟutiyāka aṟiñar alekcāntar irīyā avarkaḷ naṭattiya tolporuḷ āyvil kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ pala. āṉāl, iṅku mutaṉmutalākak kuṟippiṭat takka poruḷkaḷ poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷēyākum. intap poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷ ātitta nallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ elumpu kaḷukkum maṇṇiṟkum ūṭē oḷi kuṉṟātu miṉṉeṉat tikaḻ vatai aṟintu eṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṟiñar irīyā akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ mottam irupattu mūṉṟu poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭam kaṭṭal
ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil arum piya ariya poruḷ kaḷaip paṟṟi aṟiñar irīyā avarkaḷ kūṟum poḻutu tāḻikaḷuḷ poṉṉālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷum veṇ kalattālāṉa caṭṭikaḷum viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum irumpā lāṉa paṭaikkalaṅkaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷum ciṟu tāḻikaḷum piṟavum iruntaṉa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. poṉṉā lāṉa poruḷ paṭṭaṅkaḷ maṭṭumēyākum. avai iṟantārkku iyaṟṟum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟāṉa paṭṭam kaṭṭum cey muṟaiyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. uyir nīttār neṟṟiyil ip paṭṭattaik kaṭṭuvatu tamiḻar marapu. ippaṭṭam peritum muṭṭai vaṭivilē uḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa caturavaṭiviṉālāṉa orucila paṭṭaṅ kaḷum ōrōr iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭṭattiṉ iṟutiyil irupuṟamum kayiṟu allatu kampi kaṭṭuvataṟkuc ciṟiya aḷavil tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭṭam melliya poṉ takaṭṭil aḻaku poliya mukkōṇa vaṭivil kaṭuku pōṉṟa puḷḷikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ellām maṭittuc curukki vaikkak kūṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. ivai iṟantārk kuriya caṭaṅkukaḷukkut takka kuṟiyīṭāka amainta poruḷkaḷākum eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu irīyā avarkaḷ viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār.
paṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ amaippu
paṭṭaṅkaḷ potuvāka muṭṭai vaṭivil irukkum. ivai melliya poṉ takaṭṭil ceyyappaṭṭu iru nuṉikaḷilum tuḷaiyiṭṭu nūl allatu melliya kampiyāl neṟṟiyil kaṭṭap paṭum. iḵtu iṟantārkku iyaṟṟum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟākum. iṟantārkku neṟṟiyil poṉ paṭṭam kaṭṭappaṭuvatu perum pālum caiva vēḷāḷar marapiṉar iṉṟum ceytuvarum iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷil oṉṟākum.
poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ cila tuḷaiyiṭappaṭāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. poṉ paṭṭam mika melliyatāka iruppatāl ciṟiya irumpuk kampikaḷaik koṇṭu eḷitākat tuḷaiyiṭṭu appāl antak kampikaḷaik koṇṭē paṭṭamākak kaṭṭi neṟṟiyil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytirukkiṟārkaḷ. ippoṟpaṭṭam cila iṭaṅkaḷil nīḷamāṉatākavum, aḻakiya mukkōṇa vaṭivākavum irukkiṟatu. āṉāl, muṉpuṟam puḷḷikaḷ kaṭukuvaṭivil aṇiaṇiyākat turuttik koṇṭirukkum. muṟkālattil paṭṭaṅkaḷ māṟṟuyarnta poṉṉilē ceytu kaṭṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa. iṉṟu poṉvilai peritum uyarntupōṉatāl paṭṭam māṟṟukkuṟainta poṉṉil kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. palar paṭṭam kaṭṭum vaḻakkattaiyē viṭṭum varukiṉṟaṉar. potuvākap poṉṉiṉ vilai uyarvāl tamiḻarkaḷ talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka vaḻakkil ucciyiliruntu uḷḷaṅkāl varai uṟuppukaḷ aṉaittilum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintu varum marapu vaḻakkaṅkaḷaik kūṭa ovvoṉṟāka viṭṭuvarukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
mukkōṇa vaṭivil aṇikalaṉkaḷ pōl aḻakuṭaṉ tayārikkappeṟṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷ ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ piṉpuṟam muttu muttāy aḻakiya vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa ciṟu puḷḷikaḷ mēl eḻumpi niṟpaṉapōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa (uruvam 15 mutal 20 varai pārkka). mūṉṟu ciṟiya paṭṭaṅkaḷ tāḻnta ulōkattāl ceyyappaṭṭavaiyākum (uruvam 18 mutal 20 varaiyuḷḷavai pārkka) muṭṭai vaṭivilāṉa neṭṭaiyāṉa neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ 2.3/1611 7/811 mutal 6-5/811 2.5/32 varaiyuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoru ciṟiya paṭṭam oṭukkamāṉa kōṭukaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟatāy 15/16 nīḷamum 1/411 akalamum uḷḷatu. eṇ 1 aṭaiyāḷa miṭappaṭṭa neṟṟippaṭṭattil iru varicaiyākat tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷa puḷḷikaḷ uḷḷa iṇaivāṉa kōṭukaḷ iraṇṭu nuṉikaḷilum toṭarntu cellukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭukaḷ naṭuvē ūṭuruvic ceṅkuttākac cellukiṉṟaṉa. ivai pōṉṟa iṇaivāṉa kōṭukaḷ iru pakkaṅkaḷiṉ mītum cāyvāṉa kiḷai yāka etirppuṟam uḷḷaṉa. avai takaṭṭiṉ iṭaivari mēṭṭai ottuḷḷaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṇkaḷ 2-10 paṭṭaṅkaḷi luḷḷa puḷḷikaḷ iru iṇaivāṉa varikaḷ uḷḷa amaippu iṟutivarai vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavē ceṉṟuḷḷatu. atōṭu mūṉṟu iṇaivāṉa kōṭukaḷ naṭuvē ceṅkuttākac cellukiṉṟaṉa.
eṇ 20 uḷḷa paṭṭattil pōyilikkāy vaṭivil kal nīrttuḷi pōṉṟu taṅkat takaṭṭil takatakaveṉṟu miṉṉumpaṭi cetukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. itaṉai aṟiñar irīyā aḻakupeṟa viḷakkikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. irīyā avarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭutta taṅkat takaṭukaḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ neṟṟiyil kaṭṭum paṭṭam eṉpatai jē.ār. heṇṭarcaṉ. ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
cintuveḷip poṉpaṭṭam
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭilum iṟantupōṉavarkaḷiṉ cavac caṭaṅkil paṭṭaṅkaṭṭuvatum peruvaḻakkāka iruntatu eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷiyil naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa cuṇṇāmpuc cilai namatu kavaṉattai īrppatāyuḷḷatu. atu tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ cāyalil uḷḷatu. aḵtu ōr aracar allatu purōkitar uruvampōl uḷḷatu. antac cilai mārpiṟkuk kīḻē uṭaintupōṉa tākat terikiṟatu. ataṉ mēl caṭṭaikaḷ illai. ōraḻakāṉa pūvēlaippāṭu uḷḷa pōrvai valap puṟamuḷḷa iṭuppi liruntu iṭappuṟattōḷmītu pōrttap paṭṭu mutukuppuṟamākat toṅkaviṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. mukattil tāṭi mayir oṭṭa veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. akaṉṟa mukamum taṭṭaiyāṉa taṭitta mūkkum periya utaṭum paranta neṟṟiyum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. neṟṟiyil oru melliya nāṭā vil paṭṭattaik kōttup piṉpuṟam muṭiccuppōṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eñciya nāṭāviṉ ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷ piṉpuṟam aḻakuṟat toṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
itaṉaic cintuveḷiyil akaḻ āyvu naṭattiya pēraṟiñarkaḷ car. cāṇ mārcal, car. mārṭṭiyar uyilar, ār.ṭi. pāṉarji, muṉaivar i. cē. eyc. em. ecu. mekkē.vāṭcu pōṉṟa aṟiñarka ḷellārum eṭuttukkāṭṭit tirāviṭarkaḷ paṭṭam kaṭṭum marapu vaḻakkam uṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉpataṟkum cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉpataṟkum cilaiyiṉ neṟṟiyil uḷḷa paṭṭamum, cilaiyiṉ muka amaippum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭu kaḷākum eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar.3 cintuveḷiyiṉiṉṟu ātitta nallūrvarai tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu nilaviyuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku ātitta nallūril akaḻntukaṇṭa poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷum cintuveḷiyil kiṭaitta tirāviṭa aracaṉiṉ neṟṟi naṭuvil kāṇum paṭṭamum aḻiyāc cāṉṟukaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa eṉalām.
aṟiñar kilparṭ cilēṭṭar āṅkilattil eḻutiya intiya nākarikattil tirāviṭap paṇpu eṉṟa cīriya nūlil ātitta nallūriṉ akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa neṟṟip poṉ paṭṭam tirāviṭa makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya ekiptu naṭunilak kaṭaṟpakuti yil kirīṭṭu, piṉiciyā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām vaḻakkil iruntatu eṉpatai virivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atu varumāṟu :
mēlum ātittanallūrc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum mika melliyatāka aṭittut tuvaikkappaṭṭa poṉ neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai perumpālum kurumārkaḷiṉ neṟṟip paṭṭaṅkaḷāka amaintiruntaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu. avai muṟṟilum maicēṉāvilum kirīṭṭilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭavaṟṟaip pōṉ ṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai tavira oru veṇkala uruvam ekimō iṉattavarukku uriya āṭai aṇinta ōr arampaiyiṉ aḻakuruvi lum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intat taṅka neṟṟip paṭṭam oru puṟamum aitarāpāttuk kaṟkuviyalkaḷil uḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ uḷveṭṭuk kīṟṟukkaḷ maṟṟoru puṟamumāka niṉṟu āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar kaḷukkum, nōcācuvait (Knossus) talainakarākak koṇṭu vāḻnta kirīṭṭiṉ kaṭalōṭi nākarikattiṉarukkum toṭarpiruntirukka vaḻi yuṇṭu eṉpataik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. pīṉiciyarkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭē miṉōvarkaḷ naṭunilakkaṭal tīraṅkaḷilum orukāl aṭlāṇṭit tīraṅkaḷilum ceṉṟu paranta toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. atēpōlac ceṅkaṭal, pāracīka vaḷaikuṭā, intumākkaṭal ākiyavaṟṟaiyum muṉaintukāṇpatil muṉṉōṭikaḷāyirun tārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟac cāṉṟukaḷ illai. itaṟku māṟāka mīṉō varkaḷ mēṟku nōkkiyum pīṉiciyar kiḻakku mēṟku nōkkiyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa orēkālattilēyē paravat toṭaṅki iruntaṉar eṉṟu niṉaikka iṭamuṇṭu.
eṉavē, cariyotta ivviru nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ parappukaḷukkum kāraṇamāṉa kappal kaṭṭum toḻilum kaṭal pōkkuvarattup paḻakkamum, miṉōvarkaḷiṉ putuvatu puṉaiviṟkō, pīṉiciyariṉ putuvatu puṉaiviṟkō uriyatu eṉṟu koḷvataiviṭa ekipti yarukku uriyateṉṟu eṇṇuvaru ēṟṟamuṭaiyatākum. aitarāpāttu kaṟkukaip pāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ, perumpālum pīṉiciyat toṭarpu, miṉōvat toṭarpu ākiya iraṇṭiṉ viḷaivukaḷāka vum irukkalām. attuṭaṉ pīṉiciyak kurumārum itupōṉṟa neṟṟip paṭṭam aṇintiruntaṉar.
tirāviṭarkaḷ nēraṭiyākavō, cuṟṟumuṟaiyākavō nōcācu (Knossus) nākarikattuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākak kūṟalām. teyvaṅkaḷ civaṉaippōl āṇpālāyiṉum cari, kāḷiyaip pōl peṇpālāyiṉum cari, iru pālkaḷumē mikaippaṭuttap paṭṭu nuṇṇiṭai uṭaiyavarāka atuvum iṭaiyē oṭukkuvataṟ kēṟṟa karuvi etuvum illātapaṭi iṭai oṭuṅkiyavarākavē amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. miṉōvakak kalaiyil iṭai iṉṉum ciṟitu. āṇkaḷum, peṇkaḷum iṭaiyil oṭṭiyāṇam aṇipa varkaḷāyk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. itu kuḻantaip paruva mutal toṭarntu vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. iṭaiyaic ciṟukka vaikkum vaḻakkam intiyāvil paravāviṭiṉum ap paḻakkattai aṭip paṭaiyākak koṇṭa kalaimarapu maṭṭum intiyā varai vantu paraviṟṟu eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
tamiḻar paṇpāṭāṉa poṉpaṭṭam kaṭṭum paḻakkamum, oṭṭiyāṇam aṇiyum vaḻakkamum, peṇkaḷukku iṭai curuṅki iruttal aḻaku eṉṟa tamiḻar kaṇṭa aḻakuc ciṟappum kirīṭṭu, ekiptu, pīṉiciyā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷi lellām paraviyuḷḷatu eṉṟu naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.4
teṉ intiya makkaḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattiṉiṉṟu - ciṟappāka ulōkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷaip periya aḷavil aṇintuvarum vaḻakkam uṭaiyavar eṉpatu namatu kavaṉattiṟku varukiṉṟatu. irumpukkālattil uḷḷa iṭukāṭukaḷil poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu pala paṭaikkaruvikaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷum putaikkappaṭṭu vantatuḷḷaṉa. aṟiviṟ keṭṭāta paḻaṅkālattiliruntu palvēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum palvēṟu aḷavil poṉaṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum maṇikaḷ iḻaitta poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum ceyvatil tamiḻnāṭṭup poṟkollarkaḷ kaivaṇṇam vāyntavarkaḷ eṉpataṟku ipporuḷ cāṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mutalil poṉṉaik kaṇṭa nāṭu
intiyāvilē, mutaṉmutalākat tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa ulōkap poruḷ poṉ ākum. atu vaṇika muṟaiyil pala ūḻikkālaṅkaḷil eṅkum paraviyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu intiya araciṉ kaṭumaiyāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷāl poṉ kiṭaippatu aritākiviṭṭatu. vilaiyō naccuppōl ēṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟālum, tamiḻaka makaḷiriṉ poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyum marapuvaḻakkam kaiviṭa muṭiyāta tāy irukkiṟatu. kāraṇam avarkaḷiṭaiyē paṇṭu toṭṭu nilavi varum intap paḻakkam, camayattōṭum, kulattōṭum iṇaikkap paṭṭatāka iruppatēyākum. itaṉai aṟiñar vi.ār. irāmacantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ tam nūlil virivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
intiyāvil - ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil tirumaṇattil peṇ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatum piḷḷaip paruvattilē kātu kuttik kaṭukkaṉ aṇivippatum tirumaṇattiṉ pōtu āṇum peṇṇum aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭu aḻakuṟa iruppatum ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷ kūṭa palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷil aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭiruppa tum, teyva uruvaṅkaḷukkut talaiyiliruntu kālkaḷ varai poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṭṭiyiruppatum, teyvaṅkaḷum aracarkaḷum poṟṟērkaḷilum poṉ pallakkukaḷilum ulāvaruvatum maṉṉarkaḷ poṟkācukaḷ veḷiyiṭṭu varuvatum nām aṟiyum poḻutu paṇṭaiya intiyāvil ērāḷamākap poṉ iruntatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. intiyāviṉiṉṟu poṉ ekiptu, mecapot tāmiyā, ellam, pālattīṉam mutaliyanāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭatākak kūṭat terikiṟatu.5
poṉviḷaiyum pūmi
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil ērāḷamākap poṉ viḷaintatu. paṇṭait tamiḻakattil poṉviḷainta kaḷattūr, poṉṉēri, poṉṉāṇi, poṉṉūr, poṉ amarāvati, poṉvayal, poṉ mukanallūr eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷilellām poṉ kiṭaittuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻakattil mutaṉmutalākak kiṭaikkap peṟṟa mañcaḷvaṇṇattil takatakaveṉa oḷiyūṭṭiya tātupporu ḷukkup poṉ eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu. avarkaḷ aṉṟu poṉṉai māḻai (ulōkam) eṉṟa poruḷiltāṉ peyar cūṭṭiṉar. piṉṉark kiṭaitta karuppu vaṇṇam vāynta tātup poruḷukkuk karumpoṉ eṉṟu peyar aḷittaṉar. atu piṉṉar, irumpāyiṟṟu, veṇmai niṟamāṉa tātupporuḷaik kaṇṭatum atai veṇpoṉ eṉṟaṉar. atu iṉṟu veḷḷiyāyt tiripuṟṟatu. civappu niṟamāṉa tātup poruḷaic cempoṉ eṉṟu aḻaittaṉar. atu piṟkālattil cempu eṉṟum ceppu eṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭatu.6
tamiḻakattil poṉ ērāḷamākak kiṭaittatu. vaṇika muṟai yil yavaṉa nāṭṭiṉar poṉṉaik kācukaḷākavum poṟpāḷaṅ kaḷākavum, poṟkaṭṭikaḷākavum ērāḷamākap peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻakat til āṅkila āṭci arumpu muṉ makkaḷ ucciyiliruntu uḷḷaṅ kālvarai poṉ aṇikaḷaiyum maṇikaḷaiyum pūṇṭiruntaṉar. poṉṉiḻai koṇṭa paṭṭāṭaikaḷaip puṉaintu vantaṉar. poṉvaṭṭililum, poṉkumpāvilum uṇavu uṇṭum poṟ kiṇṇaṅkaḷilum, poṉ kalayaṅkaḷilum nīr aruntiyum vantaṉar. uṭal uramuṟa, mēṉi civakka, piṇi akalap poṉṉiṟ ceyta maruntiṉai (poṉpacupam) arunti vantaṉar. tirukkōyil vimāṉaṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa kuṭaṅkaḷum, vāyil kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa kuṭaṅkaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa. tillai yil uḷḷa āṭavallāṉiṉ uruvam poṉṉāl vārkkappeṟṟa tāy iruntatu. anta āṭavallāṉ āṭum tirucciṟṟampalam poṉ ōṭukaḷ vēyappeṟṟu poṉṉampalam eṉṟu iṉṟum pukaḻn tēttap peṟṟu varukiṟatu. palvēṟu kōyilkaḷil tiruvulāp paṭimaikaḷ poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟavaikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. tirukkōyilkaḷiṉ poṉ viḷakku vaittu ney vārttu ēṟṟip pēṟu peṟṟavar nam nāṭṭil palar uṇṭu. iṟaivaṉ tiruvuruvaip poṟṟēril ēṟṟi ulā varacceytaṉar. teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷai muḻukkāṭṭa, poṟkuṭaṅkaḷum teyvaṅkaḷukku uṇavu paṭaikkap poṟṟaṭṭum poṟkalaṅkaḷum palar nērttikkaṭaṉāka aḷitta taṟkuk kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷum, vaḷḷalkaḷum, pulavarkaḷukkum pāṇarkaḷukkum, pāṭiṉikaḷukkum, viṟaliyarkaḷukkum poṟ ṟāmaraippūvum poṟkiḻiyum, poṟ kācukaḷum, poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷum varaiyātaḷittup pērum perumaiyum īṭṭiṉar eṉpa taṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. araṇmaṉai kaḷilum, kōyilkaḷilumuḷḷa yāṉaikaḷ viḻānāṭkaḷil (poṉ mukapaṭāmum), poṉ ōṭaiyum, kompukaḷil kimpuri eṉṉum poṉ pūṇkaḷum, poṉ ampārikaḷum tāṅki iruntaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa kōnakaraṅkaḷil poṉ vaṇikarkaḷiṉ kaṭait terukkaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅku, cātarūpam, kiḷicciṟai eṉṟa māṟṟuk kuṟainta iḷam poṉvakaikaḷum āṭakam, cāmpūnatam eṉṟa māṟṟuyarnta cempoṉ vakaikaḷum viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.
māṟṟuyarnta pacumpoṉṉait tamiḻar pattarai māṟṟup paimpoṉ eṉpar. itu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyvataṟkum, maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉ camaippataṟkum payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟatu. pacum poṉ kuḻaivāṉatu; atu kuḻaiyāmal irukkavum, vaḷaiyāmal irukkavum ataṉōṭu, ciṟitu cempu kalantu aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytaṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaṇikam naṭattip pērum perumaiyum peṟṟavarkaḷ. mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē marakkalaṉkaḷ kaṭṭi ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, ilaṅkai, malaiyam, cāvakam pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam vaḷarttavarkaḷ, yavaṉa nāṭṭiṟku miḷaku, cantaṉam, muttu, ēlam, kirāmpu, melliya tuṇi mutaliyavaṟṟai ēṟṟumati ceytu ataṟku īṭākap poṟkaṭṭikaḷai yum poṟkācukaḷaiyum peṟṟup poṉṉai malaiyeṉak kuvittavarkaḷ.7
aḻakiya peṇṇaip poṟpāvai eṉṟu pukaḻntu pōṟṟiṉar. eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikaḷ aṇinta arampaiyaraip poṉ kāytta pūṅkoṭi eṉap pukaḻntaṉar. civaperumāṉaip poṉṉār mēṉiyaṉē eṉṟu ēṟṟip pōṟṟiṉar.
putiya kaṟkālattiṉ naṭuvilē tamiḻnāṭu poṉṉaiyum ataṉait tōṇṭiyeṭuppataṟku ēṟṟa irumpuk karuvikaḷaiyum periya aḷavil peṟṟiruntatu. kaltēyarkaḷiṉ (Chaldean) kalveṭṭukaḷil ūr nakaril poṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatāka aṟikiṉṟōm. antap poṉ anta nāṭṭiluḷḷataṉṟu. ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. anta ayal nāṭukaḷ intiyāvum āppirikkāvumēyākum. teṉ intiyap poṉ, āppirikkāvil viṟkappaṭṭatu. āppirikkā antap poṉṉil tēvaiyāṉa aḷavu vaittukkoṇṭu eñciya pakutiyai kaltēyāviṟku viṟṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl āppirikkāvaip poṉ viḷaiyum nāṭākak karutuvatu tavaṟu, eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm. 1927ām āṇṭu, aṟiñar liyōṉāṭu ulli (Leonard Willey), ūr nakaril naṭattiya akaḻ āyvil cupāttu araci (Queen Subath) putaikkappaṭṭa kallaṟaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittu aṅkup pala poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum poṉ talaiyaṇikaḷ, poṉ nāṭā, poṉ mōtiram poṟṟakaṭṭāl pōrttappeṟṟa yāḻ (Harp) pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum kaṇṭupiṭittār. cālṭiyāvai aṭutta mecapottāmiyāvil muṟkālattil poṉ atikam kāṇap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl anta nāṭu intiyāviṟkup peritum kaṭamaip paṭṭa nāṭākum eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.8
poṉṉai atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttiya paḻam perunāṭu ekiptu. aṅku iṟantapiṉ maṉṉar uṭalukkup poṉ aṅki cāttappaṭṭatu. pārō maṉṉarkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aracu kaṭṭilaip peṟṟiruntaṉar. annāṭu intiyāviṉiṉṟum, mecapottāmiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum poṉṉaip peṟṟatu. ekiptil maṉitap paṇpu paravuvataṟku intiyā āṟṟiya perum paṅkē kāraṇamākum. poṉṉaip pīṉiciyā, kirī mutaliya piṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷ palvēṟu ceyalkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉa. cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷukkup poṉ tēvaip paṭṭatu. eṉavē, cintuveḷi teṉ intiyāvōṭu uṟavu koṇṭatu. teṉ intiyā, taṉ iṉa uṟavuḷḷa cintuveḷi nāṭukaḷukku ērāḷamāṉa aḷavu poṉṉai ēṟṟumati ceytatu. teṉ intiyā viltāṉ mutal mutalākap poṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. teṉ intiyāvil ērāḷamāṉa irumpuk karuvikaḷ iruntatāl viraivil periya aḷavil poṉ akaḻntu kuvikkap peṟṟatu.9 ataṉāl, eṉ naṇpar oruvar, āṅkila āṭciyiṉpōtu,
poṉviḷainta pūmieṉṉum intiyāvē - iṅkup
pucikkauṇa vaṟṟateṉṉa intiyāvē
eṉṟu mukārip paṇṇil oru pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
iṉṟu ulakiluḷḷa iculāmiyarkaḷum yūtarkaḷum kiṟittavar kaḷum puṉitamāṉa malaikkōyilāṉa erucalēm tirukkōyilaip paṟṟi naṉku aṟivar. atu cālamōṉ aracar avarkaḷāl iṟṟaikku 2,978 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kaṭṭiyeḻuppap peṟṟatu. appuṉita māṉa tirukkōyilai naṭuvākakkoṇṭu (kiplāvākak koṇṭu) oru kālattil ulakameṅkumuḷḷa iṟaippaṟṟāḷarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu muculim aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.10 akkōyilaik kaṭṭiya cālamōṉ aracar (cālamōṉñāṉi) intiyāviṟkup pīṉiciyak kappalkaḷai aṉuppi, cantaṉakkaṭṭai, yāṉaikkompu, mayiliṟaku, kuraṅku, poṉ pōṉṟavaṟṟai vāṅkivaracceytu antat tirukkōyilaic ciṟappuṟac ceytār. pīṉiciyak kappal intiyāvil uḷḷa uvarit tuṟaimukattiṟku vantu apporuḷkaḷai vāṅkic ceṉṟuḷḷatu. antat tuṟaimukattiṉ peyar opir (Ophir) eṉṟu paipiḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cilar opir āppirikkāvil uḷḷatu eṉṟum, cilar cōmālilāntil uḷḷatu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. opir eṉṟu paipiḷ kuṟippiṭum iṭam uvaritāṉ; inta uvarip paṭṭiṉam nellai māvaṭṭattaic cērnta nāṅkunēri vaṭṭārat tiṉ kaṭaṟkaraiyil uḷḷa ūrēyākum. itaṉai nallāyar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. intiyāvil mutalāvatu kiṟittavak kaṇkāṇiyar (Bishop) āka vanta nallāyar vi.ecu. acariyā avarkaḷum ātarittuḷḷār. avarkaḷuṭaiya uṭaṉ piṟantārāṉa vi.ecu. jhkR v«.V., avarkaḷ ceṉṉaik kiṟittavak kallūri itaḻ oṉṟil ēṟattāḻa 60 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ opir eṉpatu uvarippaṭṭiṉantāṉ eṉṟu uṟuticeytu oru muttirai aṭittuḷḷār. āppirikkāvil mayiliṟakum, cantaṉamum illai eṉpatai aṟiyāta cilar opir āppirikkāvil uḷḷatu eṉṟu colliviṭṭaṉar eṉa ariya āppuk kaṭāviyuḷḷār. ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭilkūṭa, poṉ kiṭaittiruppataik koṇṭu poṉ mikutiyākat teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷatu eṉpatai aṟiyalām.
irumpum poṉṉum
irumpu ūḻiyil irunta makkaḷāl, teṉintiyāvil mikappaḻaṅkālattil irumpiṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākap poṉ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉiyāvil - ciṟappāka ūr nakaril poṉ mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatāka kaltēyāvil (Chaldean) kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukkaḷ veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. poṉṉaip, paḻaṅkālak kaltēya makkaḷ vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭuvanta poruḷākak koṇṭaṉar eṉpataṟku atu cāṉṟu tarukiṟatu. mikattolaivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷiliruntu ūrukku (UR¡F)¢ contamāṉa paḻaṅkālak kappalkaḷ poṉṉaik koṇṭuvantaṉa eṉpatāka atil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intat tolaivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ intiyāvum, āppirikkāvumēyākum.
mecapottāmiyāvil intap paimpoṉ ulōkam, mikap paḻaṅkālattil paravalāka mikunta aḷavil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirunta taṟku annāṭu intiyāviṟku mikavum kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiyap pacum poṉṉaip paḻaṅkālattil peritum payaṉpaṭuttiya maṟṟoru nāṭu paḻam perum ekiptu ākum. poṉṉai nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoru pakutikkum oru nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoru nāṭṭukkum koṇṭu celvatu eḷitāka iruntatu. ekiptil intiyap poṉ pāyntu ceṉṟatu paṟṟi yārum viyap paṭaivataṟkillai; anta nāṭṭil puṉitamāṉa maṉita nākarikam paravuvataṟku intiyarkaḷē peritum kāraṇamāka iruntaṉar. piṟkālattil pīṉiciyarkaḷum, kirēkkarkaḷum ēṉaiya airōppiya nāṭṭiṉarkaḷum intat tātup poruḷaip palvēṟu kāriyaṅ kaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. cintu veḷiyil kūṭap poṉṉiṟku mikutiyākat tēvai iruntatu eṉṟu nām kūṟiṉāl atu perum tavaṟu eṉṟu evarum eṇṇār. cintuveḷikkum teṉ intiyāviṟkum iṭaiyē aṭikkaṭi naṭanta vaṇikap pōkku varattut toṭarpil teṉ intiyā antap pakutikkup peritum poṉṉai eṟṟumati ceytu irukka vēṇṭum.
poṉṉaip paṟṟiya āyvil pukunta āciriyarkaḷ palarum teṉ intiyāvil poṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa tātupporuḷ eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ. intiyā oru kālattil poṉṉiṉ nilam eṉṟu pativu ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. teṉ intiyāvil oru cila māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ maṭṭuntāṉ poṉṉait tāṅkum taṉittaṉmai vāynta taḷamāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu uṇmaiyākum. intiyāvil cila pakutikaḷil maṭṭum kiṭaittatālum atu kiṭaippataṟku aritāka irunta tālum poṉ vilaiyuyarnta tātupporuḷāka matikkappaṭṭatu.
itai naṭuvunilaiyil niṉṟu ārāyntāl, paḻaṅkālatti liruntu intiyat tīvakattil poṉṉai nīrmait tōyppu (Washing Gold) ceyyum vaḻakkam iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākum. āṟṟu maṇaliṉum, poṉ uṭkoṇṭa pāṟai aṭukkukaḷilum (Gravels), vaṇṭal cārnta paṭukaiyamaivilum poṉṉai alaci eṭukkum muṟai iruntu vantatu. intat tātup poruḷai nīril alacum vaḻakkam, muṟkālattil cila varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa pakutikaḷil poṉ cēkarikkappaṭṭataik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. āṉāl, itaṟku appāl teṉ intiyat tīvakattil ērāḷamākak koṭu varippāṟai (Schist) kaṉma īruyirakaiyuṭaṉ poṉṉum kalanta kaṉimap poruḷum uḷḷa pāṟai eṉṉum iraṇṭum ērāḷamākak kalanta poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉip paṇpuṭaiyatāy amaintiruppatai nām naṉkaṟivōm eṉṟālum, paḻaṅkal ūḻiyiliruntu iṉṟu varai intap poṉ eṉṉum tātupporuḷiṉ payaṉai naṉku aṟiyap paṭṭiruntālum paṇṭaik kāla makkaḷ vāḻnta malaikaḷilum kuṉṟukaḷilum paṟampukaḷilum uḷḷa poṉṉiṉ ulōkak karu (Gold Ores)it¡ kaṇṭupiṭippataṟkup pōtiya muṉṉēṟṟam maṉitaṉ iṉṟuvarai peṟavillai.
makkaḷ piṟkālattil ataṉuṭaiya taṉicciṟappil uyirt tuṭippuṭaṉ iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpataṟku ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. poṉṉaip peṟuvataṟkum, poṉṉiṉ karuvaik kāṇavum, ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil poṉṉaic ceyyavum maṉitaṉ taṉ kālattaiyum āṟṟalaiyum, poruḷaiyum atikamākac celaviṭṭuḷḷāṉ. nīla malaiyil namvōluk koṭi amcāvil (Namvolukodi Amsham) uḷḷa tēvālam eṉṉum iṭattil ērāḷamāṉa mikap paḻamaiyāṉa poṟcuraṅka vāyiṟ kuḻikaḷ (Mining Shafts) ākiyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭuvataṟkuc cāṉṟākap pala uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟukku appāl periya kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷ 70-80 aṭi uyarattil nīṇṭu vaḷarntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tēvāla (Devala) eṉṉum iṭattaic cuṟṟip palvēṟu vakaiyil poṉṉaik kaṇṭupiṭikkum paṇikaḷ naṭai peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṉum cāṉṟai mēṟpōnta vāṟākap pārkkak kūṭātu. oru kālattil tamiḻ makkaḷ iṅkup poṉṉaik kaṇṭu piṭippatil perum paṇiyāṟṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa. ippoḻutum kūṭa poṉṉai eṭukkum paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷ viyakkattakka muṟaikaḷil takaval tēṭupavarkaḷāy iruntu varukiṟārkaḷ. eṉṟālum iṉṟaiya makkaḷ vaṇṭal cārnta nīrmait tōyvil tam naṭavaṭikkaikaḷil kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭuk kiṭantu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. paḻaiya poṟ curaṅkaṅkaḷ paḻamaic ciṉṉaṅkaḷākap pōṟṟappeṟum nilaiyil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cāṉṟuṟuti aḷikkum mēmpaṭṭa nilai eyti yuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa kālak kaṇippu muṟai cārnta muṟaiyil varaiyaṟuttu nilaināṭṭuvataṟku muṭiyātirukkiṟatu.
muṟkālattil curaṅkam uḷḷa ciṟṟūrkaḷ poṉṉai akaḻn teṭuppatil atika ārvam kāṭṭi vantaṉa. inta ūrkaḷ paṇṭu taṉic ciṟappaip peṟṟiruntaṉa. atāvatu ūrkaḷaic cuṟṟi araṇkaḷ eḻuppap peṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpatākum. inta vilaiyuyarnta tātup poruḷai akaḻntu eṭukka evvāṟu paṇṭaik kāla maṉitaṉ poṟuppōṭu paṇiyāṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyai nām paḻaiya aḻipāṭukaḷiliruntu tokukka muṭiyum. āṉāl, irumpu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭu, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu, potu makkaḷiṭaiyē paravat toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarē poṟ curaṅkaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu poṉṉai eṭukkum paṇi perum aḷavil naṭantirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟuvatu nalla poruttamākum. māperum maraṅkaḷ tōṉṟi vāṉuṟa vaḷarntu niṟkum kāṭukaḷil uḷḷa paḻaiya curaṅkaṅkaḷai nām āyvu ceytu kāṇumpōtu, paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ curaṅkaṅkaḷait tōṇṭi, poṉṉai eṭuppatil evvaḷavu viruppam uṭaiyavar kaḷāy iruntaṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām. ac curaṅkaṅkaḷaip pātu kāttu vanta avarkaḷ aṟiyalām. ac curaṅkaṅkaḷaip pātukāttu vanta avarkaḷ ceyal poruḷātāranilai mēṉmai yaṭaivataṟku utaviyāka iruntatu.10
paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ teṉ intiyā poṉṉiṉ tāyakamākap pōṟṟuvataṟku maṟṟoru eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ēṉ nām pativu ceytuḷḷōm eṉpatai uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ mikap piṟkālattavai ākum. nam ilakkiyaṅkaḷil koḷkaik kōṭpāṭukaḷaic ceyaluruppaṭuttukiṟatu pōla ilakkiyap paṇpiṉ marapiṉ matippu ēṟṟuk koḷḷāmal iruppatu mika varuttamāka irukkiṟatu. ilakkiyap paṇpuṭaiya cāṉṟukaḷ teṉ intiyāviṉ tēcīya māḻaiyākap poṉ payaṉpaṭuvatu eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉac cuṭṭik kāṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu. piṟkālattil cempum veḷḷiyum piṟa māḻaikaḷum potu makkaḷiṭaiyē tārāḷamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirunta nilaiyiṉ pōtum māḻait toḻil perum aḷavil vaḷarcciyuṟṟu poṉṉiṉ piṟkūṟu oru māḻai maṟṟoṉṟiliruntu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṇpa taṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tuppuravāṉa poṉ puṭattaṅkam allatu cokkat taṅkam eṉṟum, cempu kalanta poṉ nakait taṅkam allatu cavaraṉ taṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. poṉ eṉpatu oru kālattil potuvāka māḻai eṉṉum poruḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum puṉitamāṉa poṉ ellāt tātukkaḷukkum mēlāka matippilum, oḷiyilum, vaṇṇat tilum, taṉmaiyilum uyarntatāy ippoḻutu viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
poṉ ceyyum vittai
tamiḻakattil, poṉ ceyyum ōr ariya toḻilaip palar aṟintiruntaṉar. cempiliruntum turucukaḷ akaṟṟap paṭṭāl atu poṉṉākum eṉṟu cilar kūṟivantaṉar. pātaracattaip payaṉpaṭuttic cilar poṉ ceytu vantaṉar. cempu allatu pātaracattaip poṉṉāka māṟṟum vittai iracavātat toḻil eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟatu. kaṉma īruyirakaiyuṭaṉ cila camayam poṉ kalanta kaṉimap poruḷ cārnta paṭikkak kallil (Quartz) iruntu terinteṭutta tuṇṭukaḷai naṉṟākak kaḻuvip periya kuḻi ammiyil iṭṭu araittup poṭiyākki, antap poṭiyai mīṇṭum kaḻuvi, oru caṭṭiyil pōṭṭu vaṟuttu atiluḷḷa kantakac cattu akaṟṟap paṭṭatu. pātaracattiṉiṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟupoṭikaḷ poṉṉāka māṟum. oruṅkiṇainta pātaracamum poṉṉum cūṭākkappaṭṭa oru irumpuc caṭṭiyil pōṭappaṭukiṟatu. muṉ kūṟiyatu āviyāka māṟip pōkiṟatu. piṉṉuḷḷatu ataṉ puṉitanilaiyil eñci niṟkiṟatu.
aṟiñar kirik (Grig) utakamaṇṭalattaip paṟṟip pēcum poḻutu kuṟippiṭukiṟār; piṣap ṭavuṇ (Bishop Down), perm  hil (Ferm hill) eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkup piṉṉuḷḷa paḷḷattākkil poṉ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum ataṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum intap pakutiyilum nañcaṉkūṭu paḷḷattākkilum ataṉ aṇmaiyilum poṉ tukaḷkaḷ maṇṇōṭu kalantiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭut taṇṇīril alacip poṉ tukaḷkaḷ vēṟākavum maṇ vēṟākavum pirittu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉ taṭaṅkaḷ āṟṟaṅkaraikaḷiṉ neṭukilum uḷḷa maṇmēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḷḷattākkukaḷiṉ arukil uḷḷa nīr ōṭaikaḷil kaṉma īruyirakaiyuṭaṉ cilacamayam poṉṉum kalantuḷḷa kaṉimapporuḷ (Quartz) kuviyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai poṉ ceyyum paṇi paṇṭaik kālattil kuṉṟukaḷil maṭṭum naṭaipeṟavillai. vēy nāṭṭilum mēṟkattiya kaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷilum naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu eṉpataic cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
poṉṉum aṇikalaṉkaḷum
mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattiṉiṉṟu teṉ intiya makkaḷ ciṟappākat tamiḻarkaḷ ittātupporuḷai aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceytu pūṇṭukoṇṭatu periya aḷavil itaṉōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpu eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya putaikuḻikaḷil kūṭa, pala taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷōṭu poṉṉum miṉṉik koṇṭiruntatu terikiṟatu. niṉaiviṟ keṭṭāta kālattiṉiṉṟu palavakaiyāṉa uruvil poṟkollarkaḷ poṉṉai urukkit taṭṭi, ataik kottiyum, aṟuttum eṇṇaṟṟa eḻil vaṭivil aṇikalaṉkaḷait tam kaivaṇṇamum uḷappāṅkum oḷira uruvākki vantaṉar. ippoḻutum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ kaṇṭa tātupporuḷkaḷil poṉ mutaṉmutalākak kaṇṭa poruḷāy miḷirkiṉṟatu eṉpatu uṟutippaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻmakkaḷ kaṇṭa kaṭal vaṇikattiṉ payaṉāyk kāla vaḷarcciyil poṉ ulakameṅkum parantu nilaviyuḷḷatu. iṉṟu poṉ kiṭaippataṟkariyatāy nāḷukkunāḷ vilai ēṟikkoṇ ṭirukkum nilaimaikkut tamiḻnāṭṭumakaḷir, poṉmītu koṇṭa viruppam kuṉṟātu vaḷarntu koṇṭiruppatum oru kāraṇamāka irukkalām.
poṉ aṇiyum iṉattavarkaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭup poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceytuvantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷaip pārttu intō āriyarkaḷ poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pūṇat talaippaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ kātiṉ kīḻppakutiyil toḷaiyiṭṭup pala poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. pōrṉiyōvil uḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷaippōl kātuvaṭittu atil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa vaḷaiyaṅkaḷai aṇintu varuvatai aṟintēṉ. āṉāl ēṟattāḻa 4,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē yūtap peṇkaḷ kātil toḷai yiṭṭup poṉ aṇi kalaṉkaḷaip pūṇṭu vantatuṭaṉ kaḻuttilum kaikaḷilum kālkaḷilum poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. yūtarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇinta tōṭu, malaitteyva vaḻipāṭṭaiyum mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar. tirukkōyilil civaṉ kāḷaimītu amarntirukkum muṟaiyil karuvaṟaiyil tiruuruvam amaittu vaḻipaṭṭum vantaṉar. teyvat tiṟku maṉaivi makkaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu nampiṉar. teyvattiṟkup pāl paḻam appam paṭaittu naṟumpukai kāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kōyil, tamiḻar kōyil amaippaip pōlak koṭimaram, palipīṭam, karuvaṟai mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭiruntatu.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
IV
1.	They are locally known as Pattams and the system of putting gold in some shape or other over the dead body is prevalent in orthodox Hindu Homes even nowadays. Dr. Sir. John Marshall makes the following note on these objects (Annual Report of the Archaeological Survey of India 1902-03 foot note on page 105). The gold frontlets are of thin gold left so flimsy that they could not possibly have been used as jewellery in real life. They are mere imitations of the genuine articles substituted by the relation of the dead who no doubt kept the more substantial jewellery for themselves. For similar instances of this economical piety towards the dead. See Frazer Pausanias Vol.III. p. 107. Schliemanu Mycenal p. 156, etc., : E. Gardner new chaplers in Greek History E.P. Tyler Primitive Culture Vol. I. p. 439.
2.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbair by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) Madras, 1915, (Introdution) pp. III-IV.
3.	Mohenjo Daro and Indus valley Civilization - Dr. Sir. John Marshall Vol. I (London) 1930.
	Excavation at Harappa - M.S. Watts Vol.I. p. 14.
4.	Dravidian Elements in Indian Civilizaiton - Dr. Gilbert Slater. M.A., D.Sc., 1920 (Madras).
5.	Pre-Historic South India by Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951.
	History and Monuments of Ur. of - C.J. Gad (London) 1929.
6.	tamiḻar mutaṉmutal payaṉpaṭuttiya tātu poṉṉāyiruntati ṉālēyē poṉ eṉṟa colliṟku ulōkam eṉṉum poru ḷum, piṟkālattil kiṭaitta veḷḷi, cempu, irumpiṟku muṟaiyē veṇpoṉ, cempoṉ, karumpoṉ eṉṟa peyar kaḷum tōṉṟiṉa - paḻantamiḻāṭci - ñā. tēvanēyaṉ. v«.V., (ceṉṉai) mutaṟpatippu. 1952. pak. 93.
7.	“Tamils traded with the Chaldeans and teak found in the ruins of Ur, the sea port and capital of Babylonia “grows”, says Ragozin in Vedic India p. 806, in Southern India close to the Malabar coast and no where-else”. They took Indigo and muslin to Egypt. King Solomon had Sandal wood apes and peacocks (thokai) from Ophir, “The navy of Tarshish brought gold, silver, ivory, apes and peacocks from India. Nineveh had gold from Tamil Country and Assyria tin. The Greeks took rice and pepper. Pearls were exported from Korkai” - Tamil India - M.S.Purnalingam Pillai, B.A., L.I. (Madras) 1927. p.76.
	(a)	“If there was intercourse between the Indusvalley and Sumerla there must have been greater intercourse between these two districts and South India. Evidence of this is found in two facts mentioned by sayce. One is that Indian teak was found in the ruins of Ur (Mugheir) which was the capital of the sumerian kings in the iv millennium B. C. and the other is that the word Sindhu or muslin is mentioned in an ancient Babylonlan list of clothing.” -Sayce Hibbert Lectures pp. 136-138.
	(b)	“Not only was there Commercial intercourse between the Tamil Country and the Mesopotamian valley, but there is some evidence that the trade of South India extended to Egypt in the iii millennium B.C. Says W.H. Schoff,” thousands of years before the emergence of the Greeks from savagery ... Egypt and nations of Ancient India came into being and a Commercial System was developed for the interchange of products within those limits having its centre of exchange near the head of the Persian gulf. The people of that region the various Arab tribes and more especially those ancestors of the Phoenicians, the mysterious Red Men were the active carriers or intermediaries. The growth of civilization in India created an active merchant marine trading to the Euphrates and Africa and eastwards we know not whither.” Periplus. p.3.
8.	Pre-Historic South India - Prof. V.R.Ramachandra Diksitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 114.
	The Sumerians - C. Leonard Willy (Oxford) 4th impression May 1930.
9.	We need not enter into the discussion whether gold was a material of the sanskirt speaking people known as the Aryans or whether it was bequeathed by the old inhabitants of South India. Examining the various points of view both in favour and against, we are almost driven to the conclusion that gold was first discovered in south India though India as a whole had once been regarded as a land of gold. It is true even in the South there are only a few districts which can exclusively be said to be gold - bearing districts. It became a precious metal simply be-cause gold was scarce and available only in a few parts of the land.”
	Pre-Historic south India - Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 114.
10.	Ibid. pp. 115-117.

5. irumpup poruḷkaḷ
(IRON OBJECTS)
putiya kal ūḻiyil ulōkap poruḷkaḷ
ātittanallūr paṟampiluḷḷa iṭukāṭṭil aṟiñar a.irīyā avarkaḷāl akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakka tātup poruḷkaḷ mūṉṟu. avai, muṟaiyē (1) poṉ (2) irumpu (3) veṇkalam eṉappaṭum. im mūṉṟil poṉ, tamiḻakattil mutaṉ mutalākak kiṭaitta tātup poruḷāṉatāl, iṅku mutalil vaittu āyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. poṉ nilattil periya aḷavil kiṭaikkappaṭṭatālum, makkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṭalai aḻaku ceyyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaic ceyvataṟkēṟṟa taṉmaiyum meṉmaiyum vaṇṇamum vāynta poruḷāka iruntatālum atu ulakil mutalāvatāka vaittu eṇṇappaṭum poruḷāy uyarntatu. makkaḷ, poruḷ parimāṟṟuk karuviyāka, poṉṉaik kaṭṭi nāṇaya mākavum, kācu nāṇayamākavum payaṉ paṭuttiyatāl poṉṉiṟku matippu eḻuntatu. āṉāl irumpaip pōl, makkaḷ vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷākap poṉṉō, piṟa tātup poruḷ kaḷō iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkillai. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpaik kaṇṭa makkaḷē maṉita nākarikattiṟku vittiṭṭa makkaḷāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpai naṉku payaṉpaṭuttat terinta makkaḷē ulakil ariya periya karuvi kaḷaip paṭaittu ulakai āḷum āṟṟal peṟṟavarkaḷāyum, ciṟanta poṟikaḷaiyum nīrāvik kappalkaḷaiyum uruvākki vaṇikat tuṟai yil vaḷarntum, poruḷātārat tuṟaiyil uyarntum vāḻn tuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉaip piṉṉar virivāy ārāyvōm.
innilaiyil 100 āṇṭiṟku muṉ ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil irumpup poruḷkaḷ ērāḷamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟaiya aṇu ūḻiyil kūṭa irumpiṉ ēṟṟam eḷ aḷavil kūṭak kuṉṟavillai. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl iṉṟu irumpiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta nilai paṉmaṭaṅku uyarntuḷḷatu. irumpē iṉṟaiya intiyāviṉ perumaikkum pēṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyāy amaintuḷḷatu.
akaḻāyvil kiṭaitta irumpup poruḷkaḷ
ātittanallūrp paṟampil aṟiñar alekcāntar irīyā avarkaḷ āṇṭutōṟum aṭuttaṭuttuc ceyta akaḻāyvil pala irumpuk karuvikaḷum cila irumput taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivaratu akaḻāyvil kiṭaittirāta oru cila irumpup poruḷkaḷ perliṉ muṉaivar cākōr avarkaḷiṭa mum pāricu aṟiñar em. uluyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷiṭamum kiṭaittirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iruvaruṭaiya akaḻāyvukaḷilum ettaṉai poruḷkaḷ, eṉṉeṉṉa poruḷkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu yārē kūṟa vallār. nkY« mt®fŸ f©blL¤j bghUŸfŸ x›bth‹¿‰F«, ïÇah f©blL¤j bghUŸfËš Ãfbuh¤j gofŸ.(Duplicates) uḷa eṉṟu evvāṟu tuṇintu kūṟa muṭiyum?
ātittanallūril akaḻāyvu naṭattiya mūvarkaḷiṉ cēkarippi lum, tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya ellā vakaiyāṉa paṭaikkalaṉ kaḷum (Armours) toḻilāḷarkaḷukku iṉṟi yamaiyāta karuvikaḷum, (Tools), vīṭṭuppayaṉpāṭṭukku uriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum (Implements) kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. mēlum tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyil uḷḷa īmak kāṭṭil ellā vakaiyāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷum, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷum, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷum irukkum eṉṟu evarum etirpārkka muṭiyātu. avaṟṟaip paṇṭaikkāla aracaṉiṉ araṇmaṉaiyilum, paṭaikkalak kiṭaṅku kaḷilum kūṭa irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkillai.
aṟiñar irīyā akaḻntu kaṇṭa irumpup poruḷkaḷ mottam 391 eṉṟu avar namakkut tantuḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa ātittanallūr, perumpāyūr ākiyavaṟṟi ṉiṉṟu kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūlil kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, eṉṉuṭaiya matippil tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paḻaṅkāla irumpup paṭaik kalaṉkaḷum, toḻiṟ karuvikaḷum, vīṭṭup payaṉpāṭṭiṟkuriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷumāka ēṟattāḻa 1000 vakaikaḷ irukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu. āṉāl, irīyā kaṇṭeṭutta irumpup poruḷkaḷ 66 eṉṟu terikiṟatu. 1ituvē nām viyakkattakka ceytiyākum. ulakil perumpālāṉa makkaḷ āṭai kaṭṭat teriyātu vilaṅkukaḷ pōl vāḻnta putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ ittuṇai irumpup poruḷkaḷai uruvākkip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟa ceyti, tamiḻulakam iṉṟum eṉṟum perumai aṭaivataṟkuriya ceytiyākum.
irumpuk karuvikaḷ vakaikaḷ
ātitta nallūr paṟampiṉ akaḻāyvil kiṭaitta poruḷ kaḷil cilavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ :
1.	eṟivēl (Spear).
2.	īṭṭi (Lance).
3.	kuttuvāḷ allatu paṭṭākkatti (Dagger).
4.	ampumuṉai pōṉṟa vēl (Barbed javeline).
5.	iru puṟamum kūrmaiyāṉa vāḷ (Double edged Sword).
6.	mūvilai vēl (tiricūlam) (Trident).
7.	kūriya amputtalai (Barbed arrow head).
8.	kaikkōṭari (Hatchets).
9.	vāḷ (Sword).
10.	vaḷainta katti (Curved knife).
11.	iru vaḷaivāṉa kokkittaṭi (Hooks with two curved rods).
12.	pali vāḷ (Sacrificed sword).
13.	amputtalai (Arrow head).
14.	vēlāyutam (Javeline).
15.	kōṭarikaḷ (Axes).
16.	cūlāyutam (Sulams).
17.	kēṭayam (Shield).
18.	ariya āyutam (A Curious Weapon).
19.	ciṟiya uṭaivāḷ (Small Dagger).
20.	ciṟiya īṭṭi (Small Lance).
21.	ceṅkōṇavāyuḷḷa īṭṭi (Lance with blade rectangular in section).
22.	kūmpuvāy īṭṭi (Lance with narrow blade).
23.	kūriya vāyuḷḷa īṭṭi (Lance with lapering blade and hollow butt).
24.	īṭṭip piṭiyuḷḷa kuḻivāṉa kuḻāy (Hallow tube handle of lance).
25.	kuḻivāṉa viḷimpuḷḷa kuttuvāḷ (Dagger with tapering point).
26.	akaṉṟa vāyp paracu (Hatch broadest at the cutting edge).
27.	nīṇṭa vaṭṭak kuḻivāṉa īṭṭi (lance with long round hollow shaft).
28.	kuḻivāṉa irumpuk kaippiṭi īṭṭi (Hollow iron handles with rods through the centre).
29.	vaḷaivukaḷuḷḷa vāḷ (Swords with various curved blades tapering with points).
30.	ciṟiya nuṉiyuḷḷa vāyuṭaiya īṭṭi (Lance with lapering blades and hollow handles).
31.	katti (Knife).
32.	vaḷainta palavakaik kattikaḷ (Reaping hook or curved knives of various forms.
aṟiñar alekcāntar iriyā (Alexander Rea) ātitta nallūrp paṟampil uḷḷa īmakkāṭṭai akaḻntu, palvēṟu paḻam poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttatu oru periya viyappiṟkuriya ceyti aṉṟu. āṉāl, apporuḷkaḷai vakuttut tokuttup peyar kaṇṭupiṭittatu viyappiṉum viyappu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl aḵtu oru poḻutum mikaiyāṉa ceyti eṉṟu evarum kūṟa muṭiyātu. atilum āṅkila nāṭṭaic cārnta oruvar, teṉ intiyāviṟku vantu orucila āṇṭukaḷ taṅki, mikappaḻamaiyāṉa kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ irumpiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷ kaḷukkum irumpup paṭaikkalaṉkaḷukkum peyarkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar eṉpatu eḷitāṉa kāriyam aṉṟu. iṅkē piṟantu vaḷarntu tamiḻ naṉku payiṉṟa tamiḻppulavarkaḷāṟkūṭa avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷai eḷitil kūṟamuṭiyātu.
aṟiñar irīyā peru muyaṟci ceytu paḻaṅkāla irumpup poruḷkaḷukkup peyarkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. āṉāl, avar ellā irumpup poruḷkaḷukkum uriya peyarkaḷaik kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷār eṉṟu colliviṭamuṭiyātu. eṉṟālum peritum muyaṉṟu palavaṟṟiṟkup peyar kāṇa muyaṟci ceytirukkiṉṟār. cilavaṟṟiṟkup peyar kāṇamuṭiyavillai. cila irumpu āyutaṅ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷai irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷ kaṇṭatum illai; kēḷvippaṭṭatum illai eṉṟālum iriyā avarkaḷ anta irumpup poruḷkaḷait tirumpat tirumpat turuvi āyntu apporuḷkaḷai nām uṇaru māṟu āṅkilattil naṉku viḷakkam tantuḷḷār. eṉṟālum iṉṟum nammāl antap poruḷkaḷukku uriya, cariyāṉa peyarkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyavillai. mēlum muṉaivar cākōrum, pirāṉcu nāṭṭaṟiñar ulūyicu ilāppikyū avarkaḷum kaṇ ṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷil nām peyar kūṟa muṭiyāta poruḷkaḷ evvaḷavu irukkum eṉṟu nammāl cintikkakkūṭa muṭiyavillai.
iṉavēṟupāṭu
ulakilēyuḷḷa palvēṟu vaṇṇamum, palvēṟu mukattōṟṟa mum palvēṟu paṇpāṭum palvēṟu moḻi amaippum peṟṟup palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum tīvukaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷ oru tāy vayiṟṟil piṟantu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟip palvēṟu vaṇṇamum palvēṟu moḻi amaippum palvēṟu paṇpāṭum peṟṟārkaḷā? kiṟittava camayak kaṟpaṉaippaṭi orutāy vayiṟṟil kāyīṉ, āpol, cēt eṉṉum mūṉṟu āṇmakkaḷ tōṉṟi ulakil maṉaiviyai eṅkiruntō peṟṟu makkaḷaip peṟṟup palkip perukiṉārkaḷā, allatu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum orē kālattilō allatu muṉpiṉṉākavō cila āṇ peṇkaḷ tōṉṟik kalantu āṇ peṇ makkaḷaip peṟṟu nāḷaṭaivil ulakameṅkum paraviṉārkaḷā eṉṟu nammāl kūṟa muṭiyavillai. maṉita iṉattiṉ orumaip pāṭṭiṟku ulakamakkaḷ aṉaivarum orutāy vayiṟṟil piṟanta piḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟu aṟavōrkaḷum aṟivōrkaḷum kūṟivaruvataiyum nām aṟivōm. āṉāl amerikkāvil vāḻum annāṭṭuk kuṭimakkaḷāṉa cevvintiyar kaḷum, aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiya āṅkilēya veḷḷaiyarkaḷum, āppirikka nāṭṭil tōṉṟiya nīkrōvarkaḷum, aṅkuk kuṭiyēṟi an nāṭṭiṉarai veṉṟu koṉṟu aṭimaippaṭuttic curaṇṭi vāḻum airōppiya pirañcu, piriṭṭicu nāṭukaḷiṉ veḷḷaiyar kaḷum, mañcaḷ niṟa maṅkōliyarkaḷāṉa cīṉarkaḷum cappāṉiyar kaḷum, veḷḷai niṟa uruciyarkaḷum, intiyāvil tōṉṟi aṅkuvāḻum tirāviṭarkaḷum iṭaikkālattil annāṭṭil pukuntu avarkaḷai veṉṟu koṉṟu aṭimaippaṭuttiya āriyar kaḷum, cermaṉ nāṭṭu āriyarkaḷum, cupeyṉ (Spain) nāṭṭu mūr kaḷum cōrṭāṉ nāṭṭu arapikaḷum icarēyal nāṭṭu yūtarkaḷum orē tāy tantaiyarkaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟanta makkaḷā eṉṟa aiyap pāṭukaḷukku aṟiviyal vallunarkaḷukkuḷ iṉṟu tiṭṭa vaṭṭa māṉa muṭivu kāṇa muṭiyavillai.
āṉāl, ulakil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum, palvēṟu tīvukaḷilum makkaḷiṉam taṭpa veppanilai kaḷukkēṟpap palvēṟu vaṇṇamum uṭal amaippum tōṟṟamum peṟṟuk kaṟkāla makkaḷāyum ulōka kāla makkaḷāyum varalāṟṟuk kāla makkaḷāyum vāḻntuvantuḷḷaṉar. cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē makkaḷ nāṭuviṭṭu nāṭu pukuntum, kaṇṭam viṭṭuk kaṇṭam pukuntum āṅkāṅkuḷḷa makkaḷōṭu kalantum irukkalām eṉṟam karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ulēkilē palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum vāḻnta makkaḷ orē kālattilē orē vakaiyāṉa nākarikattaip peṟṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyavillai. ulakilē palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum muṉ piṉṉākak kallūḻikaḷum ulōka ūḻikaḷum varalāṟṟuk kālamum arumpi yuḷḷaṉa. kal ūḻiyil, tol paḻaṅkal ūḻiyum, paḻaṅkallūḻiyum, putuk kallūḻiyum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. avaipōla maṉita nākarikat tiṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa ulōka ūḻiyilē cempum maṇṇum kalanta, kalappuc cempu ūḻi (Chalcolithic age) veṇkala ūḻi, irumpu ūḻi eṉṟu palavakai ulōka ūḻikaḷ malarntuḷḷaṉa. inta ulōka ūḻiyum kal ūḻiyum ovvoru nāṭṭilum orē kālattil tōṉṟavillai; caṟṟu muṉ piṉṉākavum tōṉṟa villai. orē kālattil oru nāṭṭil paḻam kal ūḻiyum, maṟṟoru nāṭṭil putukkallūḻiyum, iṉṉoru nāṭṭil ulōka ūḻiyum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. orē nāṭṭil vaṭa pakutiyil cempu ūḻiyum naṭup pakutiyil putuk kal ūḻiyum teṉ pakutiyil irumpu ūḻiyum eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.
ulakilē ulōka ūḻiyilē makkaḷ nākarikattiṟku aṭikōlat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. cempu ūḻiyaiviṭa, veṇkala ūḻiyaiviṭa irumpu ūḻiyiltāṉ makkaḷ naṉi ciṟanta nākarikattai aṭaintuḷḷaṉar. cempu ūḻiyil periya nāṭukaḷaiyum, nakaraṅkaḷaiyum akaṉṟa terukkaḷaiyum, māṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya vīṭukaḷaiyum, neṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷaiyum āṭai kaḷaiyum, aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum, marakkalaṉkaḷaiyum kappal kaṭṭum taḷaṅkaḷaiyum, vilpaṭai, vēl paṭai, kālāḷ paṭai, tērppaṭai mutaliyavaṟṟaip peṟṟup pēraracaiyum niṟuviya makkaḷ irumpu āyutaṅkaḷaip peṟṟa āṭumāṭu mēykkum nāṭōṭi makkaḷiṭam tōlviyuṟṟatu varalāṟu kaṇṭa uṇmaiyākum. ulakil mutaṉ mutalāka irumpu ūḻiyai aṭainta makkaḷ naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟa makkaḷāyum vallamai vāynta makkaḷāyum vāḻntuḷḷaṉar.
irumpiṉ tōṟṟamum ēṟṟamum
pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē teṉṉintiyāvil irumpu ūḻi malarntuḷḷatu - tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ muṉṉōṭi kaḷāṉa tamiḻarkaḷ irumpai naṉku payaṉpaṭutta muṉ vantaṉar. irumpu ōr uyarnta tātut taṉimam. ataṉ kuṟiyīṭu pe (Fe) ataṉ aṇu niṟai 55.84; itu tamiḻar pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākap payaṉ paṭutti varum taṉimaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. itait tamiḻarkaḷ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē naṉku payaṉpaṭuttat terintuḷḷaṉar. ki.mu. āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē irumpu makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil vaḻakkil iruntu vantatu. mikat toṉmai yāṉa kālattilēyē irumpaip piritteṭuttu ataip pala poruḷkaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttuvatil tamiḻarkaḷ kaitērnta varkaḷāy viṭṭaṉar.
kiṭaikkum iṭam
irumpu taṉi nilaiyil mika aritākak kiṭaittatu. iḵtu iyaṟkaiyaṉṉai makkaḷukku aḷitta ariya naṉkoṭaiyākum. itu viṇṇiṉiṉṟu viḻum kaṟkaḷilum erimalaip pāṟaikaḷilum ciṟuciṟu tuṇukkukaḷākak kiṭaikkiṟatu. irumpuk kūṭṭukkaḷ pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṇilum atikamāka nīrnilaikaḷi luḷḷa nīrilum tāvaraṅkaḷilum utirattilum irumpuk kūṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
perumpālum ellāk kaṉimaṅkaḷilum irumpu ciṟitu uḷḷatu. mukkiyamāka irumput tātukkaḷ āksaiṭukaḷum kārpaṉēṭṭukaḷumākum. mākṉaṭaiṭ (Fe3 O4) ke (ha) maṭaiṭṭu (Fe2 O3) iliyōṉaiṭṭu (2 Fe2 O3 3H2, O), ciṭaraiṭṭu, cālipaiṭ pōṉṟavai mukkiyamāṉa irumput tātukkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
irumpu āksaiṭu tātukkaḷaik kariyuṭaṉ kalantu urukki ulōkam piritteṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu piritteṭukkappaṭum irumpil kari ciṟitaḷavu kalantirukkum. kariyiṉ vikitamum atu kalantuḷḷa vakaiyum irumpiṉ paṇpukaḷai niruṇayikkiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṟkēṟpa (1) vārppu irumpu (Cast iron) (2) eḵku (Steel) (3) tēṉirumpu (Wrought Iron) eṉa mūṉṟu vakaiyāṉa irumpukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
irumpu vakaikaḷ
irumpu iṉṟi nākarikam vaḷarntirukka muṭiyātu. pukai vaṇṭi, pēruntu vaṇṭikaḷ, kappalkaḷ, pālaṅkaḷ, pattu irupatu māṭik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavai eḵku iṉṟi amaikka muṭiyātu. cuttamāṉa irumpu kiṭaittaṟkariya poruḷākum. irumpu eppoḻutum ciṟitaḷavu karip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ kalantēyirukkum. intak karitāṉ irumpiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷai māṟṟi viṭukiṟatu. kariyē irumpiṉ valimaikkuk kāraṇamāka irukkiṟatu. kariyiṉ kalappu aḷavē vārppirumpu, tēṉirumpu, eḵku eṉṟu māṟṟi viṭukiṟatu.
vārppirumpil 4 catavikitam kari kalantuḷḷatu. itu kaṭiṉa māṉatāka irukkātu. eḷitil uṭaikkak kūṭiyatu. cūṭēṟṟic cammaṭṭiyāl aṭittu nīṭṭa muṭiyātu. urukki accukkaḷil vārkkalām. itaṉ payaṉ ciṟitēyākum.
tēṉirumpil kari mikak kuṟaivāka uḷḷatu. paṇṭaiya tamiḻakattil tēṉirumpē atikamāka uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu. itu palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷil kāyccit taṭṭa ēṟṟatu. ciṟitu kūṭak kari illāta cuttamāṉa irumpu uṟutiyāṉatāka irukka muṭiyātu. iḵtu eḷitil turuppiṭikkātu. tēṉ irumput tuṇṭukaḷ cūṭēṟṟiṉāl oṉṟoṭoṉṟu iṇaiyum; eṉavē itu nalla payaṉuḷḷatāka irukkiṟatu.
eḵku irumpum kariyum oru kalavaip poruḷ. itil kariyiṉ aḷavu 1-7% varai irukkum. iṉṉum māṅkaṉicu kantakam, pācuvaram, cilikkaṉ ākiyavaiyum itil uḷḷaṉa. itaic civanta cūṭṭil kāyccit taṭṭalām. cātāraṇa cūṭṭil ōraḷavu uruvākkalām. naṉṟākak kaṭiṉamāvatu itaṉ mukkiyamāṉa paṇpu. karipporuḷē itaṉ kaṭiṉat taṉmaikku mukkiya kāraṇamākum. valimai itaṉ maṟṟac ciṟappiyalkaḷiṉ oṉṟākum.2
irumpiṉ varalāṟu
irumpu tamiḻnāṭṭil cumār pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa ōr ariya tātup poruḷākum itu. poṉṉaiviṭa atikappayaṉuḷḷatāyum arumaiyāṉatāyum matikkappeṟṟiruntatu. mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē tamiḻarkaḷ, ekiptu, āppirikkā, cumēriyā, kirīcu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceytu vantaṉar. ekiptiyarkaḷum, kirēkkarkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṭa miruntē irumpai urukkip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟaikaḷai aṟintaṉar. 1837ām āṇṭil irāyal ēciyāṭṭik cocaiṭṭi (Royal Asiatic Society) yil vācikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭurai oṉṟil aṟiñar kīt (Heath) eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāvil ceyyappaṭṭa eḵkup poruḷkaḷē ekiptiṟkum airōppāk kaṇṭattiṟkum ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻnāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil ulaikaḷil veppam kuṟaivāka iruntatāl irumpu toṭakka nilaiyil ciṟiya aḷavil urukkap paṭṭatu. appāl tōliṉālāṉa turuttiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittu, periya aḷavil irumpai urukki palvēṟu kāriyaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭutti vara muṟpaṭṭaṉar. paṇṭaik kālattil kallālum maṇṇālum kaṭṭappeṟṟa ciṟiya ulaikaḷil irumpuk kaṉimaṅ kaḷiṉ poṭiyai marakkariyuṭaṉ cērttu urukkic cumār 70,80 iṟāttal eṭaiyuḷḷa irumpuk kaṭṭikaḷai uṟpatti ceytu vantaṉar. mūṅkil kuḻāyiṉālum, tōliṉālum marap palakaiyiṉā lum ceyyappaṭṭa turuttikaḷāl ūtappaṭṭa kāṟṟiṉāl ulai mukattil kariyai erittu 10, 12 maṇi nērattiṟkup piṟku ulaiyai uṭaittu irumpuk kaṭṭiyai eṭuppar. akkaṭṭi urukiṉa karic cāmpalōṭum piṟa acuttap poruḷkaḷōṭum kalantiruntatu. appāl ataik kari aṭuppil kāycci cammaṭṭiyāl aṭittu irumpup pāḷaṅkaḷākac ceytārkaḷ.
tilliyiṉ (Delhi) aṇmaiyil uḷḷa kuttupmiṉārukku arukil uḷḷa irumput tūṇ intiyāvilē ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. atu ki.pi. 300il ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ittuṇai āṇṭukaḷāka anta irumput tūṇ kāṟṟilum maḻaiyilum veyililum aṭipaṭṭum turuppiṭikkātu iruntu varuvatu kaṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ viyappaṭaikiṉṟārkaḷ. intūrukku 30 kal tolaivil uḷḷa tār eṉṉum irumput tūṇ kuptarkaḷ kālattil uḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. irumpu kaḷaic ciṟu ciṟu tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭi, karittūḷaip pakkaṅkaḷil nirappic ciṟukaḷimaṇ pāttiraṅkaḷil vaittu ataṉ vāyaik kaḷi maṇ cāntiṉāl pūci, aṭaittuk kariyaṭuppukaḷil veṇcūṭākum varaiyil vaittuk kāyccic cila maṇi nēram ceṉṟatum pāttiraṅ kaḷai eṭuttu āṟa vaittu uṟutiyāṉatum, kaṭiṉamāṉatumāṉa eḵkait tayārikkum muṟaiyait tamiḻarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē aṟintiruntaṉar. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāṉa pōrk karuvikaḷum, vīṭṭu upayōkattiṟku uriya arivāḷmaṇai, tēṅkāyt turuvi, veṭṭarivāḷ, tēṅkāyk kīṟi, karaṇṭi, caṭṭi, akappai pōṉṟavaiyum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
intiyāvil ki.pi. 400ām āṇṭil mutaṉmutalāka uṭcu (wootz) eṉṟa peyaril eḵku ceyyappaṭṭatu. ērāḷamāka airōppā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭatu. uṭcu eṉṉum eḵku mūlam ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟa ṭamāciṉ eṉṉum kattikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. eḵkaik kāyccit taṇṇīril avittu eḵkaik kaṭiṉa mākkum muṟaiyaip piṟa nāṭṭiṉar intiyariṭamiruntē aṟintaṉar. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil irumpaiyum eḵkaiyum tayārikkac cila muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭup piṉ kainaḻuva viṭap paṭṭaṉa. piriṭṭicu intiyāvil vaṭaintiyap pakutikaḷil iru irumput toḻiṟcālaikaḷ niṟuvap peṟṟaṉa. cutantira intiyāvil pilāy, rūrkēlā, turkkāpūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ayalnāṭṭār ātaravuṭaṉ periya irumput toḻiṟcālaikaḷ niṟuvappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. cēlattilum oru ciṟu irumput toḻiṟcālai arumpi yuḷḷatu. cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ peṅkaḷūr vicākappaṭṭiṉam parakīp mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil putu urukkālaikaḷ niṟuvat tiṭṭam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Iron Ores)
irumpu, taṉiyāka nilattil veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭum poru ḷaṉṟu. irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavai kemaṭaiṭ (Hematite) (Fe2O), mākṉaṭaiṭ (Magnettite Fe3 O4), nīruṭaiya kārpaṉēṭṭāṉa ciṭaraiṭṭu (Fe CO3 x H2O) ākiyavaiyākum.
ikkaṉimaṅkaḷ, amerikkā, kaṉaṭā, cōviyat uruciyā, intiyā ākiya nāṭukaḷil pēraḷavilum airōppāvilum āttirēyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil mikac ciṟiya aḷavilum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ perum aḷavil uḷḷaṉa. pīkār, oricāp pakutikaḷil 8,000 miliyaṉ ṭaṉkaḷukkuk kuṟaivillāmal kuṟaintatu 60 catavikitam irumpaiyuṭaiya kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kāṣmīrilum tamiḻnāṭu, āntirā, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya mānilaṅkaḷilum irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. intiyā vil 9647 ṭaṉ uyartarak kaṉimaṅkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu aikkiya nāṭukaḷiṉ niṟuvaṉam matippiṭṭuḷḷatu. iṉṉum maṭṭavakaik kaṉimaṅkaḷ ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ nilattiṉ mēṟparappil malaikkuṉṟukaḷāka uḷḷaṉa. malaikaḷil ciṟu toḷaikaḷiṭṭuk kuṭaintu avaṟṟiṉuḷ veṭimaruntaic celutti veṭikkac ceytu atil kiṭaikkum kaṉimaṅkaḷaic cēkarittup pukaivaṇṭikaḷil ēṟṟi irumput toḻiṟcālaikaḷukku aṉuppu kiṟārkaḷ. nilattiṉ aṭiyil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ kāṇum iṭaṅkaḷil curaṅka vēlaikaḷ naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭum kaṉimattil kalantirukkum kallaiyum maṇalaiyum akaṟṟa atai nīril alaciyō valivāṉa kāntaṅkaḷaip payaṉ paṭuttiyō tuppuravu ceykiṟārkaḷ. kaṉimattaik kariyuṭaṉ cuṭṭup pañcu pōṉṟa poruḷākavō ciṟiya uruṇṭaikaḷākavō ceytu avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu irumpaip piritteṭuppatil cila vacatikaḷ uṇṭu.
kaṉimattaik kariyuṭaṉ kalantu cūṭēṟṟiṉāl irumpu pirikiṟatu. kaṉimattuṭaṉ cuṇṇāmpaik kalappatāl atil uḷḷa cilikā alumiṉā mutaliya acuttaṅkaḷ ataṉuṭaṉ kūṭi mūlak kacaṭākap pirikiṉṟaṉa.
aṟiviyal vaḷarcci peṟāta toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuc ciṟiya kaḷimaṇ ulaikaḷil tōl turuttikaḷiṉ mūlam irumpai urukki vantaṉar. marakkariyaip payaṉpaṭutti irumpai urukkiṉar. iṉṟuḷḷa māperum iyantiraṅkaḷum, nilakkariyum kāṟṟūtum iyantiraṅ kaḷum miṉ ulaikaḷum illāta kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ mukkiyamāṉa irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷāṉa mākaṉaṭaip kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Ores) kemaṭaiṭ mutaliyavaikaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittu vārppirumpu, tēṉirumpu, urukku mutaliyavaikaḷai uruvākkum muṟaikaḷait tam paṭṭaṟi vāl pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aṟintu āyiram āyiramākap paṭaikkaruvikaḷaiyum, vīṭṭukkuriya taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷai yum, payirt toḻil, taccuttoḻil, veṇkalap pāttiram ceyyum toḻil, kollar toḻil mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uḷi, cuttiyal, vāḷ, kuṟaṭu, iṭukki, aram mutaliya poruḷkaḷai ellām ceytu kuvittuḷḷataik kaṇṭu ulakam perum viyappaṭaintuḷḷatu.
teṉṉintiyā putiya kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyil aṭiyeṭuttu vaittu viṭṭatu oru viyattaku ceytiyākum. atu putuk kallūḻiyiṉiṉṟu veṇkala ūḻiyai aṭaiyavillai. atu putuk kallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyai aṭaintu viṭṭatu eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ aṉantappūr, kaṭappai, karnūl māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum aṅkumiṅkum citaṟuṇṭu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iḵtaṉṟit teṉṉintiyāviṉ ēṉaiya pala pakuti kaḷilumkūṭap putiya kallūḻiyiṉ kalappuḷḷa cāṉṟaṭaiyāḷaṅ kaḷum irumpup poruḷkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṅku irumpu ulakil mutaṉmutalākak kiṭaittuḷḷatu eṉṟu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu kāṇappaṭavillai. vaṭaintiyāvil mutaṉ mutalil irumpiṉ mūlap poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭu urukkappeṟṟup piṉ teṉ intiyāviṟku vantatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. atil aṇuvaḷavum uṇmaiyillai. teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta putiya kallūḻi makkaḷ taṟceyalāka irumpaik kaṇṭu atu paṭikakaṟ pāṟaiyaiviṭa nīṭittu uḻaikkak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar. teṉ ṉintiyāvil irumpai urukki ataip palvēṟu karuvikaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttum pāṅku airōppāvaiviṭa mikat toṉmai yāṉa kālattiliruntu nilavi vantuḷḷatu. atu piṉṉar ayalnāṭu kaḷilum - ciṟappākak kirīṭ, kirīcuvarai paraviyuḷḷatu - teṉ ṉintiyāvaippōl aṅkum pala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷākavum, niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷākavum eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.3
irumpu kiṭaitta iṭam
ātittanallūril irumpup poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. āṉāl, aṅku irumpu kiṭaikkavillai. teṉ intiyāvil irumpup poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittatāl aṅku irumput tātukkaḷ kiṭaittirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇuvatil tavaṟirukkamuṭiyātu. teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi arumpuvataṟku, ataṉ puviyiyal amaippē mukkiya kāraṇamākum. aṅkuḷḷa pāṟaikaḷ karuṅkaṟ pāṟaikaḷāka amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē appāṟaikaḷil irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ (Iron ores), māṅkaṉīcu (Manganese), poṉ vaṇṭal (Gold Deposits) īyam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅki iruk kiṉṟaṉa. cēlam māvaṭṭattilum, nellai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum nīlakiri, paḻaṉi, āṉaimalai, ēlakkāy kuṉṟu (Cardaman Hills) mutaliyavaṟṟilum nellūriṉiṉṟu makānati (Mahanadhi) varai kiḻakku malait toṭarkaḷilum irumpuk kaṉimaṅkaḷ iruppatu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅku kār ṉōkkaiṭc (Charnockites) eṉṟu iṉṟum aḻaikkappaṭṭuvarum karuṅkal (Granite) peritum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippāṟaikaḷil uḷḷa kāṭipporuḷ (Acid) kaḷiliruntu kaṭum kuṉaippāṉa irumpuc ciṭṭaṅkaḷ (Altra baeik) varaiyum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu maṇṇūlkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
āntira nāṭṭil mayilkuṉṟil uḷḷa kaṭikānūrp pakutiyil varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉar vāḻnta mutumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya kōṭari pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ ippoḻutu kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iḵtu antap pakutikku mikapperumai aḷippatākum eṉṟu varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. atōṭu, aṅku nīrāvit tuḷaikaḷuḷḷa erimalaik kuḻamputtuṇṭukaḷ niṟainta maṇmēṭukaḷ iruppatum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu pellāriyiṉ kuṉṟumukam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu. itu putiya kaṟkālam nilaviya iṭamāka eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. mēlum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum irumpu urukkiya ulōkak kacaṭukaḷiṉ uḷḷārnta taṉmai yiliruntu iḵtu irumpu ūḻi nilaviya iṭam eṉṟu eḷitil muṭiviṟku varamuṭiyum. mēlum iṅkuḷḷa kaṟutta allatu civanta irumpu uyirakaik kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ atai naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, oruvēḷai putiya kal ūḻiyiṉiṉṟu piṉ toṭarntu vanta irumpu ūḻiyil maṉitaṉuṭaiya mēmpāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcci paṭip paṭiyāy vaḷarntu varukiṟatu. putiya kallūḻi irumpu ūḻikku mika amaitiyāṉa muṟaiyil kaṭantu ceṉṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu teḷivāyk kuṟippiṭalām. pellāriyaṉ kuṉṟumukam, itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟu aḷikkiṟatu. cappāṉiya irumpu āyvuttuṟai vallunar, teṉṉintiyāvil irumpuk kaṉikaḷ iruntaṉa; oru kālattil aṅkiruntu irumpu ayalnāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumatiyāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu namakku ākkam aḷippatākum.4
teṉ intiyāvil, cempu ūḻiyō veṇkala ūḻiyō tōṉṟiyatillai; putiya kallūḻiyait toṭarntu ataṉ toṭakka kālattilē irumpu ūḻi arumpiviṭṭatu. muntuṟa eḻunta irumpu ūḻiyiṉ eñciya kūṟukaḷuṭaṉ putiya kallūḻiyiṉ eñciya poruḷkaḷai arukarukē vaittu āyntāl, aṉantappūr, kaṭappai, karnūl, nīlakiri māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil periya aḷavil irumpukkaṉimaṅkaḷ aṅkum iṅkum citaṟik kiṭappatu teriya varum. tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattila uḷḷa cērvarāyaṉ kuṉṟukaḷi lum (Shevroy Hills) teṉ intiyāviṉ ēṉaiya pakutikaḷilum irumpuk karuvikaḷum putiya kaṟkālak kalavaiyiṉ taṭaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. irumpu eṅku mutalil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu urukkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu iṉṉum muṭivākat tīrmāṉikkappaṭa villai. irumpu vaṭa intiyāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu aṅkiruntu teṉ intiyāviṟku aṟimukam ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatil uṇmai yillai. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa putiya kallūḻiyaic cērnta makkaḷ taṟceyalāka irumpaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar. atu paṭikap pāṟaiyaiviṭa valivuḷḷatāy nīṇṭa kālamāyiṉum keṭṭup pōkātatāyk kāṇappaṭṭatu. airōppāvai viṭa mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil teṉ intiyāvil irumput toḻil arumpi ēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟukūṭak kūṟalām. atu teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟik kirīṭ, kirīcu  mutaliya piṟa nāṭukaḷuk kellām paravat toṭaṅkiyatu. aṅkut teṉ intiyāvaippōl pala vaḻikaḷilum irumpup poruḷkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu an nāṭṭiluḷḷa araṇmaṉaikaḷum araṇkaḷum niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅ kaḷum, kōyilkaḷum aḻakum uṟutiyum nalla amaippum uṭaiyaṉa vāy viḷaṅkiṉa eṉṟu tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcatar avarkaḷ tam cīriya nūlāṉa varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya teṉ intiyā eṉṟa nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.5
ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil 32 vakaiyāṉa irumpup paṭaik kalaṉkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉum muyaṉṟu akaḻntu pārttāl 50kkuk kuṟaiyāta eṇṇikkaiyuḷḷa paṭaikkala vakaikaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭikkalām - tamiḻarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻi arumpuvataṟkumuṉ 30kku mēṟpaṭṭa vakaiyāṉa irumpup paṭaik kalaṅkaḷaip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpatē tamiḻ ulakam perumaip paṭuvataṟkuriya ceytiyākum. inta viyattaku ceytiyai aṟintu nāṉē perumitam aṭaikiṉṟēṉ; eṉ uḷḷam eṉṟumillāp pūrippeytiyuḷḷatu. tamiḻaṉ ulaka araṅkil, taṉ mārpil kaiyai vaittukkoṇṭu pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ irumpu ūḻiyaik kaṇṭu ulakilē oppaṟṟa eḵkup paṇpāṭṭai vaḷartta, naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam vāynta paḻamperum makkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavaṉ nāṉ eṉṟu tairiyamākak kūṟamuṭiyum.
irumpiṉ payaṉkaṇṭa makkaḷ
tamiḻarkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uyarnta paṇpāṭṭai vaḷarttu nūṟāyirakkaṇakkāṉa pōrkkaruvikaḷai yum toḻiṟ karuvikaḷaiyum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyum uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar. eṉṟālum ayalnāṭṭu makkaḷaiyō, tam aṇṭai nāṭṭu uṟaviṉarkaḷaiyōveṉṟu koṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ uḻaippaik kavarntu, uṇṭu koḻukka eṇṇiyatum illai; ceyaṟpaṭavum illai.
tamiḻar
taṉṉuyir nīppiṉum ceyyaṟka tāṉpiṟitu
iṉṉuyir nīkkum viṉai
eṉṉum aṟaneṟiyai maṟaimoḻiyāka matittu vāḻntavarkaḷ.
niṟka, tamiḻarkaḷ āṭci, kālakkoṭumaiyāl vīḻntatu. 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil innāṭṭaik kavara vanta, pōrccukīciyarkaḷō pirañcu, āṅkilēya veḷḷaikkārak koḷḷaiyarkaḷō, piṟarō (āriyarkaḷō mokalāyarkaḷō) tamiḻar kaṇṭa irumpuk karuvi kaḷaic cēkarittup paḻamporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyil vaikka villai tamiḻarkaḷ paṭaikkalaṉkaḷaippaṟṟi evvita nūlum eḻuti vaikkavillai.
nāṉ eṉ iḷamaip paruva mutal tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṭaikkalaṉ kaḷaic cēkarikkavum avarkaḷiṉ pōr muṟaikaḷ pōrkkaruvikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷaip paṭikkavum muyaṉṟuḷḷēṉ. nāṉ 1960ām āṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattilē varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōrmuṟaikaḷum pōrkkaruvikaḷum eṉṟa nūlai eḻutattoṭaṅkip pallāṇṭu kaḻittu muṭittuḷḷēṉ. annūl iṉṉum accēṟavillai.
1962 ām āṇṭu āṇṭakai ekarṭṭaṉ (Lord Egerton) 1896il eḻutiya intiyāvilum kīḻai nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa paṭaikkalaṉ kaḷiṉ viḷakkam eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlam tamiḻnāṭṭil uḷḷa āṅkila aracō, tolporuḷ ārāyccittuṟaiyō, paḻam poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyō, taṉippaṭṭa tolporuḷ cēkarippāḷar kaḷō, paḻaṅkālap paṭaikkalaṉmītu akkarai celuttavillai eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu. intiyap paṭaikkalaṉkaḷiṉ cēkarippum puṟakkaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatu teḷivākak kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉai, tañcai mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa paḻaṅ kālap paṭaikkalaṉkaḷ, pāmaramakkaḷālum, pōrk karuvikaḷaip paṟṟiya ārāycci vallunarkaḷālum aṟiviyal nipuṇarkaḷālum puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatāl avai paḻaiya ulōkap poruḷ kaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭu uṭaikkappaṭṭum ulai. mukattiliṭṭu urukkappaṭṭum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. iṉṟu muṟkāla makkaḷ evvitamāṉa paṭaikkalaṉkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpataṟku nammiṭam evvitac cāṉṟum illai. itaippaṟṟi 1896 ām āṇṭil aṇṇal ekarṭṭaṉ namakku aṟivuṟuttiṉār. āṉāl 1978ām āṇṭu varai tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷō, tamiḻnāṭṭup palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷō immi aḷavum akkarai kāṭṭavillai.6
namatu nāṭṭil palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa vāḷkaḷum, vēlkaḷum, kaikkōṭarikaḷum, kattikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatai namatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa cilaikaḷ, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ, acentā ōviyam, aṇṇal vāyil ōviyam, maṟṟum pala kukai ōviyaṅkaḷ mūlamum tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa putai kuḻikaḷil kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭa irumpup poruḷkaḷ mūlamāyum naṉkaṟiyalām eṉṟu paṇṭaikkāla intiyāvum pōrkkalaiyum eṉṟa nūlil muṉaivar pi.ci. cakkaravartti eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
(a) paṇṭaik kālattut tirāviṭa intiyāvil payaṉ paṭuttiya irumpālāṉa īṭṭi, eṟivēl, kuttuvāḷ, uṭaivāḷ irupuṟamum kūrmaiyuḷḷa vaḷainta katti (kirīcu) mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟi āriyarkaḷukkut teriyātu. māpāratap pōril īṭṭiyum vēlum mukkiya pōrk karuvikaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉa. avai palvēṟu peyarkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. mūvilai vēl teṉṉin tiyarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya taṉipperum paṭaikkaruvi. atu civa perumāṉ karattil ēntappeṟṟa cīriya karuviyākum. intak karuvi, eṅkaḷuṭaiyatu eṉṟu āriyarō, cīriyarō, mokalāyarkaḷō urimai koṇṭāṭa muṭiyātu eṉṟu tiru.e.pi. cantira cēkaraṉ pampāy pavāṉi tiṅkaḷ itaḻil virivāka paṇṭaik kāla intiyap pōrk karuvikaḷ eṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pōrk karuvikaḷ
nāṉ paṇṭaiya tamiḻ makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya pōrk karuvi kaḷaip paṟṟi eḻutiya varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōr muṟaikaḷum pōrk karuvikaḷum eṉṟa nūlil virivākap pala pōrk karuvikaḷaippaṟṟi viḷakkan tantuḷḷēṉ. namatu varuṅkāla iḷam talaimuṟaiyāḷarkaḷ paṇṭaik kālap paṭaik kalaṉkaḷaip pārkka muṭiyāmal pōṉālum peyarkaḷaiyāvatu aṟintiruk kaṭṭum eṉṟu avaṟṟai vakaippaṭutti oru paṭṭiyal aḷit tuḷḷēṉ. eṉatu paṭṭiyalil tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya ellā irumpup paṭaikkaruvikaḷum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu eṉṉāl uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu. ettaṉaiyō pōrkkaruvikaḷ viṭupaṭ ṭirukkalām eṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalām. cintu veḷiyaip pōl putaiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ oṉṟaikkūṭa akaḻntu āyum vāypput tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku ituvarai kiṭṭavillai. attakaiya vāyppu eḻuntālaṉṟip paṇṭait tamiḻar payaṉpaṭuttiya oru niṟaivāṉa paṭaikkalaṉkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalai nām pārkka muṭiyātu.
iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pōrk karuvi kaḷai maṉattiṟkoṇṭu nām ulakil oppaṟṟa nākarikattaip peṟṟu - vāḻaipaṭi vāḻaiyākat teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntu vanta vīrarkaḷiṉ marapu vaḻiyil vanta makkaḷ eṉṟu perumitattōṭu nimirntu niṉṟu ulakil evariṭamum pēcamuṭiyum. ulaka varalāṟṟil en nāṭṭilum, tamiḻakattaippōla pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ittuṇai vakai vakaiyāṉa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa irumpuk karuvikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu evarum meyppikka muṭiyumā? muṭiyātu. kāraṇam, tamiḻakattil mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē irumpu ūḻiyum irumpup paṇpāṭum nilaviyatu oṉṟu. maṟṟoṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷōṭu putai kalaṉkaḷil avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭaikkalaṉkaḷaiyum piṟa karuvikaḷaiyum vaittup putaikkum marapu vaḻakkam ākum. ātitta nallūr īmak kāṭṭai akaḻntu āyum vāyppu eḻāmal iruntāl nām intap pēṟṟai eyti irukka muṭiyātu.
nām nam tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nalaṉaik karuti ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaiyum piṟa ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kaṇṭa peyarkaḷaiyum vakuttut tokuttu itaṉ aṭiyil tantuḷḷōm. ataṉaik kaṇṭu teḷika.

(13) cakkara vakaikaḷ
tikiri (cakkaram, nēmi, āḻi, eḵkam, vaḷai, pāti, oḷi vaṭṭam, cutaricaṉam)
niṟka, mēlē eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟavai tavira, namatu ciṟpa nūlkaḷ, ilakkiya nūlkaḷ, ōviya nūlkaḷ, purāṇa nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu 500kku mēṟpaṭṭa paṭaikkalaṉkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷaic cēkarittu akara varicaippaṭutti eṉṉāl eḻuti veḷiyiṭa irukkum varalāṟṟil kāṇum pōr muṟaikaḷum, pōrk karuvi kaḷum eṉṟa nūlil oru paṭṭiyal iṇaittuḷḷēṉ. virivañci avai aṉaittaiyum iṅkuk kuṟippiṭātu viṭuttuḷḷēṉ.
vil, ulakiluḷḷa palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa malaivāḻ makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. aḵtu iṉṟu pala nākarikam peṟṟa nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭaiya tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuṉṟak kuṟavarkaḷiṭamum pōr maṟavarkaḷiṭamum palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa vilkaḷ iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ cila villēnti iruntaṉa eṉṟu paḻaṅkataikaḷ collukiṉṟaṉa. villaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatil paṇṭaiya tamiḻarkaḷ taṉittiṟaṉ vāyntavarkaḷāy iruntu vantaṉar. pōrk kaḷattil mikka cakti vāynta paṭaikkalaṉkaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻarkaḷ villaip payaṉ paṭutti vantaṉar paḻaṅkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ ampukaḷiṉ nuṉiyil eḵkuk kavacam pūṭṭi atai naṉku payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. avai maṉita uṭalkaḷai maṭṭum aṉṟi vilakkukaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷaiyum, maraṅkaḷaiyum tuḷaittuc cellattakkaṉavāy iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āntira nāṭṭil acantā ōviyaṅkaḷilum maturai mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar kōyiṟ ciṟpaṅkaḷilum vil oppaṟṟa paṭaik karuviyāy viḷaṅkiyatu eṉṟu tiru cantiracēkarar pavāṇi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
(a) ātittanallūril kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paṭaikkalaṉ kaḷil (Arms) pakaivarkaḷmītu pāyccuvataṟku ēṟṟa eṭai kuṟaintavai ēṟivēl (Javelin) īṭṭi (Spear) pōṉṟavaiyākum. ivai tirāviṭamakkaḷ pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttum mukkiya māṉa karuvikaḷākum. ittakaiya paṭaikkalaṉkaḷaic cila vētakāla āriyarkaḷum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷ intiyā viṟkuḷ aṭiyeṭuttu vaippataṟku muṉ ittakaiya karuvikaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutaṟku eḷḷaḷavum cāṉṟu illai. pāratap pōril āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭa aracaṉāṉa kiruṭṭiṇaṉ tuṇaiyaip peṟṟaṉar. tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṇṭa ciṟanta paṭaikkalaṉ kaḷāṉa vāḷ, vēl, īṭṭi, kuttuvāḷ pōṉṟa pōrkkaruvikaḷait tāṅkip pōr naṭattiṉar. pala, tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ pōrkkaruvikaḷ cakti, kuṇṭā, kuṇapā, cāliyam pōṉṟa vaṭa moḻippeyarkaḷ tāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūvilaivēl, tiricūlam eṉṟa peyaril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. inta mūvilaivēl muttalaivēl eṉṉum oruvakai īṭṭi. itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ taṉipperum kaṭavu ḷāṉa civaṉār karattil uḷḷatu eṉṟu paḻaṅkataikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
civaperumāṉ tirukkarattil uḷḷa pōrk karuviyāka avar vīṟṟirukkum kayilaimalaiyil uḷḷa mūṉṟu cikaraṅkaḷāṉa nantākuṉṟi, nantakōṭu, nantātēvi eṉpavai (mūvilai vēlākak) kaṟpaṉai ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. civaperumāṉ aintu talaikaḷai yum pattuk karaṅkaḷaiyumuṭaiya mātēvaṉ (makēcuvaraṉ) āka ēṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷār. avaruṭaiya pattuk kaikaḷilum pācam, paracu, muttalai vēl, cūlam, īṭṭi mutaliya pala karuvikaḷ iruppatākak kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ maṟaiyāṉa ākamaṅkaḷilum maṟai nūlāṉa tirukkuṟaḷ, tirumantiram, tēvāram, tiruvācakam pōṉṟavaṟṟilum pala pōrkkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ tarappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. itaṉai muṉṉāḷ pampāy mānila araciṉ amaiccarum ciṟanta civapaktarumāṉa kālañceṉṟa tiru. kē.em. muṉci avarkaḷai āciriyarākak koṇṭu veḷivanta pavāṉi eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ ēṭṭil aṟiñar cantiracēkaraṉ avarkaḷ mīṇṭum tiṟampaṭa naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
intiyāviṉ tātupporuṭ celvam
irumpuk kaṉic celvam ciṟappumikka mukkiyattuvam vāyntatuvām. intiyāviṉ irumpukkaṉic celvam 300 kōṭi ṭaṉkaḷ irukkum eṉpatu kuṟainta aḷavuk kaṇippu. piriṭṭaṉil 225 kōṭi 40 laṭcam ṭaṉkaḷ, cermaṉiyil 137 kōṭi 40 laṭcam ṭaṉkaḷ. amerikkāvilum pirāṉcilum māttiram intiyāvai viṭa atikamāṉa irumpukkaṉikaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu matippiṭap paṭṭaṉa. intiya pū vētiyal paricīlaṉait tuṟai cīcil cōṉ mūlataṉam (Capital) eṉṟa itaḻil 1929ām āṇṭu eḻutiya kaṭṭurai.
intiyā urimai peṟumuṉ 1933ām āṇṭil irāyal empayar cocaiṭṭi (Royal empire society)Æ‹ ātaravil car alpiraṭvāṭcaṉ eṉpavar aṭiyil varum uṇmaikaḷai ulakiṟku uṇarttiyuḷḷār.
toḻil tuṟaiyil intiyā pala vāyppukaḷai iḻantu viṭṭatu. itaṟku (aṉṟu) āṭcippīṭattaik kavarntu atil amarntirunta āṅkila āṭciyē poṟuppākum ... oru periya iyantirattoḻil nāṭāka oḷirvataṟkēṟṟa cūḻnilaikaḷ aṉaittum mikutiyāka iruntum poruḷātārat tuṟaiyilum, toḻil tuṟaiyilum intiyā iṉṟu ulakiṉ piṟpōkkāṉa nāṭu kaḷil oṉṟāy irukkiṟatu ... intiyāviṉ toḻil vaḷarcciyiṉ piṟpōkkiṉai nām oru poḻutum akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ tīrkka muṉ varavillai.8
intiyā, taṉ nāṭṭumakkaḷiṉ tēvaikaḷ perukuvatiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil aṭuttuvarum āṇṭukaḷil ituvarai kaṇṭirāta vēkattil iyantirat toḻil vaḷarcci aṭaiyāviṭṭāl iṉṟē añcattakka aḷavukkut tāḻntirukkum. nāṭṭiṉ vāḻkkaittaram paṭṭiṉikiṭakkum nilaiyait tāṇṭiviṭum -(eṉṟu ilaṇṭaṉ ṭaimcu pattirikai 4-1-1933il eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. iṉṉum intiya maṇṇiṉ aṭiyil putaintu kiṭakkum kaṉi vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttakkāṭṭiyatōṭu intiya uṇavu uṟpattiyilum, iyantira necavilum nām ceyya vēṇṭiya tuṇi uṟpattiyum, puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷaiyum intiyāvait tantai nāṭākavum, iṅkilāntait tāy nāṭākavum eṇṇip paṇiyāṟṟiya aṉaittulakap potu vuṭaimai iyakka māperun talaivar tōḻar iraciṉi pāmipattatar iṉṟaiya intiyā eṉṟa nūlil (4-11-1947il) tamiḻil moḻi yākkam peṟṟa nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. avar irumpaip paṟṟi maṭṭumaṉṟi, intiyāviṉ kaṉipporuḷ, viḷaiporuḷ, cey poruḷ aṉaittaiyum paṟṟiya puḷḷi vivaraṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi intiya makkaḷiṉ naṉmatippaip peṟṟār. piriṭṭicu aracu āṟṟiya koṭumaikaḷai ovvoṉṟāyt tam nūlil viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.5
cappāṉ nāṭṭil vāḻnta tātupporuḷ ārāycci vallunar kaḷil (Metallurgist) pērum perumaiyum peṟṟavar pērāciriyar kōvalāntu eṉpavarāvar. ivar cappāṉ tīvukaḷil palvēṟu pakutikaḷil irumpu kiṭaikkum iṭaṅkaḷai aṟiyac cuṟṟuppayaṇam ceytu pala irumpuc curaṅkaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuc cappāṉ nāṭṭiṉ irumpu vaḷattiṟku ākkam aḷitta aṟiñar āvar. avar, irumpaik kaṇṭu piṭittatum atai urukkum muṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittatum etirpārāta orunikaḻcciyāka iruntirukkalām. āṉāl, airōppāvaiviṭa mika mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil intiyat tīvakattil toḻil nuṭpam uṇarnta pēraṟiñar uḷḷat til irumput toḻiltuṟai etirpārāta naṟpēṟāka arumpi yirukka vēṇṭum. irumpaip payaṉpaṭuttiyapiṉ teṉ intiyāvil tārāḷamāka eṅkum kiṭaikkum nilai eḻuntapiṉ ayalnāṭu kaḷukkum ciṟappāka kirīṭ, kirīcu nāṭukaḷukkum parava ārampittiruk kalām.
kirīṭ, kirīcu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷāy eḻunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum irumpu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu teṉ intiyāvaippōl palvēṟu vakaikaḷil ciṟappuṟṟu ārampittuḷḷatu. kirēkka nāṭum kirīṭ tīvum ēṉaiya mēṟkattiya nāṭukaḷum taṅkaḷuṭaiya putiya kal ūḻiyaik kaṭantu irumpup paṇpāṭṭiṉaik kūṭa aṭaiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. kal ūḻi maṉitaṉ mutaṉmutalāka malaikaḷil vāḻntuvantāṉ. avaṉ uṇaviṉṟi aṅkumiṅkum alaintu tirinta nilaimaiyum aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉ puṟa ellaiyaik kaṭakka eṇṇum ārvamum avaṉ vāḻkkaiyil oru putumaiyaik kāṇacceytatu. irumpu ulōkak fU(Iron ore)it¡ kaṇṭupiṭitta piṉṉartāṉ, paḻaṅkāla, kuṉṟil vāḻnta mutirāp paṇpuṭaiya maṉitaṉ malaiyiṉiṉṟu iḻintuvarum āṟṟiṉ karaikaḷ vaḻiyāka malai aṭivārattiṟku iṟaṅkivantu aṭarnta kāṭukaḷai aṭaiya muṟpaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kāṭukaḷiṉ vāḻum muṟaiyaip payiṉṟāṉ. ākaiyāl irumpup paṇpāṭu tāṉ kuṉṟukaḷil vāḻnta kuṟiñci nila makkaḷai malai aṭivārat til amaintuḷḷa kāṭārnta mullai nila vāḻkkaikku mēva vaḻi kāṭṭiyatu. itutāṉ mutirāp paṇpuṭaiya mutumakkaḷāṉa kallūḻi makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyil oru putiya nilaiyākum. ituvē paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa mutaṟpaṭi yākum.8
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭum - ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭum
cintuveḷiyil mokañcatārōvai mutaṉmutalāka 1922 ām āṇṭu vaṅkap pērāciriyar ār.ṭi. pāṉarcci. muṉṉiṉṟu akaḻāyvu naṭattiṉār. akaḻntu āyntu oru periya nakar 70 aṭi āḻattiṉ kīḻ iruppataik kaṇṭu piṭittu ataṟku iṟantārkaḷiṉ nakaram eṉṉum poruḷ paṭa mokañcatārō eṉṉum peyarai aḷittār. avartām mutaṉmutalil cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu, cempum maṇṇum kalanta cālkōlitik (Chalcolithic age) kālam eṉṉum ūḻiyil eḻunta tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉṟu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār.
ivar ēṟkeṉavē ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvaip paṟṟi nuṇuki uṇarntiruntār. eṉavē tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu teṉ intiyāviṉ teṉkōṭiyiṉiṉṟu vaṭaintiyā muḻuvatilum paravip paluccicuttāṉ vaḻiyākap pāracīkam īrāṉ pakraiṉtīvu mutaliyavaikaḷilellām paravi kirīṭtīvu varai paraviyuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu ātittanallūril arumpi arappā varai aṟuntu pōkāta caṅkilit toṭarpōl tikaḻkiṟatu eṉṟu car jāṉ mārcal avarkaḷukkut tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭiṉ toṉmaiyai eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. 1927ām āṇṭu kalkattāvi ṉiṉṟu vanta māṭarṉ reviyū eṉṟa tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭil tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu eṉṉum talaippil mūṉṟu toṭar kaṭṭuraikaḷ eḻutit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmaiyāṉa nākarikac ciṟap piṟku ākkan tēṭiṉār. ātittanallūril arumpiyatu irumpu ūḻiyil kaṇṭa irumpup paṇpāṭu eṉṟu ulakatira moḻintār.9
ataṉ piṉṉarc ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka varalāṟṟu āciriyarum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaip pērāciriyarumāṉa tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcatar ātittanallūril arumpiyatu ulakil oppaṟṟa irumpup paṇpāṭu eṉṟum, atu ki.mu. eṭṭā yiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟum, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ uyaravum vāṇikam vaḷaravum vāḻvu malaravum āḻ kaṭal kaṭantu kaṭal vaṇikam perukavum mūlakāraṇamāka iruntatu avarkaḷ kaṇṭa irumpup paṇpāṭē eṉṟu 1951ām āṇṭil avar āṟṟiya ulliyam meyyar coṟpoḻivil mika virivākat tiṟampaṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. ātittanallūr akaḻvāyvil āḻnta pulamaivāynta tiru. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar tam coṟ poḻiviṉ mūlam ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭiṟku ākkam aḷitta nallārāka matikkappaṭṭuḷḷār.10

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
V
1.	Catalogue of the Prehistoric Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbair - by Alexander Rea F.S.A. (Scot) (Madras) 1915.
2.	kalaik kaḷañciyam tokuti iraṇṭu, tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam, ceṉṉai. mutaṟpatippu irumpu (ceṉṉai) 1955. pakkam: 82-84.
3.	“According to a mighty authority the civilization of the Indus valley was one that had become already age old and stereotyped on the Indian soil. How old it is we cannot definitely say that this stretch of time but we can certainly say that it was in the later stage of the iron culture of South India; for in our opinion the iron culture of the South India may be dated from eight to Ten Thousand B.C. Therefore it is not strange that the Indian Culture had many millions of human endeavour behind it” -Pre-historic South India by V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 243.
	(a)	The period of the Indus Valley Civilization is regarded as to be from 3250-2750 B.C. by Dr.R.K. Mukerji on the basis of the remains traced, in the excavation of the only one of the seven layers. Below these layers also there are other layers. These excavations would give the Indus Valley Civilization a much earlier date than 3270 B.C. Anyway since the existing remains of the Indus Valley gave to the period of 3250 and since its information is available in the literature of the Aryan the Rigveda of 3250 to 2500 is regarded as Proto-Historic period in theAnalysis of the history of Ancient India. Due to this Consideration the Indus Valley Civilization is placed in the Proto-Historic period of the Ancient Indian History” - Ancient Indian History Civilization and Culture by P.S.Joseph M.A. & others.
4.	Pre-historic South India-by Prof. V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. p. 75.
5.	In South India there was no Bronze age or Copper age as such but it was the Iron age that succeeded the Neolithic age. If we study the Neolithic remains side by side with the relics of the early Iron age, a large quantity seems to be scattered as is found in the districts of Anantapur Cudappa and Kurnool also. The shevroy hills, the district of Tinnevelly and other parts of South India contain vestiges of the mixture of Neolithic and iron implements. It is not yet settled where iron was first found and smelted. There is no truth in the saying that it was introduced from North India to the South. Our belief is that the Neolithic people of South India should have come across iron accidentally and found it more durable and together than the trappoid rock. It may also be said that the iron industry in South India is more ancient than that of Europe. It began to speread to other countries especially to Crete and Greece where architectural monuments and shrines resemble in several respects the South Indian one” - Pre-historic South India - Prof. V.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951. pp. 95-96.
6.	“Neither Collection is rich in the South Indian arms and it is a matter of regret that the Government in India while professing to watch over the preservation of archaeological remains has neglected to take advantage of the opportunity of acquiring old weapons from the armouries at Tanjore and Madras and allowed them to be broken up and the contents sold as old metal” - A Description of Indian and oriental Armour Rt. Hon. Lord Egerton of Tatton, M.A. (London 1896).
7.	It is noteworthy that all these three types of Sword, are represented in the Frescoes and Sculpture, at Ajanta while kirich and pattisa blades have been found in the Tinnevelly urn burials” - Art of war in ancient India - Dr.P.C. Chakravarti, M.A., Ph.D. 1941. p. 14.
8.	“The Knowledge and practices of this mighty weapon was not confined to North India. South India knew this weapon well from the beginning of its written History. The huge figure of warriors particularly that the Arjuna carved out in the Meenakshi Temple of Madura well might prove its existence in South India as an efficient instrument of fighting” - Apparatus of war in ancient India - A.P. Chandrasegaran (an article in Bhavans Journal) Bombay. 1959.
	(a)	Javelin and spear which came next in importance were seldom used by Vedic Aryans although these weapons were not known to them. It was during the Mahabarata war that Javelin and spear attained importance and they were known by different names Sakti, Kunta, Kunapa, Salyan Sanka etc. The trident which was a kind of spear was popular in South India and its was believed that it was the weapon of Lord Siva who of all the three gods Creation, preservation, destruction commands the highest veneration and popularity among the South Indians. Thirukkural, the Tamil Bible mentions the lance in two places. Apparatus of war in ancient India - A.P. Chandrasegaran (Bhavans Journal. 27.12.1959) Bombay p.60.
	(b)	It was Prof. Gowland the well-known metallurgist and explorer of the Japanese islands who expressed the opinion that the smelting of iron may have been hit upon by sheeraccident. The accident might have happened in peninsular India were according to the best minds the iron Industry is much more ancient than in Europe. After the use of iron became familiar in South India it began to spread in other countries specially Crete and Greece whose architectural monuments and shrines resemble in several respects those of South India. This is how Crete, Greece and other Western countries passed on also to iron culture from their Neolithic age. The stone age men were primarily living on hills and mountain fastnesses and also on the frings of rich forests ... Only after the discovery of iron ore it is resonable to suppose that primeval man took to the forest and made it his habits. Therefore it is only iron Culture that permitted the people of the hills to pass on to forest life” - Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. Madras. 1951. p. 97.
9.	Dravidian in South India such tombs vaults and cemetrles belong exclusively to the age of Iron. Iron implements, weapons and other objects are to be found in large numbers in the tombs vaults, coffins and urns. But this Iron age is not far distant from the end of the copper age, as along with Iron implements are to be found beautiful pots and other objects made of Bronze.
	Copper Age burials discovered in Northern India are very few in number but they prove the Iron age burials of South India to be a continuation and extension of the copper age methods of disposal of the Dead. It is perhaps well known that in India the remains of the copper age have not been discovered South of the Godavari but in that country copper and bronze were used for the manufacture of Art-ware, in discovered Iron age coffins. The Similarity of methods for disposae of the dead prove that the Iron age culture of Southern India is the lineal descendant of the Copper Age Culture of Northern India” - Dravidian Civilization by R.D. Banerji (Modern Review Calcutta) 1927, pp. 305, 553.
10.	These graves at Adichanallur are to be dated in the early Iron Age which succeeded the Neolithic Age in South India” - Origin and Spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1947. p. 21.
	“We can certainly say that it was in the later stage of the iron Culture of South India for in our opinion the iron Culture of South India may be dated from eight to 10,000 B.C. Therefore it is not strange that the Indian Culture had many millienia of human endeavour behind it” - Pre-historic South India by, Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A. (Madras) 1951, p. 243.
