6. veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ
(BRONZE OBJECTS)
irumpu ūḻiyil veṇkalam
ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu ērāḷamāṉa paḻam poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum oruvakai. veṇkalap poruḷkaḷil, pūmpaṭikkaṅ kaḷum caṭṭikaḷum vaḷaikaḷum ciṟappāṉavaiyākum. makkaḷ ātiyil nīr aruntum pāttiraṅkaḷākac curaik kuṭukkaikaḷaiyum, tēṅkāyc ciraṭṭaikaḷaiyum, ōlaippaṭṭaikaḷaiyum, ilai kaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa toṉṉaikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. pallāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟa piṉṉartāṉ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum piṉṉarc cuṭumaṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum ceytaṉar. nīr aruntavum cōṟu camaikkavum, kaṟivaikkavum kiṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum kalayaṅkaḷaiyum caṭṭikaḷaiyum pāṉaikaḷaiyum kuṭaṅkaḷaiyum uruvākkiṉar. appāl avai eḷitil uṭaintu pōvatai aṟintu veṇkalat tālum, cempālum irumpālum kiṇṇam, caṭṭi, pāṉai, kuṭam, akappai, kuvaḷai, pūkkiṇṇam pōṉṟavaṟṟaic ceyya muṟ paṭṭaṉar.
maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaip pōl allatu kaṟcaṭṭiyaip pōl cempuk kuṭaṅkaḷum, pittaḷaic caṭṭikaḷum veṇkalap pāṉai kaḷum eḷitil ceyyak kūṭiyavai alla. pāṭupaṭṭuc ceytāl nīṇṭa kālam uṭaiyātu. aḻiyātu payaṉpaṭum. tamiḻakattil putuk kalūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil irumpu ūḻi eḻuntatiṉ payaṉāka irumpuc caṭṭikaḷum irumpu uḷi, cuttiyal, kuṟaṭu, cammaṭṭi, veṭṭirumpu, iṭukki pōṉṟavaiyum ceyyum vāyppu eḻuntatu. eṉavē, tamiḻakattil kollar, kaṉṉār pōṉṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷ tōṉṟi irumpuk karuvikaḷāl veṇkalam ēṉaṅkaḷaic ceyya muṟpaṭṭaṉar. kaṉṉārkaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivālum toḻil tiṟamai yālum ērāḷamāṉa veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ ceytu kuvikkappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ
ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷil iṅkup paṭamiṭṭuk kāṭṭappeṟṟavai kuvaḷai, cāṭi, tōcaikkal, kiṇṇam, kuṭam, keṇṭi, callaṭaik kuṭikalam, ciṟu kuvaḷaikaḷ pōṉṟavaiyum piṟavumām. kuvaḷaikaḷ perumpālum koṟuvāykaḷai yuṭaiyaṉavāyum, uṭaintaṉavāyum iruk kiṉṟaṉa.
muṟkālattil, veṇkala ēṉaṅkaḷ vilaiyēṟap peṟṟaṉa vāyum kiṭaittaṟkariya poruḷkaḷāyum iruntaṉa. eṉavē, vīṭṭil payaṉpaṭuttutaṟkuriya ariya poruḷkaḷaiyellām iṟantavarkaḷōṭu putaikkāmal iruntirukkalām. iṟantavarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya mukkiyamāṉa poruḷkaḷai maṭṭum avarkaḷōṭu putaittirukkalām. atōṭu periya aṇṭā, kuttup pōṇi, tavalai, vārppu, pōṇiccaṭṭi, kuṇṭāccaṭṭi pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷaic cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyta tāḻikkuḷ vaikka muṭiyātu. eṭai mikunta anta veṇkalac caṭṭikaḷiṉ cumaiyait tāḻikaḷ tāṅka muṭiyātu. atu maṭṭumaṉṟip periya veṇkalac cāṭikaḷ, periya veṇkalak kuṭaṅkaḷ, cempup pāṉaikaḷ, cempuc caṭṭikaḷ, pittaḷaic caṭṭikaḷ, veṇkalap pāvai viḷakkukaḷ, veṇkalak kuttu viḷakkukaḷ, tūkku viḷakkukaḷ, nantā viḷakkukaḷ pōṉṟavai yum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭaikkavillai eṉpatu niṉaikkattakkatākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa paṭam 11l eṇ 1 mutal 15 varaiyuḷḷa pāttiraṅkaḷ ellām veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭavaiyākam. 16 mutal 30 varai eṇṇuḷḷa veṇkalap poruḷ pūkkiṇṇam, paralkaḷ, pātac cilampu, kāṟ cataṅkai, vaḷaiyal, nāy, cēval, maṟimāṉ, āṭu puli pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷaiyuṭaiya pūntaṭṭukaḷum, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷum, kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaiyaṉa. meyyākavē ceyviṉait tiṟam vāyntavai eṉa evarum oppuvar.
pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷ
pūk kiṇṇaṅkaḷ pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakat til nilaviya oppaṟṟa - uyariya ariya viyattakum nākarikattiṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamillai. ulakilēyuḷḷa enta nāṭṭu varalāṟṟuciriyarkaḷum, tolporuḷ āyvu vallunarkaḷum ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭiṟku piṉṉaṉṟi aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar kūṭa ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta pāṅkāṉa pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷukku oppāṉa etaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟum itaṉ mūlam, tamiḻakattil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē nilaviya naṉiciṟanta nākarikattil eḻunta veṇkalap pāṉai caṭṭikaḷaiyum pūk kiṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum uruvākkum kaṉṉārkaḷaip pōṉṟu toḻil nuṭpam vāynta toḻilāḷarkaḷai eṅkum kaṇṭirukka muṭiyātu eṉṟum uṟutiyuṭaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
veṇkala ūḻi
teṉ intiyāvil, irumpu ūḻi eḻuntataṉ piṉṉar atai aṭuttu, cempum veṇkalamum payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam vantuḷḷatu. oru cila tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷ. cempu ūḻiyum veṇkala ūḻiyum, irumpu ūḻikku muṉ vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟi yuḷḷaṉar. maicūr aitarāpāttu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu kaṉip poruḷkaḷiṉ aṭi nilattaṭak kāṭciḷaic cāṉṟāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. atil cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷum maṟṟum taṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷkaḷum aṭaṅki iruntaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. intac cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷai nām kavaṉamākap paṭittup paricōtaṉai ceyya vēṇṭum. irumpu eṉṉum tātup poru ḷukku muṉṉōṭiyākac cempum veṇkalamum iruntaṉa eṉṟa koḷkaiyaip pēcuvatu ēṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. cāṉṟukaḷ cila kāṭṭalām; avai puṟakkaṇikkak kūṭiyavaiyē. āṉāl, teṉ intiyāvil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ toṭarntu irumpu ūḻiyaik kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟa koḷkaiyai uṟutippaṭuttu vataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.1 teṉ intiyamakkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya aṟivai aṭaintataṟku vaṭaintiyāviṟkuc ciṟitākavō allatu peri tākavō kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām. iḵtu oru ciṟitum mikappaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭataṉṟu.
vaṭaintiyāvil veṇkala ūḻi
vaṭaintiyāviṉ paṇpāṭṭaippaṟṟiya muṟaiyāṉa āyvu naṭattiyavarkaḷ aṅku mutalil irumpiṉ upayōkam paṟṟi makkaḷ aṟiyātavarkaḷāy iruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vaṭa intiyāvil vāḻnta makkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatil muṉṉēṟṟam eyti iruntaṉar. ataṉ piṉṉartāṉ avarkaḷ irumpaip paṟṟiya aṟivaip peṟṟu, ataip payaṉpaṭutta muṉvantaṉar eṉpatu ulakaṟinta uṇmaiyāka irukkiṟatu. teṉintiyāvil iruntataip pōṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil irumpup paṇpāṭu iruntatē illai eṉpatu tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāṉa uṇmai yākum. vaṭakkiṟkum teṟkiṟkum iṭaiyē mika noymmai yāṉa toṭarpu iruntatu; ataṉ viḷaivāka aṭut taṭuttu naṭaipeṟṟu vanta kuṭiyēṟṟattiṉālum veḷiyēṟṟat tiṉālum, cempu, veṇkalam ākiya tātup poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu ēṉaṅkaḷum, vīṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum eppaṭic ceyvatu eṉṟa aṟivai virivākat teṉ intiyā peṟṟatu. ciṟitu kālañ ceṉṟa piṉṉarē, irumpu etaṟkup payaṉpaṭum? atai evvāṟu payaṉpaṭuttavatu? eṉpatai vaṭa intiyā aṟiya muṉ vantatu.
kiṭṭattaṭṭa aimpattāṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭiṉ paricōtaṉaiyāl inta nilaimai naṉku viḷakkap paṭṭatu. cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa ciṟappāṉa pakutikaḷāṉa arappā vilum, mokañcatārōvilum lokuñcutārōvilumuḷḷa niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ aḻivupaṭāk kūṟukaḷum akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu, uḷḷārnta paṇpu, kālakkaṇippu, eḻuttup pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷai iṉṉum teḷḷat teḷiya āyavēṇṭum cintuveḷi nākarikattai uruvākkiya mutalmakkaḷaip paṟṟip palvēṟu koḷkaikaḷ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. eyc. ār. hāl cintuveḷi akaḻ āyviṟkup pallāṇṭiṟku muṉ cintuveḷi makkaḷ cumēriyāviliruntu vantavarkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu tamatu aṇmaik kiḻakkiṉ paḻaimaiyāṉa varalāṟu eṉṟa nūlil oru karuttait terivittār.2 cintuveḷiyil ceyta akaḻvāyvuttuṟai iyakkunar taḷapati (Director general of Archaelogy) car cāṉ mārcal avarkaḷ aṅku akaḻntu kaṇṭa nakaram, terukkaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ, kuḷaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, māṭivīṭukaḷ, naṭaṉamāṭum naṅkaiyiṉ veṇkalap paṭimam, aracaṉ allatu kuruveṉak kāṭci aḷikkum neṟṟippaṭṭam kaṭṭiya cuṇṇaccilaikaḷ, naṭaṉamāṭum teyvac cilai, tāyt teyvaccuṭumaṇcilai muttiraiyil kāṇappaṭum pacupati uruvam. civaliṅkaṅkaḷ, muttiraiyil kāṇappaṭum teyva uruvamāṉa yāṉai, mīṉ, kāḷai, viyaṉciṉaimā, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, pācipavaḻaṅkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, veṇkalappāttiraṅkaḷ, veṇkalattil ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, veṇ kalattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa paṭaikkalaṅkaḷ, tantattiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, paṭakukaḷ pōṉṟa eṇṇilāp poruḷkaḷ ovvoṉṟaiyum eṭuttu uṉṉippāka uṟṟunōkki viññāṉap pāṅkāka alaci, āyntu cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭaip paṭaitta varkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku vantār.3 avarai aṭuttu akaḻvāyvu ceyta mekkē, car-mārṭṭimar uyilar, ār.ṭi. pāṉarji mutaliyavarkaḷum mārcal muṭipai nalla muṭipāka ēṟṟuk koṇ ṭaṉar.4 inta akaḻ āyvuttuṟaiyil īṭupaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷil perumpāṉmaiyiṉar cintuveḷinākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟu uṟutip paṭuttiṉar. cintuveḷi tirāviṭa nākarikam cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷil oppuyarvaṟṟatu; eṉṟālum iṅkuḷḷōr irumpu nākarikattai aṟiyātavarkaḷ; veṇkala nākarikattiṉāl uyarvaṭaintārkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa tamiḻmakkaḷ, irumpunākarikattaip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ; vaṭaintiyat tirāviṭa makkaḷ veṇkala nākarikattaip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatākum.
teṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ civa vaḻipāṭum, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭum, cakti vaḻipāṭum, kāḷaivaḻipāṭum, āṟṟaṅ karaip paṇpāṭum, caivaneṟiyum, payirttoḻil payiṟciyum, kaṭalnīril kalamōṭṭum aṟivum, vāṉanūl aṟivum, eḻuttaṟivum, aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇiyum marapum piṟavum cintu veḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ peṟṟiruntālum irumpu āyutaṅkaḷaiyō irumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyō peṟṟirukka villaiyeṉpatu akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa uyariya uṇmaiyākum. itil evarukkum karuttu vēṟṟumai illai. mēlum irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻum uṟuti ūkkam veṇkalap paṇpāṭṭil peṟa muṭiyātu.
teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi
teṉintiyāvil putiya kaṟkālattiṉ toṭakkattil irumpu ūḻi arumpiviṭṭatu. teṉintiya makkaḷ akkālattil cempaiyō veṇkalattaiyō aṟiyārkaḷ. vaṭaintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷiṭaittiliruntē cempu, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātupporuḷkaḷiṉ payaṉai aṟintu, avaṟṟaip pāṉai caṭṭikaḷākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttap piṟkālattil aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa uṇmai.
ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil atikamāṉa veṇkalap pāṉaikaḷō, veṇkalaccaṭṭikaḷō, veṇkalak kuṭaṅkaḷō, cempuk kuṭaṅkaḷō kāṇappaṭātiruppatu varalāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa uṇmaiyai uṟutippaṭuttuvatāka irukkiṟatu.
vaṭa intiyāviṭamiruntu teṉ intiyā cempu veṇkalam mutaliya tātupporuḷkaḷaiyum avaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟai kaḷaiyum aṟintiruntālum, teṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷāṉa tamiḻar iṉattavarkaḷ vaṭa intiyarkaḷaiviṭa ulakil uḷḷa piṟa enta iṉattavarkaḷaiyum viṭa cempu, veṇkalam, nākam, īyam pōṉṟa tātup porukkaḷai aṟintu avaṟṟait tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu varalāṟṟu uṇmaiyākum.
teṉ intiyāvil veṇkalam, cempu ākiya kālaṅkaḷ eḻāviṭiṉum, vaṭa intiyāvaiviṭap piṟkālattil ērāḷamāṉa aḷavil cempum veṇkalamum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. itaṟku evvitamāṉa ilakkiyaccāṉṟukaḷum tēvai illai. tamiḻnāṭṭil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varum cempu, veṇkalam poruḷkaḷē cāṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil ovvoru vīṭṭilum mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku kuṭaṅkaḷ tavaṟātu uḷḷaṉa. vayatu mutirnta peṇpāl oruvar iruntāl avaḷukku oru kuṭamum, avaḷ makaḷukku oru kuṭamum avaḷ marumakaḷukku oru kuṭamum avaḷuṭaiya ciṟu pēttikku oru ciṟu kuṭamum āka nāṉku kuṭamāvatu oru vīṭṭil irukkum. avaṟṟōṭu pittaḷaik kuṭamum, cempukkuṭamum, veḷḷikkuṭamum cila vīṭukaḷil irukkum. mūṉṟu nāṉku marumakaḷ vantāl cītaṉamāka mēṟ koṇṭum varum. ivvāṟē ovvoru vīṭṭukkum veṇkala viḷakkum, pittaḷai viḷakkum, kai viḷakkum, kuttu viḷakkum, nantā viḷakkum, toṅku viḷakkumākap pala viḷakkukaḷ irukkum. ivaiyaṉṟit tamiḻakattil ovvōr illattilum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu varum cempu, veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu uḷḷaṉa :
1. kiṇṇam, 2. aritaṭṭuk kuvaḷai, 3. periya kuvaḷai, 
4. tāmpāḷam, 5. pūccaṭṭi, 6. kuttuppōṇi, 7. kuṭam, 8. kopparai, 9. caṭṭi, 10. pāṉai, 11. tavalai, 12. pōṇiccaṭṭi, 13. keṇṭi, 
14. koṭṭukkaṭāvu, 15. vaṭṭil, 16. kumpā, 17. akappai, 18. caruvaccaṭṭi, 19. paṭikkam, 20. neyccaṭṭi, 21. veṇṇeyccaṭṭi, 22. pākku veṭṭi, 23. cuṇṇāmpuk karaṇṭakam, 24. veṟṟilai iṭikkum ural, 25. karaṇṭi, 26. curaṇṭi, 27. pittaḷaic cimiḻ, 28. pūmpaṭikkam, 29. eccil paṭikkam, 30. kai kaḻuvum taṭṭup pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa vakaiyāṉa cempu, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. cila vīṭukaḷil ovvoru poruḷilum 4, 5, 10 varai irukkum. ivaiyaṉṟip periya aḷavil veṇkalappāṉai, cempuppāṉai, pōṇic caṭṭi, aṇṭā, iṭṭaliccaṭṭi, vārppu eṉṉum peyaril palavita māṉa veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
cempu
ivai maṭṭumaṉṟip paḻaṅkālattil eḻutappeṟṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ceppēṭukaḷum nāṭṭil uḷa. ovvoru kōyililum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa viḷakkukaḷum camaippataṟkuriya veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷum, kuṭaṅkaḷum, cempu aṇṭākkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. 3 aṭi mutal 9 aṭi varai uyaramāṉa cempup paṭimaṅkaḷum, aimpoṉ paṭimaṅ kaḷum eṇṇaṟṟavai tamiḻakattil maṭṭumaṉṟik kēraḷam, āntiram mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. ivai pukalāka uḷḷē tuḷai uḷḷaṉavāka iruppatillai; kaṭṭiyāka irukkum. koṭi maraṅkaḷum, tūṇ viḷakkukaḷum, kōpuraṅkaḷ mītum vimāṉaṅkaḷ mītumuḷḷa kalayaṅkaḷum cempālāṉavai. poṟṟakaṭu pōrttiyō, poṉ mulām pūciyō irukkum. cempu, pittaḷai, veḷḷic capparaṅkaḷum, tērkaḷum, pallakkukaḷum pala kōyilkaḷil uṇṭu. cōṭaca pūcaikkuriya palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa pūcā viḷakkukaḷum pittaḷaiyilum veṇkalattilum uṇṭu. ivaiyaṉṟik karppūra viḷakkukaḷum, cāmpirāṇi pukaikkum tūpat taṭṭukaḷum, aṭukkut tīpaṅkaḷumāka āyirakkaṇakkil tamiḻnāṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil uṇṭu. aracikaḷum, celvac cīmāṭṭikaḷum cempu, pittaḷai aimpoṉ pōṉṟa ulōkaṅkaḷil taṅkaḷ uruvaṅkaḷai, viḷak kēnti niṟkum pāvaṉaiyil - pāvai viḷakkākac ceytu teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷiṉ muṉ vaikkac ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. avaṟṟil neyvārttu viḷakkerippataṟku māṉiyaṅkaḷ aḷittiruppataṟkup pala ceppēṭukaḷum kalveṭṭu kaḷum cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭum cumār 10 āyiram kōyilkaḷiruk kalām eṉṟāl kēraḷam, karṉāṭakam, āntiram pōṉṟa tirāviṭa nāṭṭu mānilaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil ettaṉai veṇkalac cempup poruḷkaḷ iṭam peṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu cintittup pārkka vēṇṭum.
tamiḻnāṭṭil putiya kaṟkālat toṭakkattilē irumpu ūḻi eḻuntuviṭṭatāl, palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa ulōkat toḻilkaḷ eḻuntuviṭṭaṉa. toḻil ceyyumtoḻilāḷarkaḷ, irumpuk kollarkaḷ, poṟkollarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, marat taccarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷukkuriya valuvāṉa kūriya kal uḷi, vāḷ, cuttiyal, vāycci, iṭukki, kuṟaṭu, poṭi veṭṭi, mara uḷi, iḻaip puḷi, mūlaccaṭṭam, cammaṭṭi pōṉṟavaikaḷaic ceytu kuvit tuḷḷārkaḷ. nāṭṭil irumpuvēlai ceyyum kollarkaḷ, poṟ kollarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, marattaccarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, tapatikaḷ pōṉṟa pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ perukiṉārkaḷ. ov voru toḻilukkum ovvoru iṉam ēṟpaṭṭatāl toḻilkaḷ ciṟappuṟṟeḻuntaṉa.
paṭimam vārkkum tapatikaḷ
teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa tapatikaḷukku īṭākak kōyil kaṭṭuvatilum, cilaikaḷ camaippatilum, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ vārppatilum tiṟamaiyāṉavarkaḷ ulakil eṅkumē illai eṉṟu kūṟalām. tamiḻ nāṭṭup pāvai viḷakkiṟku īṭāka intiyāvil eṅkum aḻaku vāynta viḷakkukaḷaip pārkka muṭiyātu. vaṅkan tanta tuṅkap periyār ō.ci.kaṅkūli, teṉ intiya viḷakkukaḷ5 eṉṟu 80 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻutiya kaṭṭurai oṉṟil kuṟippiṭ ṭiruppatai ulakam eṉṟum maṟappataṟkillai. tamiḻnāṭṭil ceyyappaṭṭa veṇkalap paṭimam - civapuram naṭarācar paṭimam oṉṟu amerikkāvil 75 laṭca rūpāy matippuḷḷatāyk karutap paṭuvatu tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpattiṉ perumaikku nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum.
ātitta nallūril akaḻ āyvu ceyyappaṭṭa iṭam iṭu kāṭāy iruntamaiyāl atikamāṉa irumpup poruḷkaḷaiyō veṇkalap poruḷkaḷaiyō nām kāṇa muṭiyavillai. tamiḻakat til uḷḷa araṇmaṉaikaḷaiyum, nakaraṅkaḷaiyum akaḻvāyvu ceyyum vāyppu nērntiruntāl, paṇṭaiya tamiḻaka kammiyar kaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa kaivaṇṇam vāynta - ciṟpa nuṭpam vāynta cempu, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷiṉ vakaikaḷaiyum, eṇṇikkai kaḷaiyum aḻakaiyum uṟutiyaiyum nām kāṇa muṭiyum.
paṇṭaikkālat teṉ intiya varalāṟṟiṉai, namatu āyvukkup poruttamāṉa kōṇattil maṭṭum nām paricōtaṉai ceytu varukiṟōm. perumpālum vaṇika nōkkattōṭu makkaḷiṉ pulam peyarvu kāraṇamāṉa neruṅkiya toṭarpukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu nampikkoṇṭiruppōr palar uḷar. teṉ intiyar, cempu, veṇkalam, īyam mutaliya tātupporuḷkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷa muṉvantu, vaṭa pakutiyil payaṉuḷḷa paṇikaḷil amarn tiruntaṉar. atēvaḻiyil cintu veḷiyil kuṭiyēṟiya palar tārāḷa mākap piṟkālattil irumpuk karuvikaḷaiyum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyum teṟkiṉiṉṟu vāṅki taṅkaḷiṉ tēvaikaḷaiyum avaciyattaiyum niṟaivu ceytirukkalām. itellām piṟkālat til eḻuntavai eṉpatil ciṟitum aiyamillai.
kālam
ātitta nallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mikap paḻamai vāyntaṉa aḻaku vāyntaṉa. cintu veḷiyil cempum maṇṉum kalanta oru vakai ulōka (cālkōlitikap) paṇpāṭṭil uruppeṟṟa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷaiviṭa irumpup paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭak kalai kālattāl mikattoṉmai vāyntatu eṉpatil evarukkum aiyappāṭu eḻamuṭiyātu. potu nōkkōṭu intiya varalāṟṟai āynta āciriyarkaḷ cempum maṇṇum kalanta oru vakai ulōka ūḻiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 4000 mutal ki.mu. 2750 varai irukkalām eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.3 teṉ intiya varalāṟṟaip potuvāka āynta āciriyarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 5000-m āṇṭil nikaḻntirukkalām eṉṟu tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaip paṭippil īṭupaṭṭa orucilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāl teṉ intiya varalāṟṟaip palvēṟu uyarnta nōkkōṭu āynta aṟiñarkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi ki.mu.8000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ nilavi irukkalām eṉṟu matippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
ātitta nallūr irumpu ūḻi, arappāviṉ cempu maṇṇum kalanta oruvakai ulōka (cālkōlitikap) paṇpāṭṭaiviṭa evvāṟṟāṉum paḻamai mikkatu - uyariyatu eṉpatil aiyamillai.
ātittanallūr paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kālattāl 3000 ām āṇṭiṟku muntiyavaiyāyiṉum cey nērtti yil arappā paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiviṭac ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, pūvēlaippāṭukaḷ atikam kāṇappaṭavillai. koṟkai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiviṭa aḻaku vāyntaṉa eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptu, kirīṭ, kirī mutaliya mēlai nāṭukaḷil kiṭaitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ēṟattāḻa arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai ottavaikaḷākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēlaippāṭukaḷai oppiṭṭup pārttāl arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭilum ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭilum arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, cumēriyā, mecapottāmiyā nāṭukaḷilum ēṉaiya piṟa nāṭukaḷilum naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻvu āyvil kaṇṭupiṭitta palvēṟu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu nalla orumaippāṭuṭaiyavai kaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu nām oru muṭiviṟku vara muṭikiṟatu - aḵtāvatu ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭai uruvākki atai vaḷartta ātittamiḻ makkaḷ uruvākkiya kalaikaḷum, kaip paṇikaḷum (Crafts) ayalnāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu paravic celvākkaip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; mēlum mikat tolaivil uḷḷa pala nāṭukaḷōṭum nēraṭiyākakkūṭa vaṇikat toṭarpum koṇ ṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatēyākum. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟum nāvāykaḷum, marakkalaṅkaḷum, vaṅkaṅkaḷum, tamiḻnāṭu ki.mu.4000 ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē kaṭalmūlam mēṟku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kappal vaṇikam naṭattivantatai naṉku cuṭṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.4
ātittanallūr irumpup paṇpāṭṭaik kaṇṭa ātit tamiḻarkaḷ, nilattiṉ mūlam poti māṭukaḷil viḷaiporuḷ kaḷaiyum, ceyporuḷkaḷaiyum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu cintu veḷiyiṉ vaḻiyākac cumēriyā, ellam ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam naṭattiyataṟkuk kaṭal vaṇikam evvitattilum taṭaiyāka irukkavillai - ātittanallūr paṇpāṭṭil uruppeṟṟa poruḷkaḷukkum ēṉaiya paḻaiya nāṭukaḷil ceyyappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷukkum iṭaiyē uḷḷa orumaippāṭṭaik kāṇpataṉ mūlam paṇpāṭṭiṉ kālattai uṟutippaṭuttuvatil nām uṉṉippāka irukkavēṇṭum. inta orumaippāṭukaḷ paḻamaic ciṉṉattaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvataṟkut tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar kuṟip piṭuvataiviṭa mika muntiya ki.mu. 3200il eḻunta kālattaik kuṟip piṭuvatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptiṉ kūrṅkōpurat tūṇkaḷiṉ kālattaiviṭap paḻamaiyāṉatāka illā viṭṭālum allatu kūr kōpurak kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatāka iruntālum, cintuveḷi nākarikam camakāla nikaḻcci eṉṟu icaivu koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. cintuveḷi muttiraikaḷ cārkōṉik kālattiṟku muṉ iruntaṉa eṉa uṟutiyākak kūṟuvataṟku viḷakkam tarukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ irumpup paṇpāṭu
nām inta attiyāyattai muṭikkumuṉ oru ceytiyaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭavēṇṭiya nilaiyil irukkiṉṟōm. ulakil periya nākarikamākap pōṟṟappaṭum cintuveḷi nākarikattil - irumpiṉ upayōkam oru ciṟitum illai eṉpatēyākum. teṉ intiyā viṟkum cintuveḷikkum iṭaiyē aṭikkaṭi pōkkuvarattukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, cempu veṇkala ūḻikku neṭuṅkālattiṟkup piṉṉarē irumpiṉ upayōkattaic cintuveḷi makkaḷ aṟintirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.5 teṉ intiyāviliruntu irumpup paṇpāṭu muṉ ceṉṟu, varukai terivikkumuṉ, intap paṇpāṭu oru cakāptamākak kāṭci taruvatu oru kāraṇamāka irukkiṟatu; allatu iṉṉum cariyākac colva tāṉāl intap paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu camakāla nikaḻcciyāka iruntiruk kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām. eṉavē, nām ēṉ cintu veḷip paṇpāṭṭiṉai mika uyarnta paḻamaic ciṉṉamākak kuṟippiṭa muṭiyātirukkiṟatu eṉpataṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, oru ciṟanta tolporuḷ āyvu vallunariṉ ariya karuttukkaḷai itaṉ aṭiyil kuṟippiṭṭu inta attiyāyattai muṭikkiṉṟōm! aḵtāvatu: oru karuttu mikatteḷivākat tavaṟiṉṟiyum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu; mokañcatārō arappā ākiya iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum inta nākarikam mutircciyaṭaiyāta nākarikamāka veḷippaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl oṉṟu, intiya maṇṇil māṟṟa muṭiyātatum ēṟkeṉavē paḻamai mikkatumāṉatu. ataṉ piṉṉaṇiyil pala nūṟāyirakkaṇakkāṉa, maṉitap paṇpukaḷaip peṟṟirukkiṟa muyaṟcikaḷ amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, intiyā itu mutaṟkoṇṭu pāracīkam, mecapottāmiyā, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaḷil maṉita camūkam vaḷarcciyum mutal nilaiyum peṟṟa camūkattiṉ ceyaṟ pāṅkuḷḷa - nākarikam vāynta - mika mukkiyamāṉa nāṭukaḷaip pōṉṟatāka aṅkīkarikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉpatai nām eṭuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum eṉpatākum.

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VI
1.	Bruce Foote; Indian Pre-Historic and Proto Historic Antiquities. p. 25.
	(a)	The Neolithic age in South India passed on to the Iron Age without any interval long or short. In fact, the last centuries of the Neolithic age witnessed the making of iron implements which the Neolithic people used on a large scale. The date of this transition or the length of its duration cannot be fixed, with any satisfaction, though one school of archaeologists contends that 5,000 B.C. may be roughly taken as the chronological limit for the beginning of the Iron age in South India” - Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 
1951. p. 95.
2.	Ancient History of the Near East by H.R. Hall.
3.	“Mohenjo-Daro and the Indus valley civilization by Sir John Marshall Vol. 1-3 (London) 1931.
4.	On the whole the state of civilization and culture by the finds of Mohenjo-Daro alone prove that from five thousand B.C. to two thousand B.C. the culture and civilization of Mesopotamia was in no way superior to that of India and Baluchistan. I have called this civilization Dravidian. Because the earliest example of its peculiar burial customs were discovered in Dravidian countries, but it is quite possible that the people who brought from another land were quite different from the modern inhabitant of Dravidian” - Dravidian Civilization - R.D.Banerji (an article) Modern Review - Calcutta 1927. p. 559.
5.	South Indian Lamps O.C. Gancoly (London) 1916.
6.	Pre-Historic South India by V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951. p. 124.
	“We have only examined such of the aspects as relavant to our study early the South Indian history. Believing as we do intimate contacts due to migration of people mostly for purposes of trade and commerce, the Indians came to contact with metals like copper and bronze, tin and lead which are usefully employed in the northern area. In the same way the inhabitants of the Indus - region freely borrowed iron tools and implements from the South to supplement their needs and wants” - Pre-historic South India by V.R.Rama-chandra Dikshtar M.A., (Madras) 1951.
 p. 126.

7. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ
maṭkalaṅkaḷ
paṇṭaikkāla, mutirāp paṇpuṭaiya mutumakkaḷ nākarika ulakil mutal aṭiyeṭuttu vaittatum avarkaḷ uruvākkiya kaiviṉaip poruḷkaḷ maṭkalaṉkaḷēyām. mutaṉ mutalākap paccai maṇṇāl pāṉai caṭṭikaḷ ceyyap payiṉṟaṉar. appāl anta maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāṇṭaṅkaḷaic cūḷaiyiliṭṭu, nāṉṟāka vēka vaittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cūḷaiyiliṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ maṇṇiṉ taṉmaik kēṟpa, cila civappākavum cila kaṟuppākavum iruntaṉa. ivai eḷitil uṭaiyātavaikaḷāy valuvāyntavai kaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa kalaṅkaḷ
ātitta nallūrp paṟampil aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil aṇipeṟat tikaḻum maṭkalaṅkaḷ 600kku mēl uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cumār 100kku mēṟpaṭṭavaikaḷai avar vakuttut tokuttuc ceṉṉai araciṉariṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟac ceytuḷḷār. avai iṉṟum nām kaṇṭukaḷittu āyvu ceyvataṟku ēṟṟavaikaḷāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ceṉṉai mānila araciṉ arumporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟṟirukkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil 110 kalaṉkaḷai niḻaṟpaṭam piṭittu iriyā avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya ātitta nallūrilum perumpāyūrilumiruntu kaṇṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa ariya nūlil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷār. (paṭam, 6,7,8, pārkka) paṭam 6il 41 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, paṭam 7il 39 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum paṭam 8il 30 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷumāka 110 kalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa aṭiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa peyarkaḷ uḷḷa kalaṉkaḷākum.
kalaṉkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ
1. kiṇṇam	2. pāṉai
3. caṭṭi	4. kuṇṭāppōṇi
5. pāṉaimūṭi	6. tāḻi
7. kutir	8. kalayam
9. maravai	10. cempu
11. pōṇi	12. kuṇṭā
13. kuṇṭāccempu	14. cāṭi
15. uruḷi	16. paḷḷai
17. kuṭuvai	18. citai
19. tamḷar vaṭivāṉa kiṇṇam	20. mūkkuk kiṇṇam 					(keṇṭi)
21. tālam	22. pāli
23. curaikkāy lōṭṭā	24. kuṭam
25. kūcā	26. tōṇṭi
27. tavalai	28. cantaṉappēḻai
29. akal	30. kuvaḷai
31. vāṇali	32. maiyak kiṇṇam
33. mukkālikkutil	34. koṭṭuk kaṭavai
35. eṇṇeyk kiṇṇam	36. tōcaikkal
37. paṇiyāraccaṭṭi	38. mukkuḷiccaṭṭi
39. curaikkāyp pōṇi	40. vaṭṭil
41. porikkaṉ caṭṭi	42. veṇṇeyccaṭṭi
43. tayirppāṉai 	44. kumpāccaṭṭi
45. aṇṭāc caṭṭi	46. pōṇiccaṭṭi
47. poṅkalpaṭippāṉai	48. cōṟṟuppāṉai
49. kuḻampuccaṭṭi	50. toṭṭi
irīyā avarkaḷ paṭam piṭittu namakkut tantuḷḷa 110 maṭ kalaṉkaḷil 50kkut tāṉ peyar kāṇa muṭintatu. maṟṟavaikaḷukkup peyar kāṇa muṭiyavillai. pala maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻak koḻintu pōṉavaikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nām peyar kuṟip piṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻakkil illātavaikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kaviṉuṟum kalaṅkaḷ
putiya kallūḻiyil, teṉ intiyāvil kalaṉkaḷ ākkum kalai eḻuntuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattil irumpukkāla ūḻiyil kalaṉkaḷ ākkum kalai, ataṉ ucca kaṭṭattai eṭṭiyuḷḷatu. cintuveḷi yil vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ uruvākkiya kalaṉkaḷai viṭa, tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kalaṉkaḷ toṉmaiyāṉavai, tiṇmaiyāṉavai, ciṟap pāṉavai. avai tamiḻar nākarikattiṟku ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nāḷaṭaivil tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṭpāṇṭak kalai vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. vaṭivanilai, vaṇṇap polivu, eḻiṟ ceṟivu eṉṟa mupperum paṇpukaḷiṉ uccap pāṅkāka uyarntu oḷirat toṭaṅ kiyatu. kalaṉkaḷukkuk kaviṉuruvam kāṇpatil intiyarkaḷ iṇai yaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya aḻaku vāynta maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. aḻakum, vaṇṇamum uṟuti yum vāynta maṭkalaṉkaḷ eṉpataṟku koṟkaiyil akaḻntukaṇṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷ nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ātitta nallūr, perumpāyūr, kōvai mutaliya ūrkaḷil kiṭaitta pāṉai caṭṭikaḷum, cāṭikaḷum vaṇṇamum tiṇṇamum, vaṉappum vāyntavaikaḷāl uḷḷattaik kavarvaṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ātitta nallūr, perumpāyūr, kōvai, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, pallāvaram iṉṉōraṉṉa eṇṇilā iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu kaṇṭa kalaṉkaḷum, tāḻikaḷum, uṭainta maṭpāṇṭa ōṭukaḷum ciṟappumikkaṉavāy matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ toṉmai, tiṇmai, vaṭivaḻaku, vaṇṇappolivu, ceyaṟ ceṟivu mutaliyaṉa pōṟṟaṟkuriyaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, ilaṇṭaṉ mānakariṉiṉṟu, 1858ām āṇṭil veḷivanta intiyak kalai, toḻiltuṟai itaḻ eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil, ceṉṉai ōviyak kallūrik kaṇkāṇippāḷar, tiru. eṭviṉ hōlṭar, ceṉṉai (mānila) maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟa talaippil oru ciṟappuk kaṭṭurai eḻutiyuḷḷār.2 atu mēṉāṭṭu makkaḷ paṇṭaiyat tamiḻmakkaḷ kaṇṭa maṭpāṇṭak kalaiyiṉ kaviṉuṟum ciṟappai naṉkaṟiyumāṟu amaintuḷḷatu.
kalaṉ ceyyum toḻil vaḷarcci
putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ, eṅkuḷḷavarāyiruntālum avarkaḷ tam vāḻkkaiyil oru putiya toḻilaik kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ eṉṟu evar kūṟiṉum atu kalaṉ ceyyum kaippaṇiyait tavirap piṟitoṉṟāka irukkamuṭiyātu. tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻnta putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷē, mutal varicaiyil, mutal kaittoḻilaik kaṇṭa maṉita iṉamāka matikkappaṭuvar. teṉ intiyāvē, niṉaivuk keṭṭāta kālattiliruntu kaittoḻilkaḷilum kalai vaḷarppi lum taṉic ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyum. teṉ intiyāvil iṉṟum poṉṟātu uyiruṭaṉ nilavum oru paḻaiya kaittoḻil pāṉai-caṭṭi ceyyum kaittoḻilē yākum. putiya kaṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭa palvēṟu vitamāṉa vaṭivamum vaṇṇamum vāynta maṭkalaṅkaḷ, aṉantappūr, karṉūl, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, putukkōṭṭai, tirucci, cēlam, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭavaikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ vāḻnta inta māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa toṉmaiyāṉa kuṭiyiruppukaḷil palavitamāṉa paḻaiya maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil cila, cātāraṇa māṉavaikaḷāyum, cila aḻaku vēlaippāṭukaḷaiyuṭaiyavai kaḷākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṟiñar puruṣ puṭ paḻaṅkālak kuṭimakkaḷ vāḻnta intap pakutikaḷil āyvu naṭatti mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṅkāla intap pakutikaḷil āyvu naṭatti mutaṉ mutalākap paḻaṅkāla maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittār. avar kaṇṭupiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cila cūḷaiyil iṭappaṭātavai. avai aṭuppilēṟṟic cōṟu, kuḻampu camaippataṟku ēṟṟaṉavāka illai. paccai maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷākum. pala kaṟuppāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ; ciṟappāka mēṟpakuti vēkātavai kaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. cila pāṉai caṭṭikaḷ ventum vēkāma lum iruntaṉa. appāṉai caṭṭikaḷil civappāṉa pakuti ventu iruntatu. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ toṭakka kālattil ceyta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ karaṭu muraṭākavum ventum vēkāmalum iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, vaṭivaṅkaḷum muḻu niṟaivākak kāṇap paṭavillai; kavarcciyāṉa tōṟṟamum aḷikkavillai. ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭeṭutta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ putiya kal ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil ceyyappaṭṭavaiyāka irunta pōtilum avai nalla aḻakuṭaiyaṉavākavum naṉṟāka ventaṉavākavum, muṟṟilum civappu vaṇṇam vāyntavaiyākavum eṇṇilā vakaikaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavākavum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa.3
ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, naṉṟāka ventu civappu, kaṟuppu vaṇṇaṅkaḷil aḻakum uṟutiyum uṭaiyaṉavāy irunta taṟkuk kāraṇam, avai irumpu ūḻiyilē eḻuntavai eṉpatē yākum. anta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai vaṉainta maṇṇīṭṭāḷar, nalla karuvikaḷaip peṟṟavarkaḷāyum paṭṭaṟivu vāyntavarkaḷākavum iruntatēyākum. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil vaṉaiyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷ ciṟappumikka vaikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.4
ātittanallūr akaḻvāyvu 110 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ naṭai peṟṟatu. aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya paṭṭiyal veḷi vantu aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlākiviṭṭatu. antap paṭṭiyal iṉṟuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷukkuk kiṭaippatu aritu. atu tolporuḷ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. āṉāl, ataippaṟṟiya āyvu nūl ituvarai evarum eḻuti veḷiyiṭātatāl, iṉṟuḷḷa pala aṟiñarkaḷukku ataṉ mukkiyattuvam teriyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu. aṇmaiyil veḷivanta māperum kalaikkaḷañciya akarāti (The Readers Digest Great encyclopaedic Dictionary) eṉṟa ciṟappumikka āṅkila nūlaippaṭittēṉ. atil, tolporuḷ āyvu (Archaeology) eṉṟa talaippil cila kuṟippukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. atil 1937il car mārṭṭimar uyilar avarkaḷāl akaḻ āyvu ceyyap peṟṟa, ki.mu. 27il nilaviya nākarikattaik kāṭṭum arikkamēṭu eṉṉum iṭattaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ataṟku eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nilaviya ātitta nallūr putaiporuḷ akaḻvukaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaram etuvum tarappaṭa villai. atai aṟintu mikavum varuntiṉēṉ.
ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya āyvu tamiḻ nāṭṭut tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaikkum varalāṟṟu āyvut tuṟaikkum mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatākum. ataṉuṭaiya meymmaiyāṉa, aḻikka muṭiyāta nilaikaḷaiyum, payaṉkaḷaiyum paṇpāṭukaḷaiyum oppiṭṭup pārppataṟkum, namatu nāṭṭiṟkum, piṟa nāṭukaḷukkumuḷḷa vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷaik kāṇpataṟkum, ataṉ kālaṅkaḷaik kaṇippataṟkum nayattaku nākarikattiṉ naṉiyuṟu nilaikaḷai nirṇayippataṟkum ōviyac ciṟappai uṇarvataṟkum kait toḻil vaḷattaik kāṭṭuvataṟkum ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷ potuvākat teṉ intiyāvil tuḷirttuḷḷaṉa putiya kallūḻiyaip paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ ākum. āṉāl, putukkallūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālattil arumpiya irumpu ūḻiyai aṭuttu eḻunta maṭpāṇṭattoḻil, vaḷarcci peṟṟuk kalainayan taḻuviyatāy vaḷarntatu. ataṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, cey nērttiyum kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavarvaṉavāyt tikaḻntaṉa.5
paṇṭaikkālattil vāḻnta paḻaṅkaṟkāla maṉitaṉ vilaṅkukaḷ pōl vāḻntāṉ. avaṉukku atikamāṉa tēvaip poruḷ kiṭai yātu. pacikku ētāvatu uṇavum, nīrvēṭkai tīrkkat taṇṇīrum vēṇṭum. iyaṟkaiyiṉ kōḷāṟukaḷāl cila kālaṅkaḷil uṇavum nīrum taṭṭuppaṭum. appoḻututāṉ avaṉ cintikkat talaippaṭu vāṉ. uṇavaittēṭi, kiṭaittatai uṇṭu niṟaivu peṟṟa maṉitaṉ appāl uṇavaic cēkarittu vaikkamuṟpaṭṭāṉ. putukkaṟkālat til avaṉ vāḻkkaiyil oru tiruppam ēṟpaṭṭatu. avaṉatu cintaṉai vaḷarattalaippaṭṭatu. kallālāṉa karuvikaḷaiyum villākiya paṭaikkaruviyaiyum payaṉpaṭutta muṉaintāṉ. nalla kallāyutaṅkaḷaik kāṇa, kuṭi peyarntu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kilōmīṭṭar tolaiviṟkum - ēṉ ataṟku appālum naṭantu pōkat talaippaṭṭāṉ. āṭumāṭukaḷaip paḻakka muṟpaṭṭāṉ. puṉceyttoḻilai vaḷarttāṉ. pālaikkaṟakkavum neymōr tayir ceyyavum payiṉṟāṉ.
appāl kāḷaimāṭukaḷaip paḻakki uḻuvataṟkum, potikaḷ cumakkavum payiṟṟiṉāṉ. payirttoḻilai vaḷarttāṉ. naṉceyp payirkaḷai vaḷarttu nalam peṟṟāṉ. tiṭīreṉṟu irumpu ūḻiyaik kaṇṭu ataṉāl ēṟṟam peṟṟāṉ.6
kalappaik koḻu, maṇveṭṭi, kaṭappāṟai, kōṭari, katti arivāḷ, paṉṉarivāḷ, toraṭṭi īṭṭi, eṟivēl, cūlam, muttalaivēl, paracu mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu vēḷāṇmai ceytu teyvampōl uyarntāṉ. viḷaiporuḷkaḷai malai yeṉak kuvittāṉ. vayiṟāra uṇṭāṉ; piṟamakkaḷukkum aḷittu akamakiḻntāṉ. ēṉaiya nilattiluḷḷa makkaḷukku vārivāri vaḻaṅki vaḷḷalāka uyarntāṉ. tamiḻakattil uṇavu illai eṉṉum pēccukku muṟṟuppuḷḷi vaittāṉ. uṇavukkuriya nel, pul, eḷ, koḷ maṭṭumaṉṟip paruttiyaiyum payiriṭṭup pañcai uṟpatti ceytāṉ. nūl nūṟkavum, necavu neyyavum vaḻikaṇṭāṉ. parutti āṭaiyaiyum, paṭṭāṭaiyum neytu araiyil aṇin tāṉ; uṭal mēlē āṭaiyaip pōrttiṉāṉ; talaiyil talaip pākai aṇintu perumitam eytiṉāṉ. kāṭṭait tirutti, vīṭṭaik kaṭṭi caṭṭipāṉaikaḷai vaittu uṇavu camaittāṉ. vīṭukaḷil kutirkaḷil uṇavukkuriya kūlap poruḷkaḷaiyum, kuṭaṅkaḷil taṇṇīraiyum piṭittu vaikkat terintāṉ. avaṉatu vaḷamvāynta vāḻviṟku ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷ tuṇaiyāka iruntaṉa.
cintuveḷi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ
1920ām āṇṭil arappāvum 1922ām āṇṭil mokañcatārōvum akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cintu veḷi akaḻvāyvu intiya maiya araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai iyakkunar taḷapati car. cāṉ avarkaḷ talaimaiyil toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. arappāvai mātō carūp vāṭ (M.S.Vats) avarkaḷum, mokañcatārōvai irākēl tā pāṉarji (R.D. Banerji) avarkaḷum akaḻvāyvu naṭattiṉar.
cintu veḷi akaḻvāyvil, pañcāpil uḷḷa arappā, cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa mokañcatārō nakaraṅkaḷ maṭṭumalla, iṉṉam 20kku mēṟpaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷ akaḻntu kāṇappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟil vīḻccikkālam ki.mu. 3500 āṇṭu mutal ki.mu. 2750 āṇṭukaḷ varai irukkalām eṉṟu kaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
iṅku naṭaipeṟṟatu, āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭu. intap paṇ pāṭṭil eḻuntatu uḻavar nākarikamākum. iṅkuk kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭa ulōkam cempum, veṇkalamumēyākum. iṅkuk kiṭait tuḷḷa talaiyāya poruḷkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum ōviya eḻuttuk kaḷaik koṇṭa muttiraikaḷumām. iṅkuk kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷai naṉku cōtittu, avai paluccitāṉattiluḷḷa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu oppuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu kāṇap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 1924ām āṇṭu mokañcatārōviṉ meyyuru mella ulakiṟkut teriyat toṭaṅkiyatu.
cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa mokañcatārō, arappā, kulli mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ātittanallūr maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. arappā, mokañcatārō, caṅkutārō, lokuñcutārō, urūpār, kul, nāl, amri kāḷipaṅkaṉ mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷ akaḻntu kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. cintuveḷi nākarikamē ulakil akaḻntu kaṇṭa nākarikaṅkaḷil periya nākarikamāka matikkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭanākarikam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatum ulakiṟku oru pēratircciyāy iruntatu. cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭai viṭac ciṟantatākavum ataiviṭap paḻamaiyāṉatākavum uḷḷa paṇpāṭāka viḷaṅkiya ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu ulakil kavaṉippāraṟṟuk kiṭantatu. āṉāl, mokañcatārō nakarai akaḻntukaṇṭa vaṅkap pēraṟiñar arappāviṟkum ātitta nallūrukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa toṭarkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, makkaḷiṉ kavaṉattai ātitta nallūr īrkkumpaṭi ceyya muyaṉṟuḷḷār.
maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ māṇpu
ātitta nallūril vāḻnta makkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. avarkaḷait tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ (Proto Tamils) eṉṟō, tamiḻarkaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil vāḻnta avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ (Pre-Tamilian) eṉṟō, kūṟa vēṇṭiya tillai vēṇṭumāṉāl tamiḻarkaḷait tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ (Pioneers of the dravidian race) eṉṟu kūṟalām. tamiḻarkaḷ, vāḻviṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum peṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta vakaivakai yāṉa maṭkalaṉkaḷum, tāḻikaḷum, veṇ kalappāttiraṅkaḷum, poṉṉaṇikalaṉkaḷum, irumpup poruḷkaḷum paḻaṅkālat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nākarikattaiyum kaittoḻil ciṟappaiyum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭu upayōkat tiṟkuriyaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷēyākum. eṉavē, avaṟṟaip paṟṟi iṅku āyvōm.
tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar, eṅku āyvait toṭaṅkiṉum aṅkuk kiṭaikkum pala tiṟappaṭṭa poruḷ kaḷukkuḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷukku oru ciṟappāṉa iṭam aḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum oru nakaram, allatu nāṭṭaiviṭṭu makkaḷ kuṭipeyarum poḻutu aṅku viṭṭuc cellum poruḷkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷēyākum. iṉṉum atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvaṉa. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟapiṉpum kāṇappaṭuvaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ uṭainta ōṭṭuc cillukaḷēyākum. avaṟṟaiyē avarkaḷ avviṭattai, viṭṭucceṉṟa paḻaṅkālamakkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kavaṉamāka eṭuttu āyvu ceykiṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ citaivukaḷum toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ nākarikattai uṇarttum aḻiyāccāṉṟukaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa maṇkaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭavaikaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. karical maṇṇaikkoṇṭu kalaṉkaḷaic ceytu cūḷaiyi liṭṭu vēkumpaṭi ceytāl kalaṉkaḷ kaṟuppu vaṇṇam uṭaiyavai kaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. mañcaḷ niṟamāṉa maṇṇum āṟṟu maṇalum kalantu ceyyappaṭṭa maṭkalaṉkaḷ cūḷaiyiliṭṭu ventatum civappu niṟamāka irukkum. iḵtaṉṟip palavaṟṟiṟku vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. cila maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaic cuṭuvataṟku muṉ ataṉ kaḻuttuppakutiyil vaṭṭamākac ciṟu kuccukaḷ allatu kampikaḷ koṇṭu pū vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceytu cūḷaiyiliṭṭu vēkavaittup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟārkaḷ. intap pū vēlaippāṭu kaḷil aḻakiya mayilum kuyilum, māṉum māṭum, palvēṟu vitamāṉa ilaikaḷum pūkkaḷum kāykaḷum kaṉikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītu merukiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. cila, mēlē kaṟuppu, nīlam, ūtā pōṉṟa vaṇṇaṅkaḷum, uṭpuṟam veḷḷai niṟamum tīṭṭappaṭṭavaikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil poṅkalpaṭi koṭuppataṟkup periya pāṉaikaḷ mītu veḷḷaic cuṇṇattiṉāl pūṅkoṭikaḷ tīṭṭi ataṉuḷ poṅkal viḻāviṟku uriya poruḷkaḷai vaittu vaḻaṅkuvatum uṇṭu.
maṭkalaṉkaḷ, avai ceyyappaṭṭakālam, makkaḷiṉ aṟivu, kaivaṇṇam, ceyvaṇṇam iṉṉōraṉṉa palavaṟṟai uṇarttu vaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya pala kāraṇaṅkaḷai eṇṇi ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvaic ceytavarkaḷ aṅkup pala iṭaṅkaḷait tōṇṭi, tāḻikaḷaiyum avaṟṟuḷ irunta kalaṉkaḷaiyum, piṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum oṉṟaiyum viṭātu cōtittu, cēkarittu, iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷaic cuttam ceytu arumporuḷ kāṭciccālaiyil iṭam peṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar.
palavakaip pāṇṭaṅkaḷ
ātittanallūrp paṟampil akaḻntu kaṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum āyntāl avai palatiṟappaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷ vāyntavaikaḷākak kāṇappaṭum. pala naṉṉilaiyil uḷḷaṉa. pala citaintum cīrkulaintum uṭaintum pōṉavaiyākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. koṟkaiyil aṇmaiyil naṭattappaṭṭa akaḻvāyvil uṭaiyāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaippatu aritāka iruntaṉa. āṉāl, aṭukkaṭukkāyp palvēṟuvakaiyāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ uṭaintupōṉa tuṇṭukaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭil maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu vaṇṇan tīṭṭiya maṭkalaṅkaḷ vaṇika muṟaiyil ayal nāṭukaḷukku aṉuppappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. eñciyavai aṅkut tīṇṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭantu maṟaintum uṭaintum pōyuḷḷaṉa. cempuk kālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi makkaḷukkup putaikkum vaḻakkam putitāka iruntatu. irumpuk kālattil vāḻnta teṉ intiya makkaḷ vakaivakaiyāṉa vaṇṇam vāynta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaic ceytuḷḷaṉar. avaikaḷai ērāḷa māka aṅkuḷḷa putaikuḻikaḷil vaittum putaittuḷḷaṉar. cintu veḷiyil cempu ūḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ irumpu ūḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭa ēṟṟamiku teṉṉintiya maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷukku īṭāka irukka muṭiyātu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟuvar.7 atu iṅku meyyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷukku iṇai yākap piṟa enta ūḻiyilum aḻakiya - nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ amainta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaik kāṇamuṭiyātu. maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷai vaṉaiyum vēṭkōvarkaḷiṉ iyantiraṅkaḷum tikirikaḷum, ūcikaḷum piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷum irumpiṉāl iruntatāl, avar kaḷāl vaṉaiyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ taṉic ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar ṭākṭar kōrṭaṉ cailṭu camūkap pariṇāma vaḷarcci eṉṟa nūlil virivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷār.8 ivar ātittanallūril arumpiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil tikaḻum ariya, nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭukaḷaip pārttuttāṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉārō eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. āṉāl avar ātitta nallūril irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷaip pārttatē illai.
aṟiñar ār.ṭi.pāṉarcci avarkaḷ, tirunelvēli māvaṭ ṭattil uḷḷa ātittanallūril ceyyappaṭṭa akaḻāyvukaḷum avaṟṟil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭam, teyva uruvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷum, cintu, pañcāp, paluccittāṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅ kaḷil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ uyariya paṇpāṭukaḷiṉ oṟṟumaiyai uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. avai vaṭa intiyāviṉiṉṟu teṉ intiyā varai caṅkilit toṭarpōl toṭarntu nilavukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.9

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VII
1.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallur and Perumbair - by A Rea (Madras) 1915 pp. 21-40 Plate VI-IX.
2.	Madras pottery-Edwin Holder (The journal of Indian Art and Industry (London) 1858.
3.	In the shevaroy hills in the district of Tinnevelly the ceded districts, Hyderabad and Baroda, we meet with plenty of neolithic pottery. Bruce Foot mentions 127 place (Including Adichanalloor) where he discovered Pre-historic pottery 56 places yielded neolithic types, two of the transitional formation between the Neolithic and iron Ages 60 Iron Age types and the rest specimens of the later Iron Age.
4.	We came upon another aspect of the ancient culture of the Iron age and this is the development of the Neolithic pottery (at Adicha-nalloor)...
	“The vessels of this period are not rough but have a better polish. Gradually also potter began to use varieties of colours. Though the necessary pigments for producing the greenish hue and blue and blue varieties are still later, we can credit the potter of the Iron Age with some varieties ranging from yellow brown and grey. This shows that the potter had considerably improved his knowledge of firing the vessels which was rather limited inscope and extend till then. The pottery produced in this age is therefore not dull in colour but lustrous enough. In addition to this the potter of the Iron Age made his ware more and more an object of beauty by artistically decorating some of his vessels. Originally continued to the decoration of the objects with the figures of leaves and flowers now we see more and more of human and animal figures” - Pre-Historic South India - V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951 pp. 107-108.
5.	Pre-Historic South India - V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A., (Madras) 1951 p. 107.
	“We come upon another aspect of the ancient culture of the iron age and this is the development of the Neolithic pottery. We must examine this question with some circumspection. No doubt the Neolithians knew the art of pottery and had to some extent a knowledge of the wheel which the potter skillfully used. But speaking generally about pottery it has been concluded that the vessels were more or less crude and presented a rough exterior. The majority of the vessels show that they were black in colour though red varieties were also known. But we come to the age of the iron culture we see a surprising varieties of vessels made of clay”.
6.	Ibid p. 97.
7.	The absence of painted pottery in the parts of India and in all other periods of Indian History is extormely significant. I probably indicate the first period after the immigration of the people into Baluchistan and Indus vally in which habit, manners and customs were almost the same with faience-using copper age people who had burial customs peculiar to themselves. They with the near approach of the iron age the use of painted pottery suddenly dies out the best forms of South Indian pottery though carefully wheel turned cannot compare with copper age product of the Indus valley and Baluchistan” -Dravidian Civilization Prof R.D. Banerji (Modern Review, Calcutta) Nov. 1927. p. 558
8.	Social evolution by Gordon Child (London) 1951.
9.	The Recent Discoveries in Sindh and Baluchistan prove that the cultural affinities of the Dravididan extended in an unbroken line from the Tinnevelly district in the extreme South of the Indian peninsula through Sindh and Baluchistan the island Bahrein in the Persian gulf South Persia, Mescpotamia into crete and some of the islands of the Eastern Mediterranean” - Dravidian Civilization by Prof.R.D.Banerji (Modern Review) Sep. 1927 p. 308.

8. tāḻikaḷ
(Earthernware Sarcophagi)
mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ
ātitta nallūrp paṟampil uḷḷa iṭukāṭṭil akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa piṇaṅkaḷai vaittup putaikkum tāḻikaḷ (Sarcophagi) ākum. ittāḻiyai, ulaka vaḻakkil nāṭṭu makkaḷ matamattaṉ tāḻi eṉpar. aṟiñarkaḷ atai mutu makkaḷ tāḻi eṉpar. mutumakkaḷ tāḻi eṉṉum coṟṟoṭar tiripuṟṟu matamattaṉ tāḻi āyiṟṟu. itaic cilar, piṇañ cuṭu cāmpaṟ koḷkalam (Urn) eṉṟa peyarāl aḻaittu varukiṉṟaṉar. iḵtaṉṟic cāṭi, kuṭuvai, maṇcavap peṭṭi eṉṟellām aḻaippatum uṇṭu. iṉṟu it tāḻi eṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭālum ātitta nallūrttāḻi eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. it tāḻi ātitta nallūriṉ ciṉṉamāka amaintuḷḷatu.
tāḻiyiṉ ciṟappu
tolporuḷāyvuttuṟaiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭil aimpatu iṭaṅkaḷil akaḻntu palatāḻikaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, ātitta nallūr akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷē ciṟappiṟkuriyavaikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭil uḷḷa putaikuḻikaḷ 4 aṭimutal 9 aṭi varai akalamum, 6 aṭi mutal 15 aṭi varai āḻamum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai akaḻntu naṭuvē 4 mutal 11 aṭivarai uyaramuḷḷa tāḻikaḷil iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalai vaittup putaittuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kuḻikaḷil nāṟpuṟamum peruṅkaṟkaḷai vaittuc cuvarpōl uyaramāka eḻuppi naṭuvē cuṭu maṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa tāḻiyil cavattiṉai vaittup putaittuḷḷaṉar. iv vaḻakkam tol paḻaṅkālattil nilaviyatu. ātittanallūril akaḻnteṭutta tāḻi kaḷil maṉita elumpukaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil oru cila elumpukaḷ maṭṭumē uḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷ uṭkārn tirukkum nilaiyil vaittu aṭakkam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tāḻi kaḷil (putai kalaṉkaḷil), uyir nītta makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷ vaikkap paṭuvataṟku muṉpāka uṭalil oruvakai maruntaip pūci uṭal aḻiyātu irukkac ceyyum muṟaikaḷai muṭitta piṉṉarē, tāḻiyai mūṭi, avaṟṟai eṭuttuc ceṉṟu kallaṟaikaḷil aṭakkam ceyvar. āṉāl, avvāṟu aḻiyātirukkum uṭal etuvum tamiḻakattil illai. ekiptil 4000 āṇṭukaḷākiyum aḻiyā tirukkum piṇaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṟkaḷai varicaiyāka oṉṟaṉmēl oṉṟāka aṭukki naṭuvil piṇaṅkaḷai vaittu mūṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iruntatu. anta ūḻiyaip peruṅkallaṟaip paṇpāṭṭu ūḻi (Magalithic age) eṉpar. teṉ intiyāvil varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil piṇakkuḻikaḷaic cuṟṟip peruṅ kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amaippataic ciṟanta vaḻakka mākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. intak kaṟkaṭṭup paṇpāṭu, palavakaip paṭum. āṉāl, teṉ intiyap piṇakkuḻikaḷil irumpup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa intiyap piṇak kuḻikaḷilum maiya nilak kaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷilumuḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum irumpu kāṇappaṭavillai. aitarāpāttilum maicūr mānilattilum cilavakaip peruṅkallaṟaip paṇpāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ekiptunāṭṭil tamiḻnāṭṭaip pōl, iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷukku maruntu pūci vaittu uḷḷaṉar. avai keṭātiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avar kaḷiṉ kurutit toṭarpuṭaiya tamiḻarkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷ aḻiyātu irukkak kaiyāṇṭa muṟaikaḷ veṟṟipeṟṟatākat teriyavillai.
ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭutta tāḻikaḷiṉ vāykaḷ akaṉ ṟuḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷmītu nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷmītu mūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tāḻikaḷiṉ uḷḷē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭaikkalaṅkaḷum veṇkalap pāttiraṅ kaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum avarkaḷuṭaṉ putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷil poṟpaṭṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟil vēl, īṭṭi, vāḷ, mūvilaivēl pōṉṟa irumpup paṭaikkalaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil piṇaṅkaḷai naṉṉīril kuḷippāṭṭip puttāṭai puṉaintu paṉṉīr teḷittuc cantaṉam pūci, naṟumaṇa mālaikaḷ cūṭṭi, nalla cāmpirāṇip pukaiyūṭṭi, pāṭaiyil ēṟṟicceṉṟu iṭukāṭṭil putaippatu iṉṟuvarai iruntuvarum vaḻakkam ākum. āṉāl, inta marapu vaḻakkam cila tamiḻ iṉattavarkaḷiṭam maṭṭumēyuḷḷatu. pala iṉattavarkaḷiṭam illai; kāraṇam etuvum namakkut teriyavillai.
tāḻi vakaikaḷ
iṟantu pōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷai vaittup putaitta tāḻikaḷ pala iṉṟu tamiḻakattil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ pala uḷa. avaṟṟil iru vēṟ ṟumaiyāṉa amaippukaḷ uḷa. oṉṟu vāyakaṉṟu aṭi kuvintu, nuṉi maṭṭum taṭṭaiyāy uḷḷatu. (paṭam eṇ 1). ivai ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa ēṉaiya maṭkalaṉkaḷōṭu oppiṭa muṭiyātaṉa. ataṉ ceynērtti ivaṟ ṟil pulappaṭavillai. ivai karaṭumura ṭākak kōra vaṭivil uḷḷaṉa. ivai iṟantāraip putaippataṟkeṉṟē taṉi muṟaiyil ceyyappaṭṭavaikaḷāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum it tāḻikaḷ ēṉaiya kalaṉkaḷaiviṭa mikak kaṉattavaiyākavum kutirkaḷ pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(paṭam 2) itu ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. itaṉuṭaiya tōṟṟam muṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟa eṇ 1il uḷḷa tāḻiyiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟu paṭṭatu. āṉāl, itaṉ tōṟṟamum vēlaippāṭum ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. oru vēḷai uyar nilaiyil uḷḷa aṟivarkaḷ, camaya kurukkaḷ, cāṉṟōr, aracar, atikārikaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ uṭal kaḷai vaittup putaikkum cāṭiyāka iruntālum irukkum. vāy ciṟitu, uṭal nīḷamāṉatu, kaḻuttu nīṇṭatu. puyam kaccitamāṉatu, aṭi ciṟuttup puyam varai akaṉṟuḷḷatu. eṇ oṉṟum, eṇ iraṇṭum aṭippakuti ciṟitu maṇṇiṟ kuḷ vaittuc ceṅkuttāka niṟkumpaṭi putaikkattakkaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa elumpukaḷai vaittup putaikkum putaikalaṉ vaṭṭamāṉa ciṟiya taṭṭaiyāṉa nuṉiyai yuṭaiyatu. aṭippākam vaṭṭamāṉa nilaiccaṭṭattōṭu amain tuḷḷatu. kaḻuttu ciṟuttu, tōḷ pākam akaṉṟu vāy ciṟuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ aṭippakuti vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa nilaic caṭṭattiṉ mītu, tāḻi cāyntu viṭātavāṟu poruttap peṟ ṟuḷḷatu. (paṭam 3) itu oru periya kūcā vaṭivil aḻakuṟac camaik kappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. mēṟpakuti, karaṭu muraṭāka aṉṟi vaḻuvaḻuppāka merukūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa pallāvarattil akaḻntu kaṇ ṭeṭukkappeṟṟatu. itaṉ amaippu, ātittanallūril akaḻntu, eṭukkap paṭṭa mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu muṟṟi lum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa perumpāyūrp pakutiyil pala putai kuḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu aṟiñar iriyā avarkaḷ cila putai kalaṉkaḷai eṭuttu, āyntuḷḷār. (paṭam 4) avai pallā varattiluḷḷa putaikalaṉkaḷiṉ vaṭi vattai ōraḷavu ottiruppiṉum aṭiyil amaintuḷḷa pakutiyum mūṉṟu kāl kaḷum uṭalum ātitta nallūrt tāḻi kaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉavāka viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. kaḻuttum vāyum pallāvaram putaikuḻikkalaṉai ottiruppiṉum ataippōṉṟu nīṇṭirāmal kuṭṭaiyāka uḷḷaṉa. ivai ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa mutumakkaḷiṉ tāḻikaḷ tōṉṟiya kālattiṟkup piṉ eḻuntavaiyāka eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa intiyāvil cempu ūḻiyil eḻunta putai kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭutta tāḻikaḷ oruvitamāka uḷḷaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻiyil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ vēṟu vita māka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ : itu tavaṟu. tirāviṭa nāka rikam eṅku nilavi iruppiṉum orē aṭippaṭaiyil amaintirukkiṟatu eṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmai yākum.
perumpāyūril aṟiñar alek cāntar irīyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭa putaikalaṉkaḷil (tāḻikaḷil) cila, ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu cila vēṟupāṭukaḷai uṭaiyaṉa. (paṭam 5 aip pārkka). aṭippaṭai oṉṟutāṉ eṉṟālum itu. paṭam 2aip pōṉṟu vāyum kaḻuttum, tōḷum aḻakāka amaintuḷḷatu. āṉāl, aṭip pākam maṭṭum māṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivai, nilattil tāḻi ceṅkuttāka niṟkattakkatāka aṭi akaṉṟu ciṟiya mūṉṟu kuṭṭaiyāṉa kāl kaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavākat tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. viḷakkamākak kūṟuva tāṉāl ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷtām ivaṟṟaiviṭa amaivuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intap perumpāyūr tāḻi ceṅkuttāka, niṟka vaittu iṟutic caṭaṅkukaḷ ceyvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu amain tuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟalām. mēlum perum pāyūril itaiviṭa akaṉṟu nīṇṭa periya kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya tāḻiyum (putaikalaṉum) oru kālaiyuṭaiya nilaip pēḻaimītu, uruḷai vaṭivamāṉa pāṉai pōṉṟu vaikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. āṉāl, ataṉ vāy ēṉaiya putaikalaṉkaḷiṉ vāykaḷai viṭa mikac ciṟiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muṉṉāḷil uḷḷa ceṉṉai mānilattiṉ malapār māvaṭṭattil kaṟuppuvaṇṇam vāynta oruvakait tāḻikaḷ akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivai ātitta nallūrt tāḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu atika vēṟṟumai yuṭaiyaṉavalla. āṉāl, ciṟiya kaḻuttum akalamāṉa aṭippakutiyum uḷḷaṉa. vaṇṇamum kaṟup pāka amaintuḷḷatu. kaḻuttu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭut tāḻikaḷaip pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭālum aṭippakuti akaṉṟu nilattil vaittāl ceṅkuttāy naṉku niṟpataṟku ēṟṟatāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uṭal arappāvil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷaippōl akaṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. it tāḻikaḷ mika uṟutiyākac ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷaip pōṉṟu pallāvaram, perumpāyūr, paḻaiyakāyal, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta tāḻikaḷ aṉaittum karuñcivappu vaṭivattil kāṇap paṭum poḻutu inta malapār māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tāḻikaḷ maṭṭum kaṟuppuvaṇṇam vāyntaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātittanallūrilē karumaivāynta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟālum, tāḻikaḷ kaṟuppu vaṇṇattil kāṇappaṭuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ātittanallūril kāṇappaṭṭa pala maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟākak kaṟutta nalla paḷapaḷappuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum nilaippēḻai mītu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pārppataṟku aḻakāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.
aṟiñar nallāyar irāparṭ kālṭuvel avarkaḷ paḻaiyakāyal pakkattil akaḻntukaṇṭa cāṭi eṉṟu collap paṭum putaikalaṉ oṉṟu 11 aṭi nīḷam iruntatu. itaṉai ēṟkaṉavē viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷ ḷōm. itaṉ uruvam tirunelvēli māvaṭṭat tiluḷḷa muṟkālap payirt toḻilāḷar illaṅ kaḷil nel, pul, eḷ, koḷ, ciṟupayaṟu, perum payaṟu pōṉṟa kūlapporuḷkaḷaip pātukāppāka vaikkappaṭum cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa periya kutirkaḷiṉ vaṭivil uḷḷatu. vāy caṟṟu akalamāyum uṭal nīḷamāyum uḷḷatu. kaḻuttu kaṭṭaiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ piṇaṅkaḷai niṉṟa nilaiyil vaittup putaikkakkūṭiyatu. itu paṭukkum nilaiyil uṭalai vaittuc cāṭi vāyilil mūṭiyiṭṭup putaikkum nalla kallaṟaip peṭṭi (Sarcopagi) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu vantatu eṉat terikiṟatu. itaṉāl, irumpu ūḻiyil, tamiḻakattil palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa putaikalaṉkaḷil vaittup piṇaṅkaḷaip putaittatākat terikiṟatu.
cintuveḷiyil cempu ūḻiyil eḻunta arappaṉ paṇpāṭum tamiḻakat til irumpu ūḻiyil taḷirtta ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭum ēṟattāḻac cama kālattil arumpiyatākac cilarāl karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. iru nākarikaṅ kaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōr kaḷaic cērnta āṟṟaṅkarai nākarikaṅ kaḷēyākum. iṉṉum teḷivākak kūṟuvatāṉāl civaṉai muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu, paṇṭutoṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vāḻum vēḷāḷar paṇpā ṭākum. inta arappaṉ paṇpāṭum ātittanallūr āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇ pāṭum mikat tolaivil vevvēṟu ūḻikaḷil eḻunta paṇpāṭāka iruppiṉum kālattāl iṉat tāl paṇpāl, camayattāl nākarikattāl mika neruṅkiya orumaippāṭu uṭaiyaṉavākum. ātitta nallūrukkum, cintu veḷiyil uḷḷa arappā (Hara-ppa)É‰F« cumār 2400 ki.mī. iṭaiveḷiyiruppiṉum, pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷ illāta kālattil aḵtāvatu 4000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉpē nāṭṭut toṭarpum iṉattoṭarpum paṇpāṭṭut toṭarpum camayattoṭarpum eḻun tuḷḷatu; neruṅkiya orumaippāṭu malarntuḷḷatu. arappaṉ paṇpāṭum ātitta nallūr tirāviṭappaṇpāṭum caṅkilit toṭarpōl amaintuḷḷatu eṉṟu ulakaṟiya uraittār mokañcatārō akaḻ āyvai muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattiya nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam vāynta pērāciriyar tiru. ār.ṭi. pāṉarci avarkaḷ ātittanallūr tirāviṭa nākarikattaip paṟṟi, car. cāṉ mārcal, muṉaivar i.cē.eyc. mekkē, em.e.em. vāṭcu, muṉaivar em.uyilar pōṉṟa pēraṟiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nūlkaḷil eṅkum kuṟippiṭātirukkum poḻutu tiru.ār.ṭi.pāṉarci mikattuṇivāka ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭṭaiyum naṉkaṟintu tirāviṭa intiyā viṉ talainakarāṉa arappā nākarikattaiyum teṉintiyāviṉ tamiḻar nākarikattiṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttakkāṭṭāka ilaṅkum mutu perum paṇpāṭākiya ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭai iṇaikkum nākarikattaiyum aṟintu eṭuttakkāṭṭiyataṟku nām viyappaṭai yātirukka muṭiyavillai.1 tamiḻarkaḷ enta nāṭṭilum, kaṇkāṇāt tīvukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi iruntālum, ettuṇai āṇṭiṟku muṉ kuṭipeyarntiruntālum, aṉṉiya iṉattavarkaḷōṭu kalantu pōyiruntālum evarum tamiḻarkaḷai eḷitil taṭam kaṇṭupiṭittu viṭa muṭiyum. avarkaḷiṉ camayam, teyvam, uṭai, naṭai, paṇpu, nākarikam, moḻi, marapu vaḻakkam ākiyavai māṟātu iruntu varuvatu avarkaḷai eḷitil kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku ētuvāy amaintuḷḷatu.
ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷum arappaṉ paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta tāḻikaḷum ottaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. arappāvil kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷ 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷaṉavākak kaḻuttiṉṟi vāy akaṉṟu nuṉi kūmpiyatākak kāṇappaṭuvatu viyappākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivai ātitta nallūrt tāḻikaḷiṉ piratipalippu eṉpatil ciṟitum aiyam illai. itaṉai viḷakka ār.ṭi. pāṉarci mīṇṭum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
orumaippāṭu
cempu ūḻimakkaḷ veṇkala ūḻimakkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅ kaḷai iṅkut teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. atōṭu mecapottāmiyā, maiya āciyā, ciṉṉa āciyā (Asia Minor) kiḻakku maiyanilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷiṉ tīvuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟappākak kiriṭ tīvukaḷilum uḷḷa veṇkala ūḻip paṇpāṭ ṭōṭu mikat teḷivāka iṇaintiruppatai mika virivāka vivarittuc cempu ūḻi, veṇkalaūḻi makkaḷiṉ eccamiccaṅkaḷ aṅku nilaviyaṉa eṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. mokañcatārō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa mūṭappaṭṭa kallaṟaip putaiyalkaḷum (Cist burials) cāṭipputaiyalkaḷum (Jar burials), kallaṟaikaḷilum, putaikalaṉkaḷilum tāḻikaḷilum vaittup putaikkum vaḻakkattai ottiruntatu. teṉ intiyā, teṉ kiḻakkup pakutikaḷōṭum mekrāṉ (Mekran) teṟkup pāracīkam mecapottāmiya, maiyanilakkaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷuṭaiya putaikkum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷōṭu mikavum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. paḵraiṉ tīvilum pāracīkattiluḷḷa puhair (Buhire) arukilum mātirip putaiyalkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
car. ulliyam auli (Sir William Ouseley) eṉṉum aṟiñarāl puhair aṇmaiyil uḷḷa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷa irukāṭukaḷil uḷḷa putaikuḻikaḷil maṉita elumpukaḷ cāṭikaḷi lum maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷilum vaittup putaikkappaṭṭiruntatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.3
maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷil vaittō, allatu ataiviṭac ciṟappākac ceyyappaṭṭa cāṭikaḷil vaittō cavaṅ kaḷaip putaikkum vaḻakkamāṉatu mika nākarikamāṉatu. inta nākarika māṉa ceyal ekiptu, mecapottāmiyā, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭu kaḷilum, cintuveḷippakutikaḷilum paraviyuḷḷatu. āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil camakālattil alla itaṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē vakaivakaiyāṉa tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷai vaittup putaikkum vaḻakkam nilaviyuḷḷatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu.
veḷināṭṭut tāḻikaḷ
mokañcatārōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa putai kalaṉkaḷ aṉaittum vaṭṭavaṭivilāṉa aṭippakutiyai uṭaiyavai. arappā vil akaḻntu kaṇṭa putaikalaṉkaḷākiya cāṭikaḷil oṉṟu nuṉi kūriyatāka uḷḷatu. puhairil kūṭa attakaiya cāṭikaḷ kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil pirāmaṇapāttilum ittakaiya cāṭikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟil oṉṟu intiyāvil uḷḷa arumporuṭ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.4 āṉāl, iṅku 1882 ām āṇṭil etuvum kāṇappaṭavillai.5 ciṟitu kālattiṟku muṉṉar pahraiṉ tīvil ceyyappeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa kallaṟaikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa cāṭi kaḷum, tāḻikaḷum kūriya aṭippakutiyai uṭaiyaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa.6 ṭirāy (Troy) nakaril cilaimāṉ (Schleman) avarkaḷ itē pōṉṟa tāḻikaḷ palavaṟṟai akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. putaikalaṉkaḷ (larnakes) mecapottāmiyāvilum, mokañcatārōvilum pirāmaṇapāttilum teṉṉintiyāvil palaviṭaṅkaḷilum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇṇāl ceyyappeṟṟa piṇappēḻai yil (Coffin-š) mika nuṭpamākac ceyyappeṟṟa putai viṉaiyil pāyiliṭṭu mūṭappaṭṭa elumpukaḷ iruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.7 kuḷippu-kuḻāyvaṭivāṉa (Bath-tube-shape) putaikalaṉ kaḷum (Larnakes) nippūr cāṭipputaiviṉaiyum nīṅkaḷ aṟintirup pīrkaḷ. āṉāl, kirīṭ tīvil, cāṭip putaiviṉaikaḷil mika nuṭpa māṉa, putaikalaṉkaḷum, ciṟitum peritumāṉa kallaṟaip putaikuḻi kaḷum (Cist graves), camāti maṇṭapaṅkaḷum, niṟaivuṭaiyaṉavāka, kālavaricai muṟaippaṭiyāṉa niyatiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa.8 aṅkup putaiviṉaikaḷ, pūccāṭikaḷ, cempu allatu pittaḷaittaṭṭu muṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, katti kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa vāy iruntaṉa. naṭumiṉkaṇ (Middle mincan) kālattil, kirīṭtīvil tāḻipputai viṉaikaḷ niṟaivāṉa paḻakkamāy nilaviyiruntaṉa. avaikaḷai nām ātittanallūril kāṇkiṟōm eṉṟa varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.9
tamiḻnāṭṭilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum piṇaṅkaḷaip putaikkum muṟaikaḷai iṅku virivāka eṭuttakkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm. vaṭa intiyā, pāracīkam, mecapottāmiyā, ciṉṉa āciyā, kirīṭ tīvu pōṉṟavaikaḷil kāṇum muṟaikaḷ, orē kāla veḷiyīṭṭut tokutiyil kāṇappaṭupavaikaḷāy uḷḷaṉa. aḵtu ucca nilaiyil viṭāt toṭarpuḷḷatu. iḵtaṉṟi intiya varalāṟṟaṟiñar cuṉiti kumār caṭṭarjiyiṉ intiyap pataṅkaḷāṉa. tamiḻ, tammil, (Damil) tirāviṭa eṉpavaikaḷum ulakiṉ mutal varalāṟṟāciriyarāṉa heraṭṭōṭṭa eṉpavar termilāy (Termilai) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭa atē poruḷilum, tirimamilai (Drimmilai) eṉṟu paḻaiya ilaiciyaṉ cācaṉaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭupavai eṉṟu ōr aṟiñar kalkattāviliruntu veḷivarum mārṭaṉ reviyū eṉṟa tiṅkaḷ itaḻil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatākum.10
tamiḻarkaḷmoḻiyālum, paṇpāṭṭālum, nākarikat tālum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷālum mikavum toṉmaiyum, ciṟappum, uṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum avarkaḷ kiṟittava ūḻi eḻuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē, ulakil palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi ariya toḻilkaḷaiyum uyariya camayattaiyum nalla paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum parappiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum iṅku naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
putai kuḻikaḷ
maṉitaṉ āti kālattil vilaṅkukaḷōṭu vilaṅkāy vāḻn tāṉ. avaṉ vilaṅkiṉum koṭiya uyiriṉamāy iḻi ceyalkaḷ aṉaittum peṟṟavaṉāy māṟiyatum uṇṭu. avaṉ ātiyil kāykaṉi, kiḻaṅku kīrai mutaliyavaikaḷait tiṉṟu uyir vāḻum pirāṇiyāyt tōṉṟiṉāṉ. appāl vilaṅkukaḷaiyum paṟavai kaḷaiyum koṉṟu tiṉṟu vāḻum vēṭar nilaiyai eytiṉāṉ. appāl avaṉ taṉṉaip pōṉṟa maṉita iṉattavarkaḷaik koṉṟu, utirattaik kuṭittu ūṉait tiṉṟu uḷam makiḻnta kataiyum ulakil uṇṭu. ittakaiya koṭiya pēy maṉitarkaḷ iṉṟum cila tīvukaḷil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. ittakaiya kuruti veṟi koṇṭa ūṉ cuvai kaṇṭa vilaṅku maṉitarkaḷai mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṉipāl (Cannibals) eṉṟu aḻaikkiṉṟaṉa. ittakaiya koṭiya maṉitap pēykaḷukku aṟivūṭṭi avarkaḷai maṉitarkaḷākkak karuti avarkaḷ vāḻum tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu camaya aṟivurai kūṟa muyaṉṟa veḷḷaik kiṟittavap pātirimār palar avarkaḷukku nalla uṇavāṉatākac cila kiṟittavarkaḷ pēciyataik kēṭṭirukkiṉṟōm; eḻutiyuḷḷataip paṭittirukkiṉṟōm. intiya nāṭṭil kūṭa, cila malaikaḷil vāḻum nākarikam aṟṟa makkaḷ, malai aṭivārattil uḷḷa ciṟṟūr kaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ pāl maṇam māṟāp pacciḷaṅ kuḻantaikaḷaik koṉṟu tiṉṟuḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷai nām ceytittāḷkaḷil paṭittatum uṇṭu. inta maṉita vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vayiṟē, makkaḷiṉ putai kuḻiyāy iruntatu eṉṟu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum putai kuḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai eḻutum āciriyarkaḷum vēṭikkaiyāyk kuṟippiṭum nilaiyil oru kālattil namatu camūkam iruntatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
paṇṭaikkāla maṉita iṉam tammōṭotta maṉitarkaḷ iṟantuviṭṭāl, anta uṭalai nāyum nariyum, kaḻukum tiṉṉumāṟu aṅkēyē pōṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟa kālamum uṇṭu. nīṇṭa kālattiṟkup piṉpē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalai, maṇṇiṟ kuḻitōṇṭip putaikkum vaḻakkattai maṉita camūkam peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. makkaḷ nākarikam peṟṟa kālattil piṇaṅkaḷai maṇṇāl ceyta putai kalaṉkaḷil vaittup putaikkum vaḻakkattaiyum cuṭṭerikkum paḻakkattaiyum peṟṟārkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. mikap piṟkālattilētāṉ piṇaṅkaḷaip peṭṭikaḷil vaittu maṇṇil putaikkum vaḻak kattaiyum kallaṟai kaṭṭi atil piṇap peṭṭikaḷai vaittu aṭakkam ceyyum muṟaikaḷaiyum maṉita camūkam aṭaintatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. iṉṟum pāracīka iṉattaic cērnta corāṭar camaya makkaḷ iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷai ōr iṭattil vaittu, avai aḻuki nāṟṟam eṭuttuk kaḻuku pōṉṟa paṟavaikaḷ tiṉṉum paṭi ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.
tāḻipputaiyalil tamiḻar nākarikam
paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻ iṉamum, avarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya uṟaviṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirāviṭa iṉamum patiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē, kal ūḻiyil vāḻntirukkumpoḻutē piṇaṅ kaḷaik kuḷippāṭṭi nallāṭai puṉaintu, naṟumaṇam ūṭṭi, aḻutu toḻutu, neyppantam kāṭṭi, oppāri pāṭi, māraṭittu, malar mālaikaḷ cūṭṭi, malarmālaikaḷ toṅkum capparattil vaittut tūkkicceṉṟu putaittum, tāḻikaḷil vaittup putaittum, kallaṟaikaṭṭi avaṟṟil vaittu mūṭiyum, vāḻntaṉar.
tamiḻiṉattār, putiya kaṟkālattilē iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷait tāḻikaḷil vaittup putaittu vantaṉar eṉpataṟkut teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa putai kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭutta putaikalaṉ kaḷē acaikka muṭiyāta uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. aṉaittiṉum putiya kaṟkālattiṉ toṭakkakāla irumpup paṇpāṭṭil eḻunta ātittanallūrt tāḻikaḷ ciṟanta cāṉṟu kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa makkaḷ iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷaip putaippatil nalla nākarika muṟaikaḷaip peṟṟiruntaṉar. itaṟkut teṉintiyā peṟṟirunta aintu vitamāṉa putaikkum muṟaikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷāl pārāṭṭat takkaṉavāy ēṟṟukkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. tāḻiyiliṭṭup putaikkum takaimai
piṇaṅkaḷait tāḻiyiliṭṭup putaikkum cīriya muṟai tamiḻakattil patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iruntu vanta muṟaiyākum. itaṉai ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻi kaḷum, ataṉ uḷḷirukkum elumpukaḷum, poṉpaṭṭaṅkaḷum, irumpuk karuvikaḷum, veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum, piṟavum uṟutip paṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. intap putaikalaṉkaḷ uṭal muḻuvatum uḷḷaṭaṅ kum muṟaiyil periyaṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa. āti kālattil piṇaṅkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. ātitta nallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷil oru maṉitaṉuṭaiya ellā elumpukaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟum evarum kuṟippiṭavillai. cila tāḻikaḷil maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷē kāṇappaṭavillai.
niṟka, tāḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷai vaittup putaikkum vaḻakkam caṅkakālattil iruntuvantatu. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattil tuñciyatai aṟintu aiyūrmuṭavaṉār pāṭiya pāṭalē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ uyir nīttatu aiyūrmuṭavaṉārkkup perum varuttattait tantatu. akkālattil iṟantavarkaḷai oru periya tāḻiyil vaittup putaippatu vaḻakkam. eṉavē pulavar aiyūrmuṭavaṉār maṉakkaṇṇiṟkuk kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pūta uṭampu pulaṉākātu, pukaḻuṭampu pulaṉāyiṟṟu. tāḻiyāṟ kavippatāyiṉ ap pukaḻuṭampaittāṉ kavikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. av vuṭampu nilaulaku muḻutum parantu vāṉaḷāva uyarntu tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṟkēṟṟa tāḻi vēṇṭiṉ kuyavaṉ inta nila ulakai āḻiyāka vum mērumalaiyai maṇtiraḷākavum koṇṭu periyatoru tāḻi ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. kuyavaṉai nōkki, ivvāṟu orutāḻi ceyya iyalumō eṉa oru pāṭṭaip pāṭiyuḷḷār.11
naṉantalai mūtūrk kalañcey kōvē
nilavarai cūṭṭiya nīṇeṭun tāṉaip
pulavar pukaḻnta poyyā nallicai
virikatir ñāyiṟu vicumpivarn taṉṉa
cēṇviḷaṅku ciṟappiṟ cempiyar marukaṉ
koṭi nuṭaṅku yāṉai neṭumā vaḷavaṉ
tēva rulaka meytiṉa ṉātaliṉ
aṉṉōṟ kavikkuṅ kaṇṇakaṉ ṟāḻi
vaṉaital vēṭṭaṉai yāyi ṉeṉaiyatūum
irunilan tikiriyāp perumalai
maṇṇā vaṉaita lollumō niṉakkē	(puṟam 228. 4-15.)
puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷa maṟṟoru pāṭṭil
curampala vanta emakkum aruḷi
viyaṉmala rakaṉpoḻil īmat tāḻi
akalitāka vaṉaimō	(puṟam 256-4-6)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 238, 364 ām pāṭalkaḷ tāḻiyaip paṟṟik kūṟuvataṉ mūlam caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa kālattilum tāḻiyil vaittup piṇaṅkaḷaip putaittu varuvatu tamiḻar marapu eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.13
2. kālkaḷ uḷḷa putaikalaṉkaḷ
kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya tāḻikaḷ ceṉṉaiyilē pala kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala elumpukaḷuṭaṉum piṟa poruḷ kaḷuṭaṉum kāṇappeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil peritum ciṟitum uḷḷaṉa. mikapperiyatu āṟuaṭi allatu ataṟku mēlum uḷḷatāy nīḷa vaṭṭavaṭivil uḷḷatu. mikac ciṟiyatu nāṉku kālkaḷōṭu nāṟ kōṇa vaṭivil uḷḷatu. ivaikaḷellām mūṭikaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa. nām mīṇṭum, mukkālum, nāṟkālum uṭaiya putaikalaṉkaḷum uḷa eṉṟu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭṭatāka vēṇṭiyatirukkiṟatu. ivai vaṭa āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭattiṟkē ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum.
3. akaḻnteṭutta kukaic camāti
kuṭakkallu allatu kuṭaikal, kallaṟaikaḷ namatu nāṭṭil cila uḷa. ivai vaṭṭavaṭimāṉa aṟaiyāka uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu nālu aṭi āḻamum āṟumutal 8 aṭi kuṟukkaḷavum uḷḷatāyp pāṟai yil ceṅkuttāyt tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mītu mūṭipōl maṟaikkum pāvukaṟkaḷumuḷa. intak kallaṟaip putaiyalkaḷ, tāḻipputaiyalkaḷ ākiyavaikaḷukkum kalmēṭai (Dolmens) kaḷukkum iṭaiyē toṭarpukaḷ kāṇamuṭiyum.
4. akaḻntu kaṇṭa ariya camāti aṟai
intac camātikaḷ malapāril uḷḷa tuḷai (Tholai) eṉpatōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa. intac ceṅkōṇa vaṭivil veṭṭappaṭṭa pāṟaiyil uḷḷa camātikaḷ, mēlē tiṟanta kūraiyaiyuṭaiyaṉa. mēṟpakuti katavukaḷ pōl mūṭak kūṭiyatu. intac camātikaḷ paṇṭaiya kirīṭṭil kūṭa iruntaṉa. kirīṭtīvil kaṟkaḷai aṭip paṭaiyāka vaittuc ceṅkalliṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷai yuṭaiya nākarikamāṉa camātikkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. kēraḷa nāṭṭiluḷḷa tirāviṭamakkaḷ toḷai (tuvāram) eṉappaṭum putai kuḻiyil iṟantupōṉa makkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷaiyiṭṭup putaikki ṟārkaḷ. ivai tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟu akaḻ āyvu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum kirēttar (cret) makkaḷiṉ miṉōṉ kālattuc camātikaḷaippōl uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa irumpu vāḷkaḷ, pūkkiṇṇaṅkaḷ, mutti raikaḷ pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kirēttar makkaḷ talaip pakutiyil iṟantu pōṉavarkaḷāl virumpap peṟṟa poruḷkaḷai vaittup putaittu vantaṉar.
5. kallaṟaip piṇakkuḻi
kallaṟaip putaiyalkaḷ : nilattai akaḻntu, aṭiyil vaṭṭa mākak kaṟkaḷai aṭukki vaittu naṭuvil piṇaṅkaḷai vaittup pala camayac caṭaṅkukaḷaic ceytu putaippatu tamiḻ marapu vaḻakkam. inta marapu vaḻakkam teṉ intiyāvaiviṭa takkāṇattil peritum paraviyuḷḷatu. aitarāpāttil uḷḷa maulā ali eṉṉum iṭattil uḷḷa putaikuḻiyil eḻil peṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. intap piṇakkuḻiyil mikapperiyatu orē nērattil irupatu pēr nērāka niṟkak kūṭiyatāy uyarntatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅku āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa kaccalakoṇṭāvil uḷḷa oru putaikuḻiyiṉ varaipaṭam tarappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kāṇka.
piṇaṅkaḷaip putaik kum muṟaiyil intiyā eṅkum orumaippāṭu nilaviyatākak kāṇamuṭiyavillai. āṉāl, cuṭumuṟai pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ki.pi. iraṇ ṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟ paṭṭatākak kūṟappaṭum aim peruṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉ ṟāṉa maṇimēkalaiyil piṇac caṭaṅku muṟai aintu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. anta aintil oṉṟu cuṭutal maṟṟoṉṟu piṇaṅ kaḷaik kaḻivu nilaṅkaḷil vīci viṭuvatākum. itu putaippati lum cuṭuvatilum aṭaṅkāta oru taṉimuṟaiyākum. itaṉai iṭuvōrmuṟai yeṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. inta iraṇṭu muṟaikaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷukkup putiya muṟaiyeṉṟu terikiṟatu.
āriyarkaḷ inta nāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ innāṭṭil kaṇṭa putiya muṟai cuṭutal eṉpatākum. āriyarkaḷ innāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ eḻunta muṟaikaḷ eñciya mūṉṟu muṟaikaḷum ākum. ivai teṉ intiyat tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya muṟaiyākum. āḻak kuḻiyil putaippōr, toṭukuḻiyil iṭuvōr cātāraṇamāka kaṟpalakaikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭa kallaṟaikaḷil putaippōr, kallaṟaikkuḷ tāḻikaḷil uṭalaivaittup putaippōr, tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tulakkamākak kūṟappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa. itu paḻaṅkālap putaiyal muṟaiyait toṭarntu kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkakāla nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟimukap paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa putiya muṟaikaḷ nilavum varai toṭarntu vantuḷḷatu eṉpataik kāṭṭukiṟatu.13
iṉṟu piṇaṅkaḷaic cuṭukāṭṭil koṇṭupōy viṟaku kaḷai aṭiyil aṭukkivaittu, mēlē cāṇa erumūṭṭaikaḷai aṭukki ataṉmītu piṇattai vaittu vaikkōlaip pōṭṭu maṇcāntai ataṉmēṟ pōṭṭu meḻuki, mēlum vaikkōlaip pōṭṭu maṇcāntāl pūcit talaippuṟam kālpuṟam iru vilāppuṟam mēṟpuṟam ākiyavaṟṟil tuḷaiyiṭṭu neruppai (koḷḷik kaṭṭaiyai) vaippar. ippaṭic ceyvatāl uḷḷē irukkum piṇamum kaṭṭaikaḷum erintu cāmpalāy viṭukiṉṟaṉa. elumpukaḷ maṭṭumē eñcikkiṭakkum. maṟunāḷ kāṭāṟṟa eṉṟa caṭaṅkum, patiṉāṟām nāḷ kal eṭuppu eṉṟa caṭaṅkum naṭai peṟum. iḵtu iṉṟum tamiḻarkaḷil maruta nilattavarkaḷāṉa vēḷāḷarkaḷiṭamum an nilattil tōṉṟiya toḻilāḷarkaḷāṉa taṭṭār (poṟ kollar) kollar, kaltaccar, marattaccar, kaṉṉār, eṇṇey vaṇikar, pālai nilamaṟavar, mullainila āyar pōṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṭamum vaḻakkil iruntu varukiṟatu.14

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
VIII
1.	“The recent discoveries in Sindhu and Baluchistan prove that he cutural affinities of the Dravidians extend in an unbroken line from the Tinnevelly District (Adiththanalloor) in the extreme of Indian Peninsula through Sindhu and Baluchistan the island Bahrein in the persian gulf, South Persia, Mesopotamia and crete and some of the islands of the Eastern Mediterranean” -Dra vidian Civilization - R.D. Banerji, Calcutta, 1927. (Modern Review) p 308.
2.	These Lanarkes were found just above what Myres calls the Double-urn interments, what Rea called Pyriform tombs and Evans calls jar Burials in Crete are the same as the kettle burials of Mesopotamia. In Southern India these jars covered with a terra -cottah lid and in some cases with a stone - In the Northern India two of them were found with stone covers at Mohenjodaro in 1922-23 and in the chotta Nagpur Districts they are invariably placed under larger or small stone covers.
	“They therefore form a distinct part of the system of jar burial of the copper age and along with them were found some beautiful bronze chariot wheels similar to the pottery wheels discovered at Harappa and Mohenjodaro” Dravidian Civilization by Prof. Ragal Doss Banerji M.A., (2 articles in Modern Review Calcutta) 1927, p.555.
3.	The remains of the people of the copper and bronze Ages described above are very definitely connected with the bronze Age culture of Mesopotamia, central Asia, Asia Minor and the lands of the Eastern mediterranean archipelago specially that of crete. The cist Burial and Jar Burials of Mohenjo-daro, Nal and Harappa connected as they are with the cists, Larnakes jars etc., of South India, towards the South - east, are also intimately connected with the Burial customs of Mekran South Persia, Mesopotamin and the Mediterraneen people. The nearest specimens are to be found in the Isand of Bahrain and neer Bushire, in Persia, Sir William Ousely discovered a prehistoric cemetery of this type near Bashire consisting of jars with pointed ends containing human bones and covered with a shallow earthern vessel - Indian Antiquary Vol. VIII p.166 (Modern Review Nov. 1927 - p. 556).
4.	Indian Antiquary Vol. IV 1930. (London) 1930 - p.13.
5.	Anderson catalogue and Hand Book; p. 393-462.
6.	Annual Report of the Archaelogical survey 1908-09 pp.60-78.
7.	Cambridge Ancient History (London) 1948-Vol.I p.377.
8.	Ibid p. 592.
9.	Ibid pp. 596-597.
10.	“Thus the Burial custom which we have found in all its different forms in Southern India and in isolated cases in Northern India, Persia, Mesopotamia, Asia Minor are to be found in a complete series in Crete. It is the continuety and final culmination which led me to accept the very brilliant suggestion of Dr.Suniti kumar Chatterji that the brilliant terms “Tamil, Damil, Dravida” were really the same as the Termlai of Herodotus and the ”Trimili” of old Lycian inscriptions. Now the Archacological evidence is much stronger” - “Modern Review” (Calcutta) December 1924 Vol.XLII.
11.	puṟam. 228 : 4-15.
12.	puṟam. 256 : 4-6.
13.	Attention should consentrated on a view that the cremation was primarily a vedic rite but even after it was introduced into Southern India, the other methods were also practised. Another method of exposure of the body has thus been commended upon Atharvana Samhitha XVIII, 2-34 mentions among the pitrs invoked in the Pindapitry Ajina (offering of balls of rice to the Manes) the paroptas those abandened in distant places and the liddhaitas (those exposed on elevated localities) Exposure of the dead persist to-day in a modified form among the Tebateans and parsis-Yuan Chwang, the chinese Pilgrime mentions this as one of the three recognizied methods of disposal of the dead in India.
	The Editor of Puducottah Manual says that the period to which any of the burials under consideration belongs can only be determined by the nature of the associated finds. In cases where only Neoliteic implements are found without any iron or bronze belong the prehistoric Age. Where urn and megalithic burials contain an abundance of iron implements and vessels and no neoliths, these may be said to belong at the earliest to the Iron Age that succeeded the Neolithic Age and continued into historic times. See my paper on the disposal of the dead in S. India in the proceedings of Tenth All India Oriental Conference pp. 530-533 (Origin and spread of the Tamils - Prof. V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar, M.A., (Madras) 1971, (Notes to lecture I p. 86 : 31).
