9. makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ
āyvut toṭakkam
1876ām āṇṭum atai aṭuttu, 1903 ām āṇṭum iṟuti yāka 1905ām āṇṭu ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil ṭākṭar cākōr, lāppikyū, iriyā pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭukaḷ palavaṟṟai kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷaṉar. appāl avai talaiyōṭṭu āyvuttuṟai vallunarkaḷiṉ āyviṟku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷiṟ pala tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu ātirēliyap  paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭṭaip pōṉ ṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
1962ām āṇṭil varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakāla maṉitaṉiṉ, makkippōṉa maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷum elumpukaḷum pilippaiṉaic cērnta pālavāṉ tīvil oru kukaiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu pilippaiṉ tēcīyat tolporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil (Museum) cērkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
10 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa paḻaṅkālattil pōrṉiyō allatu malēciyat tīvukaḷiliruntu vanta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ cila pilippaiṉil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatākap pilippaiṉ arumporuṭkāṭciccālait talaivar ṭākṭar irāparṭapāk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
paṇṭaik kālattil vāḻntu vanta makkaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷ cila cīṉā, intōṉēciyā pōṉṟa āciyāk kaṇṭattil ēṟkeṉavē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippoḻutu cappāṉi lum cila paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
itait toṭarntu pala nāṭukaḷil paḻaṅkālattil vāḻn tiṟanta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭut talai yōṭṭārāycci vallunarkaḷāl (Craniologists) toṭarntu āyvu  ceyyappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.1 iṉṟu pala nāṭukaḷilum akaḻ āyvu ceyyappeṟṟu maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭu talaiyōṭ ṭāyvu vallunarkaḷāl āyntu pala viyakkattaku uṇmaikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟu ulakiṟku veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala nāṭukaḷilum pala tīvukaḷilum mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatākap paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pala nāḷ itaḻkaḷil ceytikaḷ veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. aṭikkaṭi nūlkaḷilum pattirikaikaḷilum talaiyōṭu kaḷum ataip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum veḷi varuvatai aṟinta aṟiñar oruvar itu talaiyōṭṭuk kālam (age of craniology) eṉṟār. atu poruttamāṉa ceytiyēyākum.
ātittanallūril akaḻntu eṭutta talaiyōṭukaḷ cila ceṉṉai arumporuḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil (musuem) naṉṟākap pātukāttu vaikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. V‹?- avaikaḷil cila iṅkilāntiṟkāka āyviṉ poruṭṭuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭum tirumpi vantu cērntuḷḷaṉa. arum poruḷ kāṭcic cālaiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa talaiyōṭukaḷ aḷavu aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
	talaiyōṭṭiṉ	nīḷam	akalam	perukka 
		ce.mī.	ce.mī	aṭukkuk kuṟi
	1.	18.8	12.4	66.0
	2.	19.1	12.7	66.5
	3.	18.3	12.4	67.8
	4.	18	12.2	67.8
	5.	18	12.8	77.1
	6.	16.8	12.1	78.0
ivai tiru. a. iriyā avarkaḷ eḻutiya ātittanallūrilum perumpāyūrilumiruntu kiṭaitta varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku mukkiya paḻam poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṟa nūliṉ muṉṉuraiyil viḷakkantarap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
talaiyōṭṭu āyvu
1900ām āṇṭu alekcāntar iriyā avarkaḷ, ceṉṉai mānilat tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉ ātaravil ātitta nallūrp paṟampil āyvu naṭattiṉār. avaratu āyvil kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa talaiyōṭukaḷ ākum.
1909ām āṇṭu ceṉṉait tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyaic cērnta aṟiñar tarṭaṉiṭam ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kiṭaitta talaiyōṭukaḷ oppuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. avar āyntu veḷiyiṭṭa teṉ intiyak kulaṅkaḷum nākarika maṟṟa iṉak kuḻukkaḷum (Castes and tribes of Southern India) eṉṟa nūliṉ mutal tokutiyil oru viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār. atil avar, iraṇṭu talaiyōṭukaḷ eṉatu karuttai īrttuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu maiyanilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷil (Mediterranean) uḷḷa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ pōṉṟatu - atu tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭu, maṟṟatu ātirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āṉāl, ciṟappumikka ātitta nallūr iṭukāṭṭu āyvil kaṇṭeṭutta talaiyōṭukaḷ paṟṟi, avaratu taṉi nūlkaḷ varalām eṉṟu ulakam etirpārttatu; vara villai. 1903-1904ām āṇṭukaḷil pireñcu nāṭṭaic cērnta pēraṟiñar, em. lūyi lāppikyū, mīṇṭum ōr akaḻ āyvu naṭatti ṉār allavā? ataṉ payaṉāka mēlum pala ariya poruḷkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟai avar naṉku āyntu ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil akappaṭṭa aṉaittup poruḷkaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkku uriyaṉa eṉṟu ōr uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu koṇṭār.
1899ām āṇṭu mutal 1905ām āṇṭuvarai ippakuti
kaḷai alekcāntar iriyā avarkaḷ akaḻntu cumār 2000kku mēṟpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai eṭuttu mika nuṇukkamākat turuvit turuvi āyvu naṭattiṉār. avaratu āyvil inta iṭukāṭu ōr arumperum paḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiya uyariya karuvūlam eṉpatai naṉku kaṇṭār. iṅkuk kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷ muḻuvatum kiṟittava ūḻi toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭavaikaḷāy irukkavēṇṭum. avai pāṇṭiyaṉ kālat toṭakkattai uṭaiyavaikaḷāy kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum karutiṉār. ṭākṭar ci. maklīṉ (Dr.C.Maclean), ātittanallūr, iṭukāṭṭil akaḻntu kaṇṭa tāḻikaḷum ataṉuḷ irunta poruḷ kaḷum paḻantirāviṭarkaḷ kālattil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām. allatu avarkaḷ kālattiṟkum muṉṉuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉār. pērāciriyar. ji.eliyaṭ mit (Prof. G. Elliot smith) eṉpavar, alekcāntar iriyā intap paḻamporuḷkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟiyatu, avaratu contak karuttukkaḷ eṉṟum atu naṉṟāka alaci āyvu ceyyātu koṇṭa muṭivu eṉṟum karutip piṉvarum karuttuk kaḷaik kūṟiṉār. nammiṭam maṟukka muṭiyāta cāṉṟukaḷ illai; āṉāl, kālattaip paṟṟi cūḻnilaikkēṟṟa poruttaṅkāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ tām uḷa. avaikaḷaip palvēṟu vakaiyākac ceyal paṭuttik kāṭṭamuṭiyum. uttēcamāka ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ kālam 400 āṇṭu mutal 4000-m āṇṭu varai irukkalām. evarum itait tavaṟu eṉṟu eṇpikkavō allatu vaṟpuṟuttavō muṭiyātu. āṉāl, inta iṭam paḻam pāṇṭiyar kālattaic cērntatākak karutappaṭattakka cāttiyakkūṟu kaḷai uṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu kuṟaintatu, kiṟittava ūḻi toṭaṅkuvataṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uḷḷatāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutiṉār.2
pērāciriyar ji. eliyaṭ mit avarkaḷukku aṉuppap peṟṟa iraṇṭu talaiyōṭukaḷaiyum avar naṉku āyntu avaratu ciṟappumikka, maṉitaṉuṭaiya pariṇāmattaip paṟṟiya kaṭṭuraikaḷ (1927) eṉṟa nūliṉ iraṇṭāvatu patippil oru viḷakkam tantuḷ ḷār.3 atil ātittanallūril kaṇṭeṭutta talaiyōṭukaḷ iraṇṭum, vevvēṟu maṉita iṉattaic cērnta talaiyōṭukaḷai ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu, ātirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ ātimuṉṉōrkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭṭai yottirukkiṟatu. maṟṟatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa maiya nilakkaṭaṟkaraip pakutiyil vāḻnta paḻaiya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭṭai yottirukkiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. aṭikkuṟippil maiyanilak kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti (Mediterranean) makkaḷ iṉam eṉpataṟkut tirāviṭa iṉam eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār.4
inta irumaṇṭaiyōṭukaḷum vevvēṟu iṉattiṟkuriya talaiyōṭukaḷāka iruntālum avai iraṇṭum orē kulattil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷiṉ talaiyōṭukaḷ allaveṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉṟāl ataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa vētakāla nūlkaḷil niṣātar, kirātar pōṉṟa āriyarkaḷallāta makkaḷ intiyāvil vāḻntaṉar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatiliruntu aṟintu koḷḷalām.
iṉappirivu
intiya makkaḷiṭam iṉṟum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai im makkaḷiṉam āṟu periya iṉattokutikaḷiṉiṉṟum avaṟṟiṉ uṭpirivukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantavaikaḷākum. āṟu periya iṉattokutikaḷ eṉpatu aṭiyil varumāṟu :
(1) nekkiriṭṭō (2) āti ātirālāyṭu (3) maṅkōlāyṭu (4) maiya nilakkaṭaṟkarai iṉam (ataṉ uṭpirivāṉa ātimaiya nilakkaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷiluḷḷa iṉamum kīḻnāṭṭu iṉamum) (5) akaṉṟa maṇṭaiyōṭṭu iṉamum (ataṉ uṭpirivukaḷāṉa tiṉārik karmiṉāyṭup pirivukaḷum nārṭik iṉamum) ākum.
nekriṭṭō āti ātirālāyṭu, maṅkōlāyṭu mutaliya iṉaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ uṭpirivukaḷum intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātik kuṭimakkaḷēyāvar. nekriṭṭōkkaḷ kuḷḷa vaṭivamum ciṟiya talaiyum, veḷippuṟam taḷḷiya neṟṟiyum vaḷarcciyuṟāta mukavāyum pammeṉṟa talai mayirum uṭaiyavarkaḷ. intat tōṟṟam intiyāviluḷḷa kāṭarkaḷ, iruḷarkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ātirālāyṭu iṉavakai mēṟkūṟiya nekriṭṭōk kaḷaip peritum ottirukkiṟatu. ivarkaḷuṭaiya uṭal uṟuppukkaḷ naṉku vaḷarcciyuṟṟirukkum. talaimayir pammeṉṟu curuṇṭirāmal vaḷaintu curuṇṭuḷḷatu. teṉ intiyāvilum maiya intiyāvi lum uḷḷa ātik kuṭimakkaḷuḷ perumpāṉmaiyōr ivvakai yiṉarēyāvar. mikattoṉmaiyāṉa kālattilē, ātirālāyṭu makkaḷum, maiya nilak kaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷum oṉṟittu vāḻntu utirak kalappu uṭaiyavarkaḷāyviṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu ātirālāyṭu makkaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷum kalan tuḷḷaṉar eṉpataṟkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṟiñar, akli (Huxly) eṉpār muṟkālattilēyē āti rēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷōṭu uṟavu koṇṭirun taṉar eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkuriya cāttiyak kūṟukaḷ uḷaveṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar maṉita iṉavakai vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum, toṭarpu kaḷaiyum, paṇpāṭukaḷaiyum paṟṟiya āyviyal muṟaikaḷum payaṉkaḷum (The Methods and Results of Ethnology) eṉṟu 1865 ām āṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭa kaṭṭurai oṉṟil, maṉita iṉaṅkaḷiṉ mikac ciṟanta - avaratu vakaippaṭuttum muṟai, aṟimukap paṭuttappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. atil avar, ātirēliyarkaḷ oru kalappaṟṟa iṉam pōṉṟatākak kūṟuvatu, teṟku intutāṉiṉ (Hindustan) tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ kavaṉattai īrttuḷḷatu. eṉavē, avarkaḷ āttirēli yarkaḷiṭattil uṭal cārnta nilaiyilum kuṟaiyā aḷavil, nila iyal cārnta nilaiyilum nammaip piṉṉōkkic cella vaḻi kāṭṭi viṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatai viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avar piṉṉar 1869 ām āṇṭil eḻutiya kaṭṭurai oṉṟil, intiyap putirkaḷai mika viḷakkamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. atil takkāṇattu makkaḷaip paṟṟi viḷakkik kāṭṭum poḻutu, avarkaḷ nīṇṭa talaiyum karumaiyāṉa tōlum kaṉṉaṅkariya viḻi kaḷum, kaṟuttu neḷivāṉa talai mayirum uḷḷavarkaḷ. evvitamāṉa viruppamum iṉṟit talaimayirai maḻittuk koḷvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, tirāviṭarkaḷ, mutirāp paṇpuṭaiya mutumakkaḷāy vāḻnta kālattil pēcappeṟṟa moḻiyaip pēci vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ iṉṉum intutāṉattiṉ vaṭamēṟku, vaṭa kiḻakkup pakutikaḷil paḻantirāviṭa iṉattavarkaḷ eṉṟu collat takka muṟaikaḷil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. mēlum avar 1870 ām āṇṭil, nila iyaliṉ pirippēṭṭil maṉita iṉattiṉ mukkiyamāṉa tiruntiya vaṭivam (The Geographical Distribution of the chief Modification of mankind) eṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyil entap putiya cāṉṟukaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭātu tammuṭaiya paḻaiya karuttukkaḷai mīṇṭum valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār. atōṭu avaratu kaṭṭuraiyil, paṇṭaikkāla ekiptiyarkaḷ kūṭa ātiralāyṭu iṉattavarkaḷiṉ kalappuṭaiyavarkaḷē yākum eṉṟu melaṉō koray (Melonochroi) eṉṟa kaṟuppu airōpya iṉamum, ācuttira lāyṭu, cāntōkurōṭ (Australoid - xanthochrod) nōrṭi (Nordi) kalappil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē eṉṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. aṟiñar akliyiṉ kūṟṟuppaṭi āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭimakka ḷum, tirāviṭarkaḷum mikap paḻamai vāynta puṉitamāṉa makkaḷiṉ cantatikaḷēyāvar. avarkaḷiṉ mayil vaṇṇa niṟattaiyum, uṭal uyarvaḷavu pōṉṟavaikaḷil mēleḻunta vāriyāṉa oppuṭaimai kaḷaiyum kavaṉikkumpaṭi kūṟiyuḷḷār. āttiralāyṭukaḷukkum tirāviṭarkaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa tōṟṟa orumaippāṭukaḷ paṟṟi akliyiṉ karuttukkaḷ nalla poruḷ potinta naṉṉōkku ēṟpuṭaiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
aṟiñar ṭōppiṉārṭu (Topinard) 1894 ām āṇṭil āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ, nīkkirōvarkaḷum melliya mayirkaḷuṭaiya, āṭṭōcṉōvā (Autochtonoes) iṉamum kalanta ōr iṉam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. akli, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa nīkrōvarkaḷuṭaṉ teṉṉintiyarkaḷāṉa kākkēciya melaṉōkurā (Caucasian Melanochro) maiyanilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷ iṉamum kalantuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. aṟiñar āṭṭaṉ (Hotton) teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻnta tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷil āttirēliya iṉamum piṟa iṉamum kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. itaip paṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ palappala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi makkaḷaik kuḻappiviṭṭālum uṟutiyāka ātittanallūril kiṭaitta iraṇṭu maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ eṉpatil aiyam eḻak kāraṇam etuvum illai.
aṟiñar cakkarmēṉ ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa talaiyōṭukaḷil eṇ 1. teṉṉintiyāvil toṉṟu toṭṭu vāḻntuvarum paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ iṉṟaiya ātirēliyat tiṇaip piṟappurimai yāḷarkaḷiṉ paṇpottavarkaḷ. eṉavē aṟiñar akli ācuttirēliyar - tirāviṭar iṉa uṟavaip paṟṟi aḷitta poruḷ viḷakkam ēṟpuṭaiyatākum. eṉpatu aṟiñar palariṉ muṭipāka irukkiṟatu. atu iṉṟu teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil vāḻum vēṭar cakkāy (Sakai) pōṉṟavarkaḷaiyum, āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakkik koṇ ṭirukkum tirāviṭarkaḷukku muṉṉiruntatākak karutappaṭum ōr iṉattavar iruntaṉar eṉpatu oru kōṭpāṭu; antak kōṭpāṭṭai itu uṟutippaṭuttuvatāka irukkiṟatu. talaiyōṭṭiṉ cāṉṟu māṟṟuvakaiyāṉa karuttukkaḷ etaiyum ātarikkavillai. ippoḻutuḷḷa āttirēliyak kuṭimakkaḷ ākkiramippāḷarka ḷāṉa intiyarkaḷukkum ācuttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷukkum iṭaiyē eḻunta kalappiṉ viḷaivākavē irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu muṭivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār.5 mīṇṭum mīṇṭum pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭi avaratu karuttu valiyuṟuttappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṭṭil teṉ intiyāvē
talaiyōṭukaḷai maṭṭum vaittu iṉavēṟupāṭukaḷaip paṟṟi iṟutiyāṉa muṭipu kāṇpatu aritu. talaiyōṭṭu āyvu vallunarkaḷ, talaiyōṭukaḷai mūṉṟu pirivākap pirittu āyn tuḷḷārkaḷ. oṉṟu nīṇṭa talaiyōṭukaḷuḷḷa iyaṟkūṟukaḷai yuṭaiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu maṭṭamāṉa uṭal uyarvuḷḷatu. iṉ ṉoṉṟu neṭṭaiyāṉa uṭal uyarvuḷḷatōṭu iyaṟkūṟu muḷḷatu. atumaṭṭumiṉṟi uyarnta maṇṭaiyōṭṭiṟkuriya kavikai mōṭupōṉṟa eṭuppāṉa tōṟṟamuḷḷa mūkkum uṭaiyatu. ivai cārkōṉikku, mecapottāmiyā kālattiṟku muṉ irunta tākak karutappaṭukiṟa al upait (Al-ubaid) kiṣ (Kish) mutaliya pakuti kaḷil uḷḷa iṉattoṭarpāṉa marapuk kūṟukaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya oppuṭaiyaṉa. itumaṭṭumiṉṟi varalāṟṟiṟku muntiya talaiyōṭṭu āyvuttuṟai (Craniology)Æ‹ nuṇ āyvu, mokañcatārō (cintuveḷi) āttirēliya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōr kaḷaic cārnta makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ mātiriyāka ātitta nallūriliruntu kaṇṭeṭutta maṇṭai yōṭukaḷ pōṉṟum iṉṟaiya vēṭarkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭukaḷ pōṉṟum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
eliyaṭ mit (Elliot Smith) kūṟṟuppaṭi, ātitta nallūril kaṇṭa maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ, paṇṭaiya ekiptiya makkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇamuṭiyātatākum. teṉ intiyak kāṭukaḷil vāḻum ūrāḷikaḷ, kāṭar pōṉṟa iṉatta varkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa karuppuniṟa iṉattavarkaḷuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya kurutit toṭarpu koṇṭa varkaḷ eṉpatu iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal kōṭpāṭākum avarkaḷ āttirēliya makkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷākat tōṉṟip pala talaimuṟaikaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. appāl avarkaḷ maiya nilak kaṭal cārnta makkaḷāyt tōṉṟit tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu muṭipu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. itaippaṟṟi namatu uḷamārnta koḷkai, vēṟu vakaiyāka irukkiṟatu. potuvāka intiyāviṉuḷ - ciṟappākat teṉ intiyāviṉuḷ - ēṉ? maiya nilak kaṭal cārnta iṉattavarkaḷ uṭpukuntatāka ārāyap pukāmalē muṉṉatākavē meyyeṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum? maṇṇiyal kālaṅkaḷiliruntu teṉṉintiyāvil makkaḷ iṉam vāḻntiruntāl, paḻaṅkal ūḻi makkaḷum putuk kal ūḻi makkaḷum nīkrō mātiri uruvil vāḻntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyirukka vēṇṭum inta iṉattavarkaḷ putuk kal ūḻiyil ācuttirēliya makkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷuṭaṉ kalappuṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu vivātam ceyyalām. āttirēliya iṉattavarkaḷiṉ āti muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭataṟkāka oru vēḷai intak kalappē poṟuppuṭaiya tāka irukkalām eṉṟu kūṟalām. ivvāṟāka makkaḷiṉ oruṅku kalattal ūḻi ūḻiyākap pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē paraviyirukka vēṇṭum.
tamiḻarkaḷ, tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷallar
ātikālattil, teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟi aṅku vāḻntu varum makkaḷaip paṟṟiya koḷkai teḷivāṉatu; uṟutiyāṉatu. tamiḻarkaḷum avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷum teṉṉintiyāvil tōṉṟi, ūḻi ūḻikālam aṅkēyē uṟaintu varupavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ paḻaṅ kal ūḻi toṭṭu, putiya kal ūḻi, ulōka ūḻi pōṉṟa ūḻik kālaṅkaḷaik kaṭantu varalāṟṟuk kālamvarai - iṉṟuvarai vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyāka, pala talaimuṟaikaḷāka vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ, avarkaḷiṟ cilar, tamiḻakattil paḻaṅkālattil eḻunta kaṭaṟkōḷkaḷukku añciyō piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālō maiyanilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷukkō, ekiptu nāṭṭiṟkō, amerikkā, kirīṭ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkō ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi irukkalām. mīṇṭum, nila naṭukkattālō āṭcik koṭumaiyālō cintuveḷikku vantiruk kalām. tāynāṭu tirumpiya tamiḻmakkaḷ, cintuveḷikku vantu tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyaraic cūṭṭiyirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar palar karuttu. namatu muṭipum atuvē. tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟai nipuṇarum varalāṟṟuttuṟai vallunarumāṉa tiru vi.ār. irāmac cantira tīṭcitar karuttum atuvē.6 iṅku atai mīṇṭum vali yuṟutti eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
intak karuttukkaḷaip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ (Prof. Langdon), muṉaivar ji.ār. aṇṭar, (Dr. G.R. Hunter) ec.ār.hāl (H.R. Hall) mutaliyavarkaḷ tam nūlkaḷil teḷivāka viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. cintuveḷi nākarikam veḷivantapiṉ ataip pōṉṟa nākarikam cumēriya nāṭṭilum nilaviyatai aṟintu cumēriyarkaḷē cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟi nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷ amaittu ulakil ōr uyariya paṇpāṭṭai uruvākkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭārkaḷ. atu avacarappaṭṭuc ceyta muṭipākum. tamiḻar teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟi, tamuḷar, timilar, tirāviṭar eṉṟa peyarkaḷuṭaṉō allatu vēṟu peyarkaḷuṭaṉō ekiptu ellam, alupait, cumēriyā, kirēttā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭi yēṟi payirttoḻil ceytu, poruḷ īṭṭi, kaittoḻil kaḷukkuk kālkōli, nāṭṭai vaḷampeṟac ceytu, paṇpāṭṭaip paṭaittu, camayamvaḷarttu vāḻviṟku vaḻikāṭṭiṉar. nāṭṭiṟkup poṉṟāy polivūṭṭiṉar.
tamiḻarkaḷum maiyanilak kaṭaṟkaraippakuti makkaḷum
makkaḷ iṉattaik kuṟittu eḻunta āḻnta āyviṉ payaṉāka vaṭakkē vāḻnta iṉattavarkaḷum maiyanilak kaṭaṟkaraip pakuti kaḷil uṟainta makkaḷum teṉ intiyā viliruntu nīr vaḻiyākavum nilavaḻiyākavum alupaitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷēyāvar. kiḻakkuk karai kaḷil paḻaṅkāla makkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai ettuṇai āyiram āṇṭuka ḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyavai eṉṟu collamuṭiyāta paḻamai vāyntaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta periya iṉattavar tamatu uyarnta aṟivaiyum ciṟanta kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum cīriya tōṟṟattaiyum vaccira vaṭivattaiyum ulaka muḻuvatilum parappiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu uruciya aṟiñar kontaret tōv teḷivāka eṭuttakkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.7
cumēriyarkaḷ tōṟṟattilum aṟivilum paṇ pāṭṭilum nākarikattilum tirāviṭarkaḷai ottavarkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷ allatu tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṭal vaḻiyākavum nilavaḻiyākavum upaitiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi mella mella ellattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkut toḻil vaḷarttu nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭaiyiṭṭu, appāl cumēriya nāṭṭiṟ pukuntu ṭaikkiricu yūppiraṭṭicu āṟṟaṅkaraikaḷil kuṭi yēṟi, teṉ intiyāvil taṇporunai āṟṟaṅkaraiyil aṭip paṭaiyiṭṭa ākkantarum ariya paṇpāṭṭai, yūppiraṭṭicu, ṭaik kiricu āṟṟaṅkaraikaḷilum nīla āṟṟaṅkaraiyilum kaṭṭivaḷarttu varalāṟṟup pukaḻpeṟṟa makkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷē aṅkiruntu mīṇṭum taṅkaḷiṉ tantai nāṭāṉa intiyāviṟku vantu vaḷañceṟinta cintu āṟṟal karaiyil kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
ayalnāṭukaḷil tamiḻar kuṭiyēṟṟam
varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷum māṉiṭaiyal vallunarkaḷum tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai aṟiñarkaḷum teṉ intiyāvil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷē. paḻaṅkallūḻi toṭṭu varalāṟṟuk kālam nikaḻum iṉṟuvarai intiyā yamuṉai cintu āṟṟiṟku appālvarai, vaṭamēṟku intiyā muḻuvatum imayamalai aṭivāram varai ceṉṟu payirt toḻil vaḷartta paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ eṉṟu muṭipukaṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vaṭakkē kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. annāṭṭiṉiṉṟu cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit teṉ intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟiṉar eṉpataṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ aṇuvaḷavum illai. atu cila mēṉāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa kaṟpaṉaik katai yākum. teṉintiyāvilum vaṭaintiyāvilum vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ pāracīkam, īrākku, ekiptu, kirēttā, caippiracu pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi nākarikattai vaḷarttuḷḷa ṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar kāḷitācu nāk kūṟiyuḷḷār.8
cintuveḷiyil malarnta māperum nākarikattil vīṭukaḷiṉ amaippukaḷaiyum muttiraikaḷiṉ ceynērttiyaiyum maṇpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṉaivuc ciṟappaiyum nāṭṭiyamāṭum naṅkaiyiṉ veṇ kalap paṭimattiṉ vārppu vaṉappaiyum nōkkiṉāl nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ pāpilōṉi yarkaḷai viṭa uyarnta nilai aṭaintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. intiya nākarikattiṉ iṟutiyil pāpilōṉiya nākarikam tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭātā? intiya nākarikamē cumēriya nākarikamāka malarntuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṇṇuvatum eḷḷaḷavum iḻukkillai eṉṟu pēraṟiñar kōrṭaṉ cailṭu eṉpār viṉāvi cintuveḷi nākarikamē ulakil uḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷukkellām muntiya nākarikam. ulakiṉ eḻunta ellā nākarikaṅkaḷukkum tāy nākarikam; oppaṟṟa uyarnta nākarikam eṉṟu pukaḻmālai cūṭṭiyuḷḷār. ataṉai viḷakkit tamnūlil vi.ār.irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2

kuṟippukaḷ - NOTES
IX
1.	Catalogue of the Pre-Historic Antiquities from Adichanallore and Perumbair by Alexandar Rea F.S.A. Scott (Madras 1915).
2.	The Adichanallur skulls (Bulletin of the Madras Govt. Museum) by Zuckerman, M.R.C.S., L.R.C.
3.	Essays on the evolution of Man by Prof. G. Elliot Smith (London) 1927.
	These two skulls conform to different racial types. One pe. i figs 1, 2 iii 7 of the present paper of them is clearly and unmistakably proto-Australian in type and the second one (pe ii figs 4,5 : iii 6) conforms more nearly to the racial type that is known as Mediterranean.
4.	“F.N.” It is not pure Mediterraneantype the breadth of the cranium and the flattening of the occiput suggesting the possibility that it may be an example of the type. I have called “Maritime Armenold” a branch of the Alphine Race that is found as one of the ingredients of the racial mixture known as Dravidian” - G. Elliot Smith.
5.	The Adichanallur skulls by S. Zuckerman M.A., M.R.C.S., L.R.C.P. London (Madras) 1930 p.19.
	“Regarded on its face value however Adichanallur No.1 provides support for the view that the oboriginal inhabitant of Southern India was similar to the present day Australian Native. It therefore upholds Huxleys’ interpretation on the Australian-Dravidian relationship which if embodied to-day in the conception of a Proto-Dravidian racial stock comprising the Australians the Sakai, the veddahs and the jungle tribes of Southern India. The evidence of the skull does not support the alternative interpretation (see p.12) that the present Australian native  is the result of a cross between an oboriginal Australian and invading indians, if anything it is against such an interpretation.”
6.	Animated by a love of adventure the ancient Southern Indian left his shore by Sea to the Mediterranian regions and the Far East and colonised these regions feeling the necessity for new homes. Those who left for making the Mediterranian race, perhaps some of them might have come back to India and stayed in their original homes. We must not forget that there was also a land route which led them to the Indus velley, Baluchistan, Mesopotamia and Egypt. Thus there was contact perhaps intimate contact of people and culture. These we may call tentatively the makers of and the people responsible for the Dravidian culture and Civilization” Pre-historic South India-Prof.V.R.Ramachandra Dikshistar M.A., (Madras) 1951 p. 134.
7.	(a)	“The Tamils who have an ancient culture speak a language allied to the language of India that from the Dravidian family spoken to-day by more than 100 million people. The Dravidian belong to one of the oldest ethnic groups in India. They lived there long before the belligerent nomad tribes of aryans mentioned in the Rig-Veda the sacred book of the Hindus came to the “land of marvels”. Today the Dravidian languages are spoken in Southern India upto 18’-20’s, but they once covered central and Northern India as well. Moreover facts show that several thousand years ago the Dravidian languages were also spoken in Baluchistan and Southern Iran. The Dravidians may have been the first settle in the Tigris and Euphrataies area preceding the sumerians whose civilization is regarded as the oldest in the world” - The riddles of three oceans A Kondratov (Moscow) 1974. P. 132.
	(b)	The features which the proto Indian culture and the Mesopotamian culture have in common may quite possibly be explained by the fact that the people who created the oldest Indian civilization and the first men to develop the valley of the Tigris and Euphrates were cognate peoples speaking Dravidian languages. Or perhaps they were simply one and the same people. It is also possible that the Dravidian languages may have been common to other peoples besides the ubaids and the Proto-Indians.
	(c)	The regions east of the Tigris in Iran called Khuzistan was once known as Elam. A civilization flourished there 5,000 years ago with citystates a distinctive culture and a written language scholars find that the culture of the Elamites had many features in common with that of Mesopotamia and even more so with the proto-Indian culture”. 
Ibid p. 143.
	(d)	It is fully possible that Elamite and the ubaid languages branched off from the common Dravidian stock at an early date, and this explains the similarities and the differences between them. There might be another explanation. The Dravidian languages, the language of the ubaids who preceded the sumerians, and Elamite might all go back to a more remote common language Ibid - 145.
	(e)	“... Dravidian ethnic type of India that the Sumerians bear most resemblance, so far as we can judge from his monuments. He was very like a Southern Hindu of the Dekkan (who still speaks Dravidian languages) and it is by no means impossible that the sumerians were an Indian race which passed certainly by land, perhaps also by sea through Persia to the valley of the two rivers. It was in the Indian Home (perhaps the Indus valley) that their writing may have been invented and progressed from a purely pictorial to a simplified and abbrevated form which  afterwards in Babylonia took on its peculiar uniform appearance owing to its being written with in a square ended stilus on soft clay. On the way they left the seeds of culture in Elam ..... there is little doubt that India must have been one of the earliest centres of civilization and it seems natural to suppose that the strange unsemitic, unaryan people who came from the east to civillse the west were of Indian origin specially when we see with our eyes how very Indian the Sumerian were in type. “F.N.” The Civilization was not Aryan. The culture of India is pre Aryan in origin as in Greece the conquered civilized conqerors. The Aryan India owed its civilization and its degeneration to the Dravidians as the Aryan Greek did to Mycenaeans” - An Ancient History of the Near East - R.H.Hall.
8.	The earliest stone age culture of India is represented by the hand axe technique of Madras and the old stone age people may have migrated from South India into Central India where in the Narbada valley, have been found middle pleistocene tools and a fauna gradually extended through the Ganges and Jamuna valleys to North Western India right upto Himalayan Hills” - India and the Pacific world - Kalidas Nag p. 279.
9.	(a)	The Ethnic and other affinities between South India and the Mediterranean basin must be due to the fact that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of northern and Mediterranean nations proceeded to the different parts of the globe which they now inhabit (Dr.E.Maclean) it is interesting that the Indians of North America and ancient Egyptians had a tradilon that they were immigrants and Herran was inclined to postulate the Indian orgin of Egiptions basing his theory on the form of the skull of the Egyptians. There is also a theory that the land of the Punt the original home of the Egyptians was perhaps the Pandian land including the Malabar coast. But more evidence, it is said, is needed to confirm this”, - origin and Spread of the Tamils - Prof - V.R.Ramachandra Dikshitar M.A.. (Madras) 1947 pp.3-4.
	(b)	Hindu America-chaman Lal (Bombay) 10.94.

intap pakuti ākkattiṟku utaviya nūlkaḷ

10. iṟuvāy
varalāṟṟut toṭakkam
 intiyāviṉ varalāṟu, intiyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟa villai. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattil intiya varalāṟṟai airōppiyar cilar eḻuta muṟpaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, avar kaḷil evarālum intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakālattai aṟuti yiṭṭuraikka muṭiyavillai. cilar kirēkkavīraṉ alekcāntar intiyāviṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttu vantu intiya maṇṇil kālaṭi yeṭutta vaitta ki.mu.330 ām āṇṭu mutal intiya varalāṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.
varalāṟṟaṟiñar tārāciṅ intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc curukkam eṉṟa nūlil, ki.mu. 6, 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ toṭakka kālattiliruntu tāṉ intiya varalāṟu tuḷirkkiṟatu : intak kālattiltāṉ, intiyāviṉuḷ eṇṇaṟṟa putiya iṉattavarkaḷ nuḻaintu intiyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu vāḻa muṟpaṭṭa ṉar; nāṭṭil putiya nākarikamum camayamum pukuntu vaḷarat talaip paṭṭaṉa; avaṟṟōṭu paḻaiya paṇpāṭum, camayamum puṟakkaṇikkap peṟṟaṉa; intiyāvil talai tūkkiya mutaṟpēraracu talaitūkkic cāyntatu; itu nāṭṭiṉ mukkiyamāṉa varalāṟākum. intiya varalāṟu ayal nāṭṭup payaṇikaḷ (vaḻippōkkarkaḷ) eḻutiya eḻuttuc cāṉṟukaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu uruvākkap peṟṟatu. avarkaḷ aḷitta cācaṉaṅkaḷum, maṭalkaḷum kācukaḷum niṉaivuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum intiya varalāṟṟai uruppeṟac ceyya utaviṉa eṉṟu eṭuttak kāṭṭiṉār.
varalāṟṟiñar cilar, ki.mu. 1500 ām āṇṭil, ayal nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu āṭumāṭukaḷai mēyttukkoṇṭu vanta nāṭōṭi makkaḷāṉa āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ puṉita pūmiyākiya intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntu intiya nāṭṭiṉ urimaiyāḷarkaḷāṉa tirāviṭarkaḷai veṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷai aḻittu, avarkaḷiṟ perumpālōrait teṉṉintiyāviṟkuḷ nerukkit taḷḷiviṭṭu, vintiya malaikku vaṭapakutiyil āriyā varttam, pirammavarttam eṉṉum peyaril taṉi nāṭukaḷai amaittu intiyāvaip piḷantaṉar. puṉitamāṉa tirāviṭa intiyāvil piriviṉai eṉṉum naccu vittai ūṉṟiṉar. āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil pukuntu āriyā varttattiṟku aṭikōliya avala nāḷē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka nāḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ; eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa eḻuti yuḷḷārkaḷ.
cintu veḷiyil intiyat tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉ akaḻ āyvu arumpiyatu. 1930ām āṇṭu varai ōraḷavu muṭi vuṟṟatu. 1932ām āṇṭu car. cāṇ mārcal mokañcatārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa mūṉṟu tokutikaḷaik koṇṭa arum perum nūlait tiṟampeṟa veḷiyiṭṭār. annūliṉ mūlam cintuveḷiyil tirāviṭap pēraracu cīruṭaṉum ciṟappuṭaṉum oḷi viṭṭōṅkiyatu. ki.mu. 1500kkum ki.mu. 2000kkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālattil ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu pōnta āriyarkaḷāl tirāviṭa intiyap pēraracu tākkappaṭṭu vīḻttappeṟṟatu. paḻaiya talai nakaramāṉa arappāvum, putiya talainakaramāṉa mokañcatārōvum aḻikkap peṟṟaṉa. aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷil palar teṉ intiyā viṟkut taḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. cintuveḷiyil oru vakaic cempu ūḻiyil (Chalcolishic age) āṟṟaṅkaraip paṇpāṭu nilaviyatu. aṅku vāḻnta tirāviṭamakkaḷ ulakilē oppaṟṟa nākarikattai uruvāk kiṉar. aṅku nilaviyatu vēḷāṇkuṭi makkaḷiṉ aracu. avvaracu uḻavut toḻilil uyariya veṟṟiyai īṭṭiyatu. annāṭṭiṉar kaḷiṉ camayam caivam; teyvam civaperumāṉ. umātēvi, murukaṉ, āṭavallāṉ pōṉṟa teyvaṅkaḷum pala ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. annāṭṭiṉar tam moḻiyaip pēṇivantaṉar. ōviya eḻuttaik kaṇṭu ēṭukaḷ pala eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar. ayalnāṭukaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki vaṇikam vaḷarttu vantaṉar. cintuveḷiyil, nantāp pēroḷi yāy nilaviya tirāviṭap pēraracai vīḻtta, āriyarkaḷ intiyāvil nuḻainta kālamē, intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka kālam eṉṟu cila varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟku varumuṉ avarkaḷukku eḻutat teriyātu. āṉāl, avarkaḷ intiyāvil nuḻaivataṟku muṉpē cintuveḷit tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷ eḻutap payiṉṟirun taṉar. āriyarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷiṭamiruntē eḻutak kaṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷ moḻi tiruttappeṟṟatu. eṉavē atu cemmai peṟṟa moḻi (camakkirutam) eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟatu. camakkirutam eṉṟa collukkut tirutti amaikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. eḻuttu arumpiyapiṉ varalāṟu arumpukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttukkēṟpa āriyarkaḷ vantu tirāviṭa intiyāvaic cīr kulaitta kālamākiya ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakka kālam eṉṟu varalāṟṟu aṟiñar cilar kaṇṭaṉar. paṇṭaiya intiya varalāṟum nākarikamum paṇpāṭum eṉṟa nūlil (pak. 54) tiru.pi.ecu.cōki. em.ē. avarkaḷum ēṉaiya mūṉṟu kūṭṭu āciriyarkaḷum eḻutiya ciṟanta nūlil āriyarkaḷ intiyā viṉ kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟa kālattiliruntu intiya varalāṟu toṭaṅku kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟi, mēṟkaṇṭa kūṟṟai valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷaṉar.
ulakilē eḻuttut tōṉṟi vaḷarcci peṟṟa piṉṉarē vara lāṟṟai varaiya muṭiyum. cintuveḷiyil āriyar pukunta kālattil eḻuttukaḷ iruntaṉa. āṉāl, avai vaḷarcci peṟṟirukkavillai. varalāṟu varaiyattakka nilaiyai aṭaiyavillai. eṉavē aṟiñarkaḷ akkālattai varalāṟṟukkālam eṉṟu kūṟātu varalāṟṟiṉ toṭakkakālam (Proto Historie Period) eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. mēlum cintuveḷiyil oru vakai maṇṇum cempum kalanta ūḻi (Chal-colithic age) eḻutuvataṟku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa ātitta nallūril ki.mu.8000-10,000 ām āṇṭaḷavil irumpu ūḻi tuḷirttu uyariya eḵkup paṇpāṭu malarntiruntatu. aṅku, cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ mūtātaiyar tōṉṟi uruveḻuttu oliyeḻuttu mutaliya palavakaikaḷaik kaṇṭu, ēṭum eḻuttāṇiyum kaikaḷil tāṅki, eḻutum vāyppaip peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. eṉavē intiya varalāṟṟiṉ mūlam taṇ porunai yāṟṟiṉ paṇpāṭṭil, teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa ātittanallūriliruntu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉalām. viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl intiya varalāṟṟu, ki.mu. 8000ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ ātittanallūr irumpup paṇ pāṭṭil - atāvatu teṉṉintiyā vil tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyum.
cintuveḷit tirāviṭarkaḷ
potuvāka airōppiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ, āriyarkaḷaippōl ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu intiyāvil, āriyar kaḷukkumuṉ cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟip piṉ, āriyarkaḷāl teṉ ṉintiyāviṟku viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷē eṉṟu eṇṇi vantaṉar. cintuveḷiyiṉai akaḻntu aṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai naṉku alaci ārāynta car-cāṇ mārcal avarkaḷ kūṭa nākarika mutircci peṟṟa cumēriyarkaḷ cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. ik karuttai vēṟu cila āciriyarkaḷum ātarittu vantuḷḷaṉar.
tirāviṭarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē
ulakam tōṉṟiya kālam mutal tamiḻarkaḷ teṉṉin tiyāvil tōṉṟip paravi vaḷarntu paṭippaṭiyāka nākarikam peṟṟu varupavar kaḷāvar. eṉavē tāṉ cermaṉ nāṭṭu nila nūṟ pulavar erṉacuṭ ekkal teṉ intiyā maṉitavarkkattiṉ toṭṭil eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
aṟiñar kāḷitācu nēk intiyāvum pacipik ulakamum eṉṟa nūlil, teṉṉintiyāvil paḻaṅkal ūḻiyaic cērnta makkaḷ nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟut teṟkiṉiṉṟu vaṭakku nōkki vantu maiya intiyā varai - ēṉ? kaṅkai yamuṉai veḷi - vaṭamēṟku intiyā varai ceṉṟu imaya malaiyiṉ aṭivārattilum vaṭakiḻakku intiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar. ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭil tirāviṭarkaḷ pāracīkam (īrāṉ), īrāk, ekiptu, kirīṭ, caippiracu nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
uruciya nāṭṭu aṟiñar alekcāntar kontārat tōḷ mūṉṟu mākkaṭalkaḷiṉ putirkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil, tamiḻarkaḷ paḻam perum paṇpāṭṭaip paṭaittavarkaḷ; iṉṟum avarkaḷ paḻam perum tirāviṭa moḻiyaip pēci varukiṉṟārkaḷ. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, avar teṉṉintiyāviṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭṭu naṭuvaṇ intiyāvilum vaṭaintiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar. vaṭaintiyāvil tirāviṭa moḻi payilappeṟṟu vantuḷḷatu. tirāviṭa moḻi paluccicuttāṉam īrāṉ īrāk nāṭukaḷilellām paravi iruntatu. tirāviṭarkaḷ īrākkil kuṭiyēṟi, kaṉṉi nilaṅkaḷai uḻutu payiriṭṭu naṉiciṟanta nākarikaṅkaḷai vaḷarttuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ, īrākkil (cumēriyāvil) vittiṭṭu vaḷartta tamiḻ nākarikamē ulakiluḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ mikat toṉmai vāynta nākarikamākavum uyariya nākarikamāvum oppaṟṟa nākarikamākavum pativu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
muṉaivar eyc ār. āl (Dr. H.R. Hall) eṉṉum pēraṟiñar, cintuveḷi akaḻāyvu toṭaṅku muṉpē tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ palvakaip paṇpukaḷ niṟainta meymmaic ceytikaḷai ulakiṟku uvantaḷittaṉar; cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷē īrākkil kuṭiyēṟi cumēriyar eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil iruntu tamiḻar cintuveḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit tirāviṭar āyiṉar eṉṟu kaṇ ṭār. tirāviṭarkaḷ nīrvaḻiyālum nilavaḻiyālum pāracīkattiṉ vaḻiyāka taikkirīcu, iyūp piraṭṭīcu āṟukaḷiṉ paḷḷattākkukaḷai aṭaintaṉar. avarkaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyil ellā, nāṭṭil intiyap paṇpāṭṭiṉ vittukkaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭup pōyirukkalām. cumēriyarkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum teṉ intiya makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum oṉṟupōlavē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cumēriyarkaḷ cintuveḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷāyum cintuveḷi makkaḷ teṉ intiyat tamiḻarkaḷākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa karuttukkaḷai pērāciriyar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉintiyā eṉṟa nūlil tiṟampaṭa viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
muṉaivar mōrkaṉ pirañcu moḻiyil ekiptu nāṭṭiṉ ēṟṟamum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku mukkiya āciyāvum eṉṟu varainta nūlil, maiya nilak kaṭaṟ pakutikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷiṉ mūlat tāyakamākavum, kalaiyūṟṟākavum viḷaṅkuvatu teṉ inti yāvē eṉṟu nāṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭamākavum uṟutiyākavum kūṟa muṭiyum eṉṟu ciṟappuṭaṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam, tirāviṭarkaḷ vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟku vantaṉar eṉpatu tavaṟāṉa koḷkaiyākum eṉpatai aṟiyalām. teṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tarai vaḻiyākavum kaṭal vaḻiyākavum cintuveḷi, ellam, (īrāṉ) cumēriyā (īrāṉ) ekiptu (micir) kirīṭ, amerikkā, āppirikkā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilellām kuṭiyēṟip payirttoḻil vaḷarttu, kaittoḻilkaḷaip perukki, moḻi, eḻuttup paṇ pāṭu, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟai uruvākki vaḷarttup pukaḻīṭṭiya tāy iṉam eṉṟu eṇṇaṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar.
āriyarkaḷ cumār ki.mu. 2003ām āṇṭaḷavil intiyāviṟ kuḷ vantavarākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. irāmāyaṇap pōr ki.mu.1500 ām āṇṭaiyoṭṭi nikaḻntatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. namatu intiya nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉāḷ mutal amaiccarum varalāṟṟu nūṟ pulavarumāṉa paṇṭitar cavakarlāl nēru avarkaḷ tam makaḷukku eḻutiya kaṭitam oṉṟil irāmāyaṇam pōr āriya - tirāviṭap pōr eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār. irāmaṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṟku varum poḻutu pāṇṭiyarkaḷ koṟkaiyil kapāṭapurattil kōṭṭai eḻuppip poṟkatavukaḷ pūṭṭiyum iṭaic caṅkam niṟuviyum tamiḻ vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kuraṅkuk kuṭumpattaic cērnta vāliyum avaṉ uṟaviṉarkaḷum aracu niṟuvi araṇmaṉai kaṭṭic ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantaṉar. civaperumāṉaik kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu aṟavaḻikaḷil pōr āṟṟi vantaṉar. irāvaṇaṉ puṭpaka vimāṉattiṉ mūlam pōkkuvarattu naṭatti vantāṉ. avaṉatu araṇmaṉai eḻunilai māṭamāy eḻil peṟṟilaṅkiyatu. araṇ maṉai pavaḷat tūṇkaḷum paṭikak kalpaṭikaḷum uṭaiyatāy eḻil peṟṟu ilaṅkiyatu. irāvaṇaṉ civapaktaṉāka iruntu poṉṉāl ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭṭu vantāṉ; māṟṟāṉ maṉaiviyait teṉṉintiya maṉṉarkaḷ marapu muṟaippaṭi ciṟai piṭittu vantālum, avaḷai araca kaitiyāy acōka vaṉattil ōr araṇmaṉaiyil vaittu, taṉ tampi vipīṭaṇaṉ makaḷ tiricaṭaiyait tuṇaiyāka irukkac ceytu, teṉṉāṭṭup paṇpu tiṟampā vaṇṇam naṭantu koṇṭāṉ - irāvaṇaṉ icai vallōṉ - yāḻ eṉṉum teyvīka icaik karuviyai mīṭṭuvatil iṇaiyaṟṟava ṉāyt tikaḻntāṉ. irāvaṇaṉ maṉaivi maṇṭōtari, maṇivācakap perumāṉāl civaperumāṉiṉ nallaruḷaip peṟṟa kaṟpukkaraci eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭupavaḷ. mēlum tamiḻakattil nilaviya uyar nalaṅkaḷellām cintuveḷiyiṉiṉṟu āriyarkaḷāl viraṭṭap paṭṭa tirāviṭarkaḷ teṉ intiyā pōntu oru cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷil periya nāṭukaḷ nakaraṅkaḷ kaṭṭi, moḻi vaḷarttu uyarnta paṇpukaḷai nilavacceyya muṭiyumā? muṭiyavē muṭiyātu. tirāviṭarkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟkālam mutal tamiḻakattil vāḻntu putiyakaṟ kālattait tāṇṭi ulōka kālattai aṭaintu ulakilē mutaṉ mutalākap poṉṉaik kaṇṭu atait tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭutti vantatōṭu irumpaik kaṇṭu atai naṉku payaṉpaṭutti ēṟṟam aṭaintuḷḷaṉar. ataṉōṭu tamiḻarkaḷ putiya kaṟkālattilē malai toṭuttuk kaṭalvarai paraviya nāṉilam amaittu mēṉilai eytiyum, irumpup paṇpāṭṭai naṉku payaṉpaṭuttip payir vaḷarttum toḻil vaḷarttum kappal kaṭṭik kaṭal vaṇikam vaḷarttum vantuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmai.
itukāṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvup poruḷkaḷāṉa poṉ, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliya vaṟṟaip paṟṟiyum maṭkalam, tāḻikaḷ, talaiyōṭukaḷ ākiyavai kaḷōṭu kūlap poruḷkaḷ, pañcāṭaikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyum nammuṭaiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ mūlam oruvāṟu naṉkaṟintirup pīrkaḷ. iḵtivvāṟirukka ātittanallūraip paṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ciṟappāka ituvarai eṭuttuk kūṟavillai.
tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakattil paṇi yāṟṟi vanta nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār kūṭa makkaḷait ticai tiruppi viṭum muṟaiyil iruntālum ātitta nallūr eṉṟa peyarai 3,4 iṭaṅkaḷil teṉ intiyāviṉ varalāṟu (A History of South India) eṉṟa tam nūlil ēṟattāḻa 6 pakkaṅkaḷukkumēl celaviṭṭut tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟṟu āyviṟku iṭar viḷaittirukkiṟār. atiliruntu cāttiriyār ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil paḻamporuḷkaḷ oṉṟum illai eṉṟu collāmal viṭṭuviṭṭārē eṉṟu viyappaṭaikiṉṟēṉ. āṉāl, oru cila āciriyar ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil kiṭaitta oru maṇṭaiyōṭu ācuttirēliya nīkrō yiṭ maṇṭaiyōṭu pōl irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya karuttai uṭkoṇṭu mīṇṭum atai eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. (See A. History of South India. pp. 53, 55, 57) ātitta nallūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṇṭaiyōṭu ācuttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ maṇṭai yōṭāka irukka muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭṭil oṉṟākavē irukka vēṇṭum. pēraṟiñar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya teṉ intiyā eṉṟa nūlil mika virivāka āyntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. ivaratu karuttukkaḷai cōviyat nāṭṭiṉ uyiriyal tuṟai vallunar muṉaivar pērāciriyar mikayil necutturk maṉita iṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟa nūlil, maṇṭaiyōṭṭai vaittu maṉita iṉaṅkaḷait tirāviṭar, āriyar, nīkrōvar eṉṟu pirittaṟivatu muṭiyāta kāriyam eṉṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭuvatōṭu, iṉap pirivu eṉṟa tattuvattiṉ aṭip paṭaiyē tavaṟu eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
cāttiriyār veḷiyiṭṭa tavaṟāṉa varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ
varalāṟṟu āciriyar oru nāṭṭaippaṟṟiyō, ōr iṉattaip paṟṟiyō eḻutumpoḻutu annāṭṭiṟkup pōy aṅkuḷḷa nakaram, vīṭu, iṉam, iṭipāṭukaḷ, akaḻāyvukaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, karuvikaḷ kācukaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cevivaḻi marapuc ceytikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai nēril pārttum, cevipaṭuttum naṉku āyntum muṭipiṟku varavēṇṭum. itaṟkut tiru.kē.ē. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār vitivilakku. ivar varalāṟṟiṟkuriya iṭattaip pārppatō, akaḻ āyvupporuḷkaḷai nōkkuvatō kiṭaiyātu. eṭuttukkāṭ ṭākat tamiḻk kalaik kaḷañciyam āṟāvatu tokutiyiṉ mutaṟ patippil tirunelvēli eṉṟa talaippil oru kaṭṭurai eḻuti yuḷḷār. ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvaip paṟṟic cila varikaḷ eḻuti viṭṭu, itaṉ kālam ki.mu.mutalāyiram āṇṭu eṉṟum ivai kiṟittava ūḻiyiṉ toṭakka nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai nīṭittiruntaṉa veṉṟum oru tavaṟāṉa ceytiyai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṟku avar evvitac cāṉṟum kāṭṭavillai. mēlum ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷai avar pārttatum illai. ataip paṟṟi ārāycci ceyta tol āyvu vallunarkaḷ karuttai avar eṉṟum nōkkiyatum illai. atōṭu avar piṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttiṟka matippuk koṭuppavarākavum teriyavillai. attuṭaṉ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu irumpu ūḻiyil arumpiya eḵkup paṇpāṭu eṉpataik kūṭak karuttuṭ koṇṭa tākat teriya villai.
mokañcatārōvai akaḻntu āyntavar vaṅkap pērā ciriyar irākēl tācup pāṉarcci (R.D. Banerji) eṉṟa nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulam vāynta varalāṟṟup pulavar āvar. avarukku nalla akaḻāyvut tuṟaip paṭippum, paṭṭaṟivum uṇṭu. avar, ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu irumpu ūḻiyil eḻunta irumpup paṇpāṭu; atu teṉ intiyāviluḷḷa kumarimuṉaiyiṉiṉṟu toṭarntu, aṟupaṭāta caṅkilit toṭarpōl arappāvarai nīṇṭu, paluc cicuttāṉ vaḻiyāy pakraiṉ tīvakattai ūṭuruvi, teṟku īrāṉ, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷaiyellām kaṭantu kirīṭ tīvu varai ceṉṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu 1927il tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟa toṭar kaṭṭuraiyil virivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattiṉ intiya varalāṟṟut tuṟai tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟai maṉita iṉa āyvuttuṟai ākiyavaikaḷiṉ pērā ciriyar, vi.ār.irāmaccantira tīṭcitar 1950-51il nikaḻttiya car ulliyam meyyar coṟpoḻivukaḷil (Sir. William Meyer Lectures, 1950-1951) ātitta nallūr irumpup paṇpāṭu teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟip paravi vaṭaintiyā muḻuvataiyum kavarnta ēṟṟamikum tirāviṭap paṇpāṭu ākum. itu ulakil utitta uyariya oppaṟṟa paṇpāṭu ākum. itaṉ kālam ki.mu.8,000 mutal 10,000 varai irukkalām eṉṟu aḻuttam tiruttamāka aṟaintuḷḷār. ataṟkuppiṉ ātitta nallūraip paṟṟi eḻuta muṉvanta kayavar kē.ē.nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār, takka ātāram kāṭṭi atai maṟut tirukka vēṇṭum. allatu atai ēṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. aḵtiṉṟi ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu ki.mu. mutalāyiram āṇṭiliruntu kiṟittava cakāptattiṉ toṭakka nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai nilavi irukka lām eṉṟu ātāramaṟṟa kayavāḷik karuttukkaḷait terivittatu ēṉ eṉṟu eṉakkup puriyavillai. ivarukkumuṉ ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭu mika mikat toṉmaiyāṉatu eṉṟa karuttai veḷiyiṭṭa pērāciriyar ār.ṭi.pāṉarcciyum, pērāciriyar vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum ivaraiviṭa evvitattilum kuṟaintavarkaḷallarē!. ivaratu poyk kūṟṟiṉaik kalaikkaḷañciyattil iṭam peṟac ceytatu tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakattiṉ kuṟṟamēyākum. aṭutta patippil ikkaruttukkaḷ akaṟṟappaṭṭu uṟutiyāṉa nalla karut tukkaḷ iṭam peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakattaip paṇivuṭaṉ vēṇṭik koḷkiṟēṉ.
mēlum nīlakaṇṭacāttiriyār iṉṉum tamiḻar varalāṟṟut tuṟaiyil pukuntu pala tavaṟukaḷai iḻaittuḷḷār. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka aṭiyil varum oru ceytiyai maṭṭum iṅku kūṟi itai muṭittukkoḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ.
nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār kalaikkaḷañciyattil tirunelvēli eṉṟa talaippil oru kaṭṭurai eḻutiyuḷḷār. atil pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭai eṉṉum ūraippaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atil cila tavaṟāṉa takavalkaḷai aḷittirukkiṉṟār. atāvatu, nellai māvaṭṭattil patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai yil, oru araṇ iruntatu. atuvē maturaikkut teṟkē mikunta vaṉmaiyuṭaiyatu eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntatu. āṉāl atai nirūpikkakkūṭiya citaivukaḷ (cāṉṟukaḷ) etuvum kāṇpataṟ killai eṉṟa karuttup peṟa eḻutiyuḷḷār.
tiru. cāttiriyār nellai māvaṭṭattil piṟantavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. atai vaittē nellai māvaṭṭattiṉ ciṟanta ūrāka viḷaṅkum pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyaippaṟṟi ivarai eḻutu māṟu kalaikkaḷañciyattiṉ patippu āciriyar kuḻu ivaraik kēṭ ṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ mutaṟ perum palkalaikkaḻakattiṉ varalāṟṟuttuṟaip pērāciriyar pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaikku varāmalē, allatu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai yil vāḻum makkaḷai vicārikkāmalē iṅku kōṭṭaiyiṉ citaivukaḷ etuvum kāṇpataṟkillai eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppatai aṟintu nellaimāvaṭṭa makkaḷum, varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum - ēṉ? paḷḷic ciṟārkaḷum pāmaramakkaḷum nakaikkiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu nāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷiṉ pulamaikku iḵtu ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu. ittakaiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kallūrikaḷilum, palkalaikkaḻakattilum iruntāl makkaḷukku varalāṟṟil nampikkai uṇṭākumā? pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyil irunta māperum araṇai iṭittapiṉ ataṉ niṉaivāka, (1) pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai pēruntu nilaiyattiṉ aṇmaiyil tiruvaṉantapuram cālaiyil oru citaivuṇṭa pakuti uḷḷatu. ataṉmītu kaṭṭapommaṉ cilai niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷatu. maṟṟōr araṇiṉ periya pakuti, nakar maṉṟattiṉ piṉpuṟam uḷḷatu. ataṉuḷ piḷḷaiyār kōyil uḷḷatu. mēlē kāval nilaiyam uḷḷatu. itu kōṭṭaippiḷḷaiyār kōyil eṉappaṭum. maṟṟoru pakuti, kōṭṭaip piḷḷaiyār kōyilil iruntu nērākak kiḻakkunōkkic cellum cālaiyil ēṟakkuṟaiya 200 mīṭṭar tolaivil nakarmaṉṟa nīrttoṭṭikku (Water tank) aṇmaiyil oru periya citaivu irukkiṟatu. itaṉ aṭiyilum mēṟpakutiyilum namatu araciṉ kāṭṭut tuṟai (Forest Department) iruntu varukiṟatu. itaṉ vaṭakkē ēṟattāḻa 120 mīṭṭar tolai vil maṟṟoru citaivu iruntu varukiṟatu. nāṉkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutikaḷ kuṉṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaip pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaiyil uḷḷa paḷḷicciṟārkaḷum aṟivar. iṅkuc citaivu kaḷē illai eṉṟu eḻutum āciriyaraip paṟṟi emmiṭam oru paḷḷi āciriyai piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. aiyarukku yāṉai pārkka veḷḷeḻuttā? eṉṟu kūṟum paḻamoḻikku nīlakaṇṭar ilak kākiyuḷḷār eṉṟu paṇpōṭu ivaratu tavaṟai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. paḻutuṟṟa kaṇkaḷāl, vayatu mutircci yāl oru viṭayattai eḻutumuṉ ataippaṟṟi āyntō vicārittō eḻutātu viṭṭa, māṟupaṭṭa karuttuṭaiya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu eppaṭit tamiḻar uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟṟuc ceytiyai etirpārkka muṭiyum? mēlum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa varalāṟṟaiyō, paṇpāṭṭaiyō, nākarikattaiyō nīlakaṇṭacāttiri pōṉṟa kayavarkaḷiṭam tamiḻarkaḷ etirpārppatē tavaṟākum eṉpatu namatu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipākum.
tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻar neṟi, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, tamiḻmoḻi ākiyavaṟṟai virumpātavarkaḷālum kāḻppuk koṇṭavarkaḷā lum eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu. kuṟippāka, tiru.nīlakaṇṭacāttiriyār pōṉṟavarkaḷtamiḻarkaḷukkup perun teyvamē kiṭaiyātu; murukaṉkūṭa āriyarkaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉākappeṟṟa teyvam eṉṟu pēciya pakuti tamiḻnāṭṭut tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟai veḷiyīṭukaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻarkaḷ, moḻiyaiyō, paṇpāṭṭaiyō, teyvattaiyō, camayattaiyō evarkaḷiṭa miruntum kaṭaṉākavō  iravalākavō eṉṟum peṟṟatillai. tamiḻarkaḷ tām taṅkaḷ moḻi, paṇpāṭu, teyvam, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟai ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu pēraṟiñarkaḷ muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itaiyaṟiyātu tiru. cāttiriyār intiyāviṉ orumaippāṭu, karuttu cutantiram eṉṟa peyarāl tamiḻarkaḷukku iḻaittuḷḷa tīmaikaḷai kālam kiṭṭumpōtu viḷakkik kāṭṭavōm.
putiya aṟivu oḷi
muṭipāka ātittanallūr akaḻāyvil kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ eṇṇila eṉpatai niṉaivil koḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṅkuk kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ matippiṭaṟkariya vilaiyuyarnta karuvūlaṅkaḷ. aṅkuk kiṭaitta poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷum veṇkalap poruḷkaḷum irumpup poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum tāḻikaḷum tamiḻar kaḷukkum, ēṉ? intiyāviṟkum perumai aḷippaṉa vākum. tamiḻarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uyarnta nākarikattait taṇporunaiyāṟṟup paṇpāṭṭai ulakiṟku naṉkoṭaiyāka aḷitta perumakkaḷ eṉpataṟku nalla cāṉṟākat tikaḻkiṟatu. ātittanallūril eḻunta viyattakum oppaṟṟa paṇpāṭṭai nūṟāṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ulakamum, ulakap pēraṟiñarkaḷum aṟiyaviṭātu ceytatupōl mīṇṭum mīṇṭum oru pulluruvik kūṭṭam, īṉat toḻilil īṭupaṭṭu varukiṟatu. innilaiyil ātitta nallūrp paṇpāṭu mīṇṭum makkaḷ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvarap paṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpatu īṇṭu niṉaivu kūṟattakkatu.
ātittanallūrp paṇpāṭṭiṉmītu kavintuḷḷa iruḷ paṭalaṅkaḷ aṉaittum akaṉṟuviṭṭaṉa. ataṉmītu putiya aṟivoḷi vīcat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu. ataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ulakam etirpārā vaṇṇam ātittanallūrum porunaiveḷi nākarikamum (Adhitha Nallur and Porunai Valley Civilization) eṉṟa innūl veḷivantuḷḷatu.
cintuveḷic cempu nākarikam ki.mu.6000 ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ nilaviya tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭiṟku aḷaviṭaṟkariya matippai aḷit tuḷḷatu. atu tirāviṭa nākarikantāṉ ulakil oppaṟṟa paḻamperum nākarikam eṉpatai ulakam ēṟṟuk koḷḷac ceytatu. āṉāl tavaṟākac cila aṟiñarkaḷāl cintuveḷi nākarikam maiyanilak kaṭaṟpakuti makkaḷ intiyāviṟku vantu tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟa peyarāl uruvākkappeṟṟa nākarikam eṉṟu kāṇappeṟṟatu. cintu veḷiyiṉiṉṟu āriyarkaḷāl viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷē teṉṉin tiyāvil iṉṟu vāḻum makkaḷ eṉṟu karuta iṭam aḷittatu.
ātittanallūr irumpu nākarikam ki.mu.8000 ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ ulakil nilaviya oppaṟṟa nākarikamākum. inta irumpup paṇpāṭṭil ulakil porunaiveḷi nākarikattiṉ mukam tōṉ ṟiyatu. itaṉ payaṉākap payirttoḻilil periya muṉṉēṟṟam eḻuntatu. kait toḻilkaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai viraintu kiḷaittu muḷaitteḻuntaṉa. nāṭṭil makkaḷiṉ tēvaikku miñciya viḷai poruḷkaḷum, ceyporuḷkaḷum malai malaiyāka eṅkum kuvintu kiṭantaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭut tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil nāvāykaḷum, kalaṉkaḷum, vaṅkamum eṇṇilātu malintu kiṭantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ palaṉākak kaṭal vaṇikam piṟantatu. tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭal vaṇikar tam kappalkaḷil tam nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷai ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tolai vil uḷḷa el, upait, ellam, cumēriyā, akkēṭiyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ, amerikkā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar. kuṭiyēṟṟam peṟṟaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷ ulakameṅ kum taṅkaḷ vaṇikam, kaṇitam, camayam, moḻi, teyvam, nākarikam, eḻuttu mutaliyavaṟṟai vaḷarttaṉar. paṇṭaiyat tamiḻarkaḷ ulakam eṅkum paraviṉar.
intiya orumaippāṭṭukku araṇceyyum nākarikam
teṉ intiyāvil tōṉṟiya tamiḻarkaḷ paḻaṅkal ūḻi, putukkal ūḻi, irumpu ūḻiyil vāḻntu nākarikam peṟṟu, vaṭa intiyā muḻuvatilum paraviyatōṭu nila vaḻiyākavum, nīr vaḻiyākavum ulakameṅkum paraviṉārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ātitta nallūr nākarikattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ vaṭaintiyā īrāṉ, īrākku, ekiptu, kirīṭ pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmai nilaināṭṭap peṟukiṉṟatu.
itu intiya nāṭṭiṟkup perumai aḷippatōṭu intiya orumaippāṭṭiṟku ātittanallūr nākarikam ōr araṇāka amaintuḷḷatu eṉpatu teriyavarukiṉṟatu. intiyar maṭṭumalla ulaka makkaḷ yāvarum tāṅkaḷ aṉaivarum ōriṉam, ōr kiṟai eṉṟa uyariya koḷkai valuppaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.
viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ
ip pakutiyil ātittanallūril akaḻntu kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭa poṉ, irumpu, veṇkalam ākiyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭap poruḷkaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ; tāḻikaḷ, maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ ākiyaṉavum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟukkuriya paṭaṅkaḷ nūluḷ āṅkāṅkē iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1. poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ (Diadems)1
ivai potuvāka nīḷ uruṇṭai vaṭivam uḷḷavai. ov voṉṟiṉ iṟutivarai nīṇṭa varikkōṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. inta varikaḷiṉ muṭivil tuḷaikaḷ iṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. it tuḷaikaḷ paṭṭattaik kayiṟu allatu kampiyil kōttu neṟṟi yil kaṭṭuvataṟkāka iṭappaṭṭavai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perum pālāṉa paṭṭaṅkaḷil varikkōṭukaḷ iṟutiyil kāṇappaṭa villai. intap poṟpaṭaṅkaḷ mika melliyaṉavāy iruppatāl nīḷ uruṇṭai vaṭivil valappuṟamum iṭappuṟamum eḷitil kampiyiṉāl kuttit tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭu vantatākat terikiṟatu. cilavaṟṟil kōṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mukkōṇavaṭivil piṉpuṟamākap puḷḷipuḷḷiyāka aḻutti aṇiceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ivaṟṟuḷ āṟu paṭṭaṅkaḷ aṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭātavaiyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ip poṉ paṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya muḻuviḷakkamum paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭil poṉ eppaṭiyellām tamiḻarkaḷāl payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu eṉṟa varalāṟum innūluḷ 76ām pakkam mutal 94ām pakkam muṭiyak kāṇalām.
piṟa putai poruḷkaḷ
maṟṟum ātittanallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa vakai vakaiyāṉa 33 veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ, īṭṭi, koṭuvāḷ, katti pōṉṟa palvēṟu irumpuk karuvikaḷ 97 vakaiyāṉa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, mutumakkaḷ tāḻikaḷ, maṇṭaiyōṭukaḷ ākiyaṉa paṟṟiya viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷ nūluḷ āṅkāṅkē iṇaikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.


1.	These are locally known as Pattam and the system of putting gold in some shapes or other over the dead body is prevalent in orthodox Hindu homes even now a days. Dr. Sir John Marshall makes the following note on these objects (Annual Report of the Archaelogical Survey of India, 1902-3 Footnote on page 105):- “The gold frontlets are of thin gold leaf so flimsy that they could not possibly have been used as jewellery in real life. They are mere imitations of the genuine article subsituted by the relations of the dead who no doubt kept the more substancial jewellery for themselves. For similar instances of this economical piety towards the dead, see Frazer Pausanias, vol III page 107. Schleimann Myvenarl page 156. E. Gardner, New Chantere
